天使在身边2
译源：Season 2: Angels In Their Presence
作者：Dr. Omar Suleiman
出品：Yaqeen Institute
译制：虔路｜TheOnePath.net
EP000 系列预告
Angels In Their Presence | Trailer

Angel Jibreel (a) personally conveyed Allah’s salaam to Khadijah (rA). Jarir’s (rA) face was touched by an angel. 70,000 angels attended Sa’ad ibn Mu’adh’s (rA) janazah. 
Angels are not the ethereal, far-away creatures of our imagination. They are ever-present in this world, recording our deeds, ferrying souls, crossing the boundaries between the heavens and the earth. And nowhere were they more present than in the lives of the Sahabah. 
Join Dr. Omar Suleiman as he delves into the moments the Companions encountered angels in the city, on the battlefield, and, of course, in their time spent with the Prophet (ﷺ).
吉卜利勒天使（愿他平安）曾亲自向赫蒂彻（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）传达真主ﷻ的“色兰”，杰里尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）的脸庞曾被天使触摸，七万天使参加了赛阿德·本·穆阿兹（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）的殡礼。  
天使并非我们想象中虚无缥缈、遥不可及的存在。他们存在于今世，记录我们的行为，护送灵魂，穿梭于天地之间。而他们最频繁显现的时刻，莫过于圣伴们的生活中。  
与欧麦尔·苏莱曼博士一起，深入探究圣伴们在城市或战场，尤其是与先知ﷺ相伴时与天使相遇的瞬间。
Back in Medina, the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam was sitting with the companions and suddenly the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam, he shakes as if something happens. The angels started to come to me. I would come to my house and they would greet me at my door and then they would even come to me when I would lay in bed and they would greet me at the head side of my bed. The way that Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala protected these companions, we felt their presence arrive in the skies. He said, that was the angels. They came close because of your recitation. He said, Suddenly I saw a man coming forward and he had a spear and at the tip of that steel spear was fire. Now, what does it mean to say that an angel wiped your face? Did angels appear in any other fashion? That he is being washed by the angels between the heavens and the earth. And he says, When you called out, O most merciful of those who show mercy. I was in the seventh heaven.
在麦地那，先知ﷺ正与圣伴们同坐，突然他的身躯震颤——天使正向我降临。我归家时，他们会在门前迎接；我卧床时，他们甚至会在枕畔致意。
真主ﷻ如何保护这些圣伴？我们感受到他们从天而降。先知ﷺ说：“那是天使。因你们的诵读声，他们临近了。”有人描述：“忽见一人持矛而来，钢矛尖端跃动着火焰。”
“天使擦拭脸庞”有何深意？天使是否以其他形态显现？他正在天地之间接受天使的沐浴。当呼唤“至仁至慈的主ﷻ啊”时，我正身处第七重天。
EP001 天使日常问候他
They Would Greet Him Regularly

What do you have to do to have angels come and greet you? Six years after hijrah, Imran ibn Husain (r) accepted Islam and became a Companion of the Prophet ﷺ.
In love with his faith and deeply attached to Allah, he maintained dhikr, or the consistent remembrance of God, to such an extent that he was gifted with the regular presence—and salaam—of the malaa’ikah.
要如何让天使亲临问候？迁徙六年后，伊姆兰·本·侯赛因（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）归信伊斯兰，成为先知ﷺ的圣伴。他深爱信仰、虔心向主ﷻ，坚持记念真主ﷻ至深，终获天使常伴身旁、亲授“色兰”的殊荣。
Imran Ibn Hussain radiAllahu ta'ala anhu was a young man when he embraced Islam. And he came later on in the life of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, but he would rise to be this person who was known as Shabih al-Malaika, a person who resembles the angels, not necessarily in his appearance or even in his character, but specifically in his worship. And he gives this example of how that worship of Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la manifested his household in the first place. By talking about how the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam actually gave da'wah to his father.
伊姆兰·本·侯赛因归信时正值青年。虽在先知ﷺ晚年才加入，却以“肖似天使者”闻名——非指容貌或品性，而是专指其拜功之虔诚。他通过先知ﷺ向其父传教的经历，展现了对真主ﷻ的敬拜如何首先显化于家庭。
He said that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam came to my father and he said to him, Ya Hussain, Oh Hussain, how many gods do you worship? So he said seven total, Sittan fil arad wa wahidan fil sama'a, six of them on this earth and one of them in the heavens. So he says, basically, I've got these idols and then I've got this one God that I deem supreme that I can't see. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, which one of them do you put your greatest hopes and fears in? Which one of those gods do you give the greatest consideration to? So he said, the one that's in the heavens. That's the one that I have the greatest reverence for. The Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said to him, then why don't you worship the one that's in the heavens and leave the ones that are on the earth? And SubhanAllah, that made sense to him. And he became a muwahid, he became a Muslim, a monotheist and a Muslim.
他说，先知ﷺ来到我父亲面前问道：“侯赛因啊，你敬拜多少神灵？”父亲答：“共七位，地上六位，天上一位。”即既有偶像崇拜，又承认有位看不见的至高神灵。先知ﷺ追问：“你最寄予厚望与敬畏的是哪位？”父亲答：“天上那位。”先知ﷺ遂劝诫：“何不专拜天上之主，弃绝地上伪神？”赞主ﷻ超绝！这番劝导令他茅塞顿开，从此成为认主独一的穆斯林。
So Imran comes into Islam around the time of Khaibar. So we're talking about about six years after the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam had come to Medina and immediately falls in love with this religion and dedicates himself to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and dedicates himself to this idea of worship of one God. And it became to the point that he gains this reputation amongst the companions of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam as being a zahid, as being an ascetic and being a scholar who's only interested in two things. He's interested in knowledge and he's interested in worship. So it's ilm and ibadah. That's all that you knew Imran Ibn Hussain radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu for. And that's the way that the companions viewed him.
伊姆兰于海巴尔战役时期归信，此时距先知ﷺ抵达麦地那已约六年。他立即深陷信仰之爱，全身心奉献于先知ﷺ与认主独一的理念，终在圣伴中赢得虔敬者、苦修者与学者之名——唯专注两件事：知识与崇拜。这正是圣伴们眼中的伊姆兰。
Now, when Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu would become the khalifa, he would actually appoint him to a government position. He'd sent him to Basra and the people of Basra loved him. They said that no one has ever come to us from the companions of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. That's better than this man. But Imran asked Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu to be excused and to basically just teach the people. But at the same time, he didn't want to hold office. We also know of him that he avoided all fitna. He hated all types of division and disunity in the Muslim ummah. He said, I will never use this tongue against another Muslim or throw an arrow against another Muslim. So when the people come to him and ask him to do this or do that, Imran radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu would shut his door and exert himself in ibadah, exert himself in worship.
当欧麦尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）任哈里发时，曾委任他担任巴士拉总督。当地民众盛赞：“从未有圣伴比此人更优秀。”但伊姆兰请求卸任，专心教学。我们也知道他避免了各种考验。他远离一切纷争，厌恶穆斯林社群的分裂，宣称：“我永不用口舌或箭矢伤害穆斯林。”每逢有人邀他参与纷争，他便闭门专注功修。
Now, one of the things that we also see from him, though, is that despite him being a great worshiper and a great ascetic, that didn't mean that he used to come out looking like a person who was in rags and a person who had abandoned this world physically. In fact, there's a narration that Imran radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu had come out to the people and he was wearing this really nice robe. The robe had a special type of stitching and embroidery. It had special buttons on it. And when they asked him about it, he said, it's because I heard the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam say that when Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala blesses a person with ni'mah, Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala loves and yara'athara ni'matihi ala abdihi. To see the effect of that blessing on his servant. [al-Mu'jam al-kabiir 5167] So detachment from this world is not that you wear raggedy clothes. It's not that you inflict poverty on yourself. It's that you attach yourself only to Allah. And in the case of Imran radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, who lived with the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam, an attachment to the messenger of Allah salAllahu alaihi wa sallam.
值得注意的是，这位伟大的苦修者并非衣衫褴褛的弃世者。据传他曾身着精美刺绣长袍现身，纽扣华美。面对询问，他引述先知ﷺ教诲：“当真主ﷻ赐福仆人时，喜爱在其身上看到恩典的痕迹。”（《大辞典》#5167）真正的出世并非粗衣陋服，亦非自陷贫困，而是心灵唯系真主ﷻ。对曾与先知ﷺ相伴的伊姆兰而言，更是将生命系于真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ。
And he, in fact, is one of the narrators of the hadith where the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam said, the best of my ummah is my generation. And then those that come after them and then those that come after them. And subhanAllah, because he was so young when he embraced Islam, he lived long after the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam. And he never stopped longing for him, especially when he remembered ibadah, worship with the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam. One of his students, a great tabi'i, al-Mutarraf ibn Abdullah radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, he said that one time he and I prayed behind Ali ibn Abi Talib radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. And after the prayer, Imran grabbed my hand and he said, this reminded me of the prayer of the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam. This took me back to that time of praying with the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam.
事实上，他正是传述一段圣训的传述人之一——先知ﷺ曾说：“我的社群中最优秀的是我这一代人，其次是他们之后的一代，再其次是之后的一代。”赞主ﷻ超绝！因为他归信伊斯兰时非常年轻，他在先知ﷺ归真后仍生活了多年。但他对先知ﷺ的思念从未停止，尤其是每当想起与先知ﷺ一起礼拜的时光。他的学生、伟大的再传弟子穆塔里夫·本·阿卜杜拉（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）讲述：有一次我们跟随阿里·本·艾比·塔利卜（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）礼拜。礼完后，伊姆兰抓住我的手说：“这让我想起了先知ﷺ的礼拜，仿佛又回到了跟随先知礼拜的时光。”
Now, of course, everyone around the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam was in a certain state. And if Imran distinguished himself radiAllahu anhu, even amongst the companions when around the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam, then how was he at all other times? And you might recall a very famous narration of Hamdara radiAllahu anhu going to Abu Bakr and then Abu Bakr and Hamdara may Allah be pleased with them, going to the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam and complaining that maybe they were hypocrites. Why? Because they couldn't maintain that same level of dhikr, that same level of remembrance of Allah, that same state of mind and state of heart when they were with the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam and then with their families after they left the presence of the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam. And what did the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam say to them? He said, listen, first of all, sa'atan wa sa'a. It's you know, you have to have a time for this and a time for that. As long as you're not sinful when you're away from the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam, it's understandable that you're not going to be able to maintain the same state of heart and mind in the absence of the Messenger salAllahu alaihi wa sallam that you had when you're sitting in his halaqas, when you're with the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam. And the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam said to them that if you were to maintain the same state in my absence that you do in my presence, the angels would shake your hands in the streets.
当然，每个在先知ﷺ身边的人都有特殊的精神状态。即使置身圣伴之中，伊姆兰在先知身边的表现也与众不同。这让人想起著名的圣训：罕达拉（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）曾向艾布·伯克尔诉说，后来他们一起去见先知ﷺ，担心自己可能是伪信士。为什么？因为他们无法保持与在先知ﷺ面前同等程度的记主ﷻ状态，无法在离开先知ﷺ、回归家庭后维持同等的心灵境界。先知ﷺ如何回应？他说：“凡事各有其时。只要你们离开我时不犯罪，无法维持与在我面前同等的心灵状态是可以理解的。”先知ﷺ还补充道：“若你们在我不在时仍能保持同等程度的记主ﷻ，天使们早就在街上与你们握手了。”
How does this apply to Imran radiAllahu anhu? Imran actually aspired to be in that state. Imran actually aspired to be in the same way that he was with the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam, even when the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wa sallam had already passed away. And some time passed where Imran radiAllahu anhu became ill for an extended period of time. And he took inspiration from the story of Ayub alaihi salam, the prolonged sickness and worship and patience. And then one day his student al-Mutarrif visits him and he sees him very sad. So he says to him, what's wrong? He says that something was happening with me and Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala took that blessing away from me. He said, what is it? He said that for an extended period of time, the angels started to come to me. That I would come to my house and they would greet me at my door and then they would even come to me when I would lay in bed and they would greet me at the head side of my bed. So the angels, sulli ma'alaiya, they were giving salam to me and I was giving salam to them as well. And this was happening from every direction. SubhanAllah, what a blessing from Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala to only happen to this one companion where the angels are saying salam to him. And he's saying salam to them as well.
这对伊姆兰意味着什么？他始终渴望保持与先知ﷺ同在时的状态，即使先知ﷺ早已归真。有一次伊姆兰病入膏肓，他从先知安尤布（愿他平安）的故事中汲取长期病痛、坚忍与崇拜的灵感。当学生穆塔里夫探望时，发现他满面愁容。询问缘由，伊姆兰说：“真主ﷻ收回了一项恩典。过去很长一段时间，天使们常来问候我——当我回家时在门口迎接，甚至在我卧床时在床头致意。天使们（愿他们平安）向我道“色兰”，我也回敬。这恩典四面八方环绕着我。”赞主ﷻ超绝！这是真主ﷻ唯独赐予这位圣伴的恩典——天使们向他道“色兰”，他也向天使们回“色兰”。
He said, but it stopped after I did al-qayy, which is a medical procedure that the Prophet salallahu alaihi wasalam generally dislike. So he said, when I did al-qayy, the angels stopped coming to me. So he was very sad about that. Mutarrif said, I told him that inshallah, they'll come back to you. And subhanAllah, sometime after that, Imran radiyaAllahu ta'ala Anhu was in joy. And he said to al-Mutarrif ibn Abdullah, he said, alhamdulillah, they came back to me. But don't tell anyone of this until after I die. I don't want anyone to know of this blessing that Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala has given to me until after I pass away. And subhanAllah, it stayed that way until he passed away radiyaAllahu ta'ala Anhu.
但他接着说：“自从我做了放血治疗后，天使就不再来了。”先知ﷺ通常不喜欢这种疗法。穆塔里夫安慰道：“若主ﷻ意欲，他们会回来的。”赞主ﷻ超绝！后来伊姆兰欣喜地告诉学生：“感赞真主ﷻ！他们回来了。但在我归真前请保密，我不愿人们知晓真主ﷻ赐予的这项恩典。”赞主ﷻ超绝！这秘密并未公开直到他归真。
And there's something very special about this that the Prophet salallahu alaihi wasalam was not just giving a random example. If you are in a state of dhikr, you are in the presence of angels, whether that's in a gathering or whether that is as an individual. And when this person Imran radiyaAllahu ta'ala Anhu is able to maintain that consistent remembrance of Allah, then Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala blessed him with the consistent presence of the angels. And for us, the more we remember Allah, the more the angels are going to be present with us.
这其中蕴含深刻哲理：先知ﷺ的比喻并非空谈。当人们处于记主ﷻ状态时——无论集体还是个人——都处于天使的环绕中。伊姆兰因持续记念真主ﷻ，蒙赐与天使常伴的恩典。对我们而言，愈多记念真主ﷻ，天使就愈常相伴。
EP002 钉刑架上的赞圣者
Crucified While Sending Salawat

Captured by the Quraysh and taken as a prisoner to Makkah, the Ansari Companion Khubayb ibn Adiy (ra) was put on death row. In the last moments, before he was crucified, his deep connection to Prophet Muhammad ﷺ was realized through a conviction-filled prayer to Allah and its immediate answer through the best of angels, Jibreel (a).
被古莱氏人俘虏押往麦加的辅士圣伴胡拜布·本·阿迪（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）被判处死刑。在被钉上十字架前的最后时刻，他向真主ﷻ发出充满信念的祈祷，并透过最尊贵的天使吉卜利勒（愿他平安）即刻获得应答，彰显了他与先知穆罕默德ﷺ的深刻联系。
SubhanAllah, I'm always amazed by how much some of these companions love the Messenger SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, even though they only spent two or three years with him. Like you think about those people in Madinah in particular, the Ansar, who met the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and some of them died within one, two, three years of meeting and receiving the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam.
赞主ﷻ超绝！这些仅与先知ﷺ相伴两三年却深爱使者的圣伴们总令我震撼不已。尤其是麦地那的辅士们，有些人在迎接先知ﷺ后1-3年内便归真了。
This Sahabi Khubaib Ibn Adi radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, when you talk about the miracles of the companions with the angels and otherwise, he really is in a league of his own. Now Khubaib radiAllahu Anhu was a young man from the Ansar, from the tribe of Aus, when the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam came to Madinah and he embraced Islam and became one of the veterans of Badr, who are of course the best of the companions of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. And just a short while after that, he's captured in this ambush by Quraysh. Now, of course, Quraysh from Mecca was trying to avenge their losses from Badr, immediately after Badr. So they captured Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu and a group of the Ansar. And in fact, in that ambush, many of his companions, eight of the 10 were actually killed. The head of them was a man by the name of Asim Ibn Thabit Al-Ansari radiAllahu Anhu, has his own miraculous story. But Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu survived the attack and was taken as a prisoner back to Mecca.
这位奥斯部落的年轻辅士胡拜布·本·阿迪堪称圣伴中与天使互动奇迹的典范，他的故事无可匹敌。先知ﷺ迁徙麦地那后，他归信伊斯兰并成为白德尔战役的老兵——须知白德尔战士是圣伴中的至优者。然而不久后，他在古莱氏为报复白德尔之败发动的伏击中被俘。当时十位辅士中八人殉难，包括拥有神奇事迹的首领阿绥姆·本·萨比特（愿主ﷻ喜悦他），唯胡拜布在袭击中幸存，被押往异乡麦加囚禁。
So SubhanAllah, you have a man that embraced the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam coming from Mecca to Madinah and now is a prisoner in this land that is foreign to him, which is Mecca. Now, when he's taken back as a prisoner to Mecca, the tribe of Al-Harith Ibn Amr Ibn Nawfal heard that Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was taken as a prisoner. Now Khubaib killed Al-Harith Ibn Amr in the battle of Badr. So his tribe realizes that the one who killed their father is now a prisoner in Mecca. Now, of course, the battle of Badr is, you know, a situation where the Muslims themselves were not going out for battle, but battle was forced upon them and Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu bravely rose to the occasion. So you have the situation where the man who was killed by Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, his family in Mecca hears about him being a prisoner in Mecca. They immediately go to those that captured him. They purchased Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu and they say that we are going to take him as a slave, take him as a prisoner. And when the time comes, we're going to execute him. So here Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu is in the strange land. And he's with the tribe of this man, Al-Harith Ibn Amr, which are full of vengeance and they are ready to execute him in the worst of ways.
赞主ﷻ超绝！当先知ﷺ从麦加迁徙至麦地那，胡拜布·本·阿迪归信了伊斯兰，如今他成为异乡麦加的阶下囚。当他被押回麦加时，纳沃法勒之孙哈里斯·本·阿慕尔的部落闻讯赶来——须知在被迫应战的白德尔战役中，正是胡拜布击杀了哈里斯。这个充满复仇怒火的部落立即从俘虏者手中买下胡拜布，宣称要奴役他并择日处决。此刻，胡拜布身处异乡，被哈里斯·本·阿慕尔的族人团团围住，这群人满心仇恨，正策划以最残酷的方式将他处死。
However, it was the time of the sacred months. So because it was the time of the sacred months and the people of Mecca in some strange way, they still weirdly observed some of these rituals, right? In a very strange way. They said, we're going to leave him until the sacred months pass, and then we'll kill him then. So basically at this point now Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu is in his chamber in Mecca and they're waiting for the sacred months to pass, and then they're going to execute him in a very nasty way. So you have the children of Al-Harith and they're taking turns watching him as a prisoner. And many of these people that are going to be involved in the story would become Muslim later. One of them, either the daughter of Al-Harith or she was a female servant from the women folk of Al-Harith. So she tells the story when she became Muslim later on, she says that we would take turns watching Khubaib, the boys, the girls, the sons, the daughters, we would take turns watching him. And she said that it was my shift. And I immediately realized that there was something very strange about this man. I would bring him food as we would with our prisoners. And when I would bring him food while he was in his chains, he already had grapes. And these were grapes from a strange place and it wasn't even in season. So there's no way he got these grapes from Mecca. There's no way he was able to communicate with the outside world. So I'm wondering where is he getting these grapes from, these special grapes from? So I asked him one day, I said, where are these grapes from? He said, Inna rabbi yuta'imuni wa yusqini He said, my Lord feeds me and he provides me drink as well. SubhanAllah. So just like in the story of Maryam Alayhi Salaam, when Zakariya Alayhi Salaam would come to her mihrab and find that she already had food out of season, fruits out of season. So this was one miracle for Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. 
然而当时正值禁月期间。由于禁月的缘故，麦加人虽然行为悖逆，却仍奇怪地遵守着某些传统仪式——以一种非常诡异的方式。他们说：“我们要把他留到禁月结束，然后再处决他。”于是胡拜布被囚禁在麦加的囚室中，他们等待着禁月过去，准备以极其残忍的方式处死他。哈里斯的儿女们轮流看守这位囚犯——这些参与看守的人中，有许多后来都归信了伊斯兰。其中一位是哈里斯的女儿（或是家族中的女仆），她后来成为穆斯林后讲述道：我们轮流看守胡拜布，男孩女孩、儿子女儿都参与轮值。轮到我值班时，立即发现这个人很不寻常：当我像对待其他囚犯那样给他送食物时，明明戴着镣铐的他手中却总有葡萄——那些葡萄来自奇异之地，而且根本不是当季产物。麦加根本不可能有这种葡萄，他也绝无可能与外界联系。她疑惑地询问葡萄来源，胡拜布回答：“我的主确已供我饮食。”赞主ﷻ超绝！这正如宰凯里雅（愿他平安）在麦尔彦（愿她平安）的净室发现非时令果品的奇迹一般。
Then she says that when it came closer to the time of his execution, he requests a blade so that he can remove from his body here, what was from the fitrah and basically groom himself before his martyrdom. He wants to meet Allah Subhanahu wa ta'ala in the best of ways. And so he wants to groom himself before his shahada. He knows that the time of his execution is coming up. So he requests a blade. She said, I brought him the blade. And while I left him, my baby boy crawled to him while I wasn't paying attention. So she said, I rushed to the chamber realizing that my baby boy wasn't around. And look at the scene. She says, I found Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala anhu sitting. In one hand, he had the blade, in the other hand or on his lap, he had my baby. So I gasped out of fear, right? I mean, obviously he's a prisoner. He's got nothing to lose anyway. We're going to execute him. Either he could hold the baby ransom or he could execute the baby because he's going to be executed anyway, sort of as a parting message to the tribe.
她继续讲述道：临近行刑时，他索要一把剃刀，以便按照天性清洁身体，为殉教做好准备。他希望以最佳状态迎接真主ﷻ，因此在见证殉教前要整理仪容。他清楚处决时刻将至，便请求给他剃刀。她说：“我递给他剃刀后离开，不料我的幼子趁我不注意爬向他那里。”她描述当时场景：“当我发现孩子不见急忙冲进囚室时，看见胡拜布一手握着剃刀，另一手（或膝上）却抱着我的孩子。”她因恐惧倒吸凉气——毕竟这是个即将被处决的囚徒，完全可以挟持孩子作为筹码，或索性杀死孩子作为对部落的临终报复。
But Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala anhu basically says, look, we're Muslims. We're not like that. So when she sees him in that state and then he sees her gasp, he says, did you think I was going to hurt him? He said, I swear by Allah, I would never do that. So this is not who we are. We're not the people that do these types of things. So she said, ma ra'aytu aseeran qattu khaira min Khubaib. She said, wallahi, I never saw a prisoner better than Khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala anhu.[Sahih al-Bukhari 3767]
然而胡拜布当即表明：“听着，我们是穆斯林，绝非（你猜想的）那类人。”见她惊慌失措的样子，他反问道：“你以为我会伤害他吗？以真主ﷻ发誓，我绝不会这样做。这不是我们的行为准则，我们（穆斯林）绝不行此等恶事。”这位女性最终作证：“以真主ﷻ发誓，我从未见过比胡拜布更高尚的囚徒。”（《布哈里圣训实录》#3767）
Then she says, the time came for his execution. So the tribe took him out to Tan'im to crucify him, away from the Ka'bah to Tan'im to crucify him. So they strung him up to a tree and they said to him, wallahi, we're going to kill you. He said, inna qatli fillahi laqaleel. He said, look, to be killed for Allah is no big deal. And he says the very famous words, walastu ubali hina uqtalu musliman ala ayi jambin kana fillahi masra'ee. He said, I don't care if you kill me. He said, I don't care in any way how I will be killed, as long as it's for the sake of Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala. As long as I'm going to be killed for the sake of Allah, kill me in any way that you're going to kill me, do to me whatever you're going to do to me. I'm satisfied with that being my fate.
她继续讲述道：行刑时刻终于来临。族人将他押往远离天房的坦伊姆地区准备钉死。当把他绑上树时，族人们叫嚣：“以真主ﷻ发誓，我们必杀你。”他却平静回应：“为真主ﷻ殉道何足道哉。”并留下千古名言：“只要是为真主ﷻ献身，无论你们以何种方式处决我，我都毫不在意。只要我的死亡是为真主ﷻ，无论遭受何等酷刑，我都欣然接受。”
So Uqba ibn al-Harith, who is the son of al-Harith now, he has him ready to be crucified. He says to him, tamanna. He said, you have any wishes, any last requests? So Khubayb radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu says, let me pray two rak'ahs. And so he's the first one to establish this practice, which we'll talk about as the series goes on. So he gives him that wish. He goes ahead and he prays two rak'ahs and he prays two rak'ahs quickly. So he didn't take long in his two rak'ahs. And when he finished his two rak'ahs, he said, had I not feared that you people would think that I was afraid I would have prolonged the prayer. Meaning, you know, I didn't want you all to think that I was scared of this fate, otherwise I would have enjoyed my prayer and I would have not rushed it the way that I did. And then he said, Allahumma ahsihim adada waqtulhum badada wa la tughadir minhum ahada Oh Allah, kill each and every single one of them. Do not spare a single person amongst them.
哈里斯之子欧格巴负责行刑前问他：“可有遗愿？”胡拜布请求：“让我礼两拜。”由此开创了殉道者临终礼拜的先例。他迅速礼完两拜后解释道：“若非担心你们误以为我在拖延时间（害怕死亡），我定会延长拜功。”——我不想你们以为我害怕这样的结局，不然我会尽情享受我的礼拜，不会如此匆忙结束。随后他祈祷：“主ﷻ啊！求祢清算他们每一个人，不要放过任何一个。”
Now, Quraysh, they just saw the miracle of Badr, the angels in Badr. They've seen some of the miracles of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam. They know what the dua, what the supplication of the believer is like. So when he said that, Quraysh ducked. They hit the ground thinking that the dua of Khubaib was going to hit them at that moment. Right. They thought something was going to come down from the sky and kill them at that moment. One of them is Abu Sufyan who lives on to tell the story as a Muslim. Again, a lot of the people would move on from this and tell the story as Muslims.
曾见证白德尔战役天使助阵、先知ﷺ身上出现的神迹的古莱氏人，深知信士祈祷的威力。听到这番祈祷时，他们吓得纷纷伏地，唯恐胡拜布的祈祷招来即刻的惩罚——害怕从天而降之力当场杀死他们。后来归信伊斯兰并传述此事的艾布·苏富扬当时也在场，他们中的很多人最终都成为了穆斯林并传述此事。
So Uqba ibn al-Harith is watching Khubaib and he says, don't kill him yet. He says, beat him a bit while he's strung to the tree, you know, torture him a bit. And then Uqba ibn al-Harith wanted to play a mind game with him. So he came up to him and he said, let me ask you Khubaib. He says, afatuhibbu anna muhammadan fee makanik Would you like that Muhammad Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam be in your place wa anta jalisun fee baitik and you could sit at home? Would you like right now that Muhammad Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam is in your place and you get to escape this? He said, wallahi I would not even want a thorn to prick Muhammad Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam wa anta jalisun fee baitik while I'm at home. No, not at all. SubhanAllah, similar to the ending of Sumayyah radiAllahu ta'ala anha. And then there's one more thing he says.
欧格巴命人先对绑在树上的胡拜布施以酷刑，继而玩心理战术，问道：“胡拜布啊！你可愿此刻让穆罕默德ﷺ替你受刑，而自己安然归家？你可愿此刻让穆罕默德ﷺ与你交换，而自己得以逃脱？”胡拜布斩钉截铁地回答：“以真主ﷻ发誓！即便让先知ﷺ被荆棘刺伤而我在家安坐，我也绝不答应！”赞主ﷻ超绝！这与苏曼叶（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）临终的壮烈如出一辙。
As he's about to die, he says, Allahumma, oh Allah laisa haahuna ahadun yuballiq rasoolaka anni salam There is not anyone here to convey to the messenger sallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam my salam faballighu anta anni salam. So, oh Allah, deliver to him salam from me. You send him salam, oh Allah, from me. Now realize again, some of these people would become Muslim later on, but this is an interesting last request. And then he was crucified and he was martyred radiAllahu ta'ala anha.
临终前他发出最后祈求：“主啊！这里无人能替我给使者ﷺ传达“色兰”，求祢亲自将我的问候转达给他。”随后，胡拜布在十字架上壮烈殉教。值得注意的是，当时在场的许多迫害者后来都归信了伊斯兰，而这个特殊的临终祈求更成为永恒的传奇。
Back in Medina, the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam is sitting with the companions. And suddenly the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam, he shakes as if something happens. And the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam says, wa alika salam ya khubaib and upon you is salam, oh khubaib. And the companions are shocked by what the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam just did. And the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam says, hadha Jibreel, Jibreel Alaihi Salam just came to me. yuqri'uni min khubaib as salam And he's giving me salam from khubaib and khubaib is about to be killed by Quraysh.
在麦地那，先知ﷺ正与众圣伴同坐，突然浑身震颤，随即说道：“胡拜布啊，愿平安也归于你。”圣伴们对先知ﷺ突如其来的举动惊诧不已。先知ﷺ解释道：“这是吉卜利勒刚刚向我传达胡拜布的“色兰”问候，此刻胡拜布正遭古莱氏人杀害。”
So subhanAllah, at that very moment, you know, when we send salam on the Messenger Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam, it's after he has passed from this dunya that we maintain that connection by sending salam and the angels send salam to him while we are still alive in this dunya. And he has passed from this dunya Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam. In the case of khubaib radiAllahu ta'ala anhu, khubaib is passing from this dunya to the next realm. And still he is so truthful in his connection to Allah and his connection to the Messenger Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam that Allah does not just choose any angel to take his salam to the Messenger Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam. Allah sends the best of the angels, Jibreel Alaihi Salam, to convey salam to the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam from this great companion Khubaib ibn Adi radiAllahu ta'ala anhu.
赞主ﷻ超绝！这个瞬间蕴含深刻哲理：当我们向已归真的先知ﷺ致“色兰”时，天使会在我们今世生活期间就将问候传达给他。而胡拜布的情况更为特殊——当他即将从此岸渡向彼岸之际，因对真主ﷻ和使者ﷺ至诚的联结，真主ﷻ没有派遣普通天使，而是特派众天使之首吉卜利勒，将这位伟大圣伴胡拜布·本·阿迪的问候亲自传达给先知ﷺ。

EP003 当天空布满天使
When They Fill Up the Skies

Ever wondered what happens when you recite the Qur’an aloud? When the Prophet’s ﷺ companion Usayd ibn Hudair (r) recited the Qur’an, the skies were flooded with angels. His witness of their presence revealed to us what happens when the angels descend to listen to the Qur’an.
你可曾想过，当你高声诵读古兰经时，会发生什么？当先知ﷺ的同伴乌赛德·伊本·胡代尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）诵读古兰时，天空中布满了天使。他亲眼见证天使降临的场景，向我们揭示了当天使降临聆听古兰时发生的神迹。
Usaid ibn Hudhir radiAllahu ta'ala anhu was one of the chiefs in what was of course known at that time, Yathrib, before the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam came to and became Medina. And he was the chief of the tribe of Al-Aws. Remember the Ansar were divided into Al-Aws and Al-Khazraj. And when Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala anhu was sent to Medina to teach the people, to call them to Islam from hajj to hajj, Usaid heard about Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala anhu and he came to him in a state of rage, wanting to stop him from corrupting his people. But Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala anhu with his manners and with his beautiful preaching, was able to convince him to just hear him out for a moment. So Usaid sat and he listened to Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala anhu read the Qur'an to him. And when Mus'ab recited the Qur'an to Usaid, it fell in his heart and immediately SubhanAllah, not only did he fall in love with it, but he became instrumental in getting Medina as a whole to become Muslim. He wanted everyone to hear this magnificent Qur'an that he had heard that caused him to embrace Islam and to call his entire people to Islam.
乌赛德·伊本·胡代尔曾是叶斯里布（在先知ﷺ到来并改名为麦地那之前的地名）的部落首领之一。作为奥斯部落的领袖，他属于辅士中的奥斯族（须知辅士分为奥斯与海兹拉吉两族）。当穆阿兹（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）被派往麦地那教导民众、号召人们从朝觐到朝觐期间皈依伊斯兰时，愤怒的乌赛德本想阻止他“蛊惑人心”。但穆阿兹以优雅的仪态和动人的宣教，说服他暂且倾听。当穆阿兹为其诵读古兰时，经文瞬间浸润了乌赛德的心灵——顷刻间他不仅深爱上启示，更成为推动整个麦地那皈依的关键人物。他渴望所有人都能聆听这使他归信的伟大经典。
So the chapter that we come to is actually a chapter in Sahih Al-Bukhari, which is Bab Nuzul Al-Sakinah Wal-Malaika Ainda Qiraatil Qur'an. The chapter of the descent of Al-Sakinah, the descent of tranquility and the angels when the Qur'an is recited. It's a Hadith where Usaid radiAllahu ta'ala anhu says, that I was reciting Surat Al-Baqarah at night. So imagine him standing outside in his courtyard, reciting it either in a state of prayer or just in a state of recitation. But he's outside at night and his horse was tied beside him. And as he recites Surat Al-Baqarah, the horse seems startled and it starts to tremble. So he said, I stopped reciting and I went out and I looked to see what the horse was seeing and the horse went quiet. So he said, I kept on reciting Surat Al-Baqarah. He said, again, the horse was startled and the horse started to tremble. So he said, I stopped reciting. I started to look around. I said, what's going on here? Why is the horse so shocked by this recitation or by what's happening? So he said, I started again. And he said, SubhanAllah, the third time when he recited the Quran, the horse started to tremble and was startled to the extent that it almost trampled his son Yahya. So he said, my son was out there as well and the horse was so moved that I thought the horse was actually going to trample my son Yahya. So he said, the next morning, I went to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and I told the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam what happened. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, Iqraa ya ibn Hudair, Iqraa, read O ibn Hudair, read O ibn Hudair. And I said to Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, Ya Rasulullah, my son Yahya was there and I thought the horse was actually going to trample him. And he said, when I looked at the sky, I saw a cloud containing what looked like masabih, these lamps or these lanterns in the sky with these clouds. [Sahih Bukhari #5018] So when I stopped reciting, they left. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, wa tadri ma thak, do you know what that is O Usaid? I said, no. He said, tilkal mala'ika. He said, that was the angels, they came close because of your recitation. He said, walaw qaraat la asbahat yandhuru ilayha an-naas la tatawara minhum. And he said, had you continued to recite O Usaid, the people would have come and they would have been able to stare at the angels and nothing would conceal the angels from them. [Sahih Bukhari #5018]
这段圣训出自《布哈里圣训集》「诵读古兰时的宁静与天使降临篇」。乌赛德传述道：“某夜我在庭院中诵读《黄牛章》——或处于礼拜状态，或仅是诵读——当时我的马匹拴在身旁。当我诵读时，马匹突然惊惶战栗。于是我停止诵经外出查看，马匹便恢复平静。当我继续诵读《黄牛》章时，马匹再次惊惶躁动。第三次诵读时，赞主ﷻ超绝！马匹剧烈颤抖竟险些踩踏我的儿子叶哈亚。”次日清晨，先知ﷺ听闻后连声催促：“诵读吧，伊本·胡代尔！继续诵读吧！”乌赛德解释道：“真主ﷻ的使者啊！当时叶哈亚在场，我担心马匹会踩伤他。”他继续说：“我仰望天空时，看见云层中似有明灯闪烁。当我停止诵经，光团便消散了。”先知ﷺ问道：“乌赛德啊，你可知道那是什么？”我回答不知。先知ﷺ揭示：“那是天使！因你的诵经而临近的天使。倘若你持续诵读，黎明时分人们将亲眼目睹天使显现，再无任何遮蔽。”（《布哈里圣训集》#5018段）
So SubhanAllah, if you were to keep on reciting the Quran, the angels would have come down until the morning time and the people would have seen the effect of the angels in the sky. 
赞主ﷻ超绝！持续诵读古兰将使天使降临直至破晓，人们将得见天使显现的奇观。
In another narration, there was a man that was reciting Surah Al-Kahf and he had the exact same experience. And he came to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and he told the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam what happened and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, that was a sakinah, that was tranquility that descended because of qiraat al-Quran, because of the recitation of the Quran. Al-Hafidh bin Hajar RahimAllah Ta'ala, who of course is the main explainer of Sahih Al-Bukhari, he said that a sakinah here actually refers to the malaika, tranquility actually refers to the angels because wherever the angels go, sakinah descends. Wherever the angels go, tranquility descends. And so whether that is a person reciting the Quran and so the angels come and they witness that Quran, especially the Quran at Fajr and the Quran at Asr. When they witness that loud recitation of the Quran, they bring with them a tranquility, they bring with them a peace. And SubhanAllah, that sakinah comes into the household. And Abu Huraira radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu mentions that homes are expanded due to the recitation of the Quran. Not only do they expel the shayateen, not only do they expel the devils, but the angels are present in that home so much that there is sakinah. There is tranquility that overtakes every single element, every single aspect of that home. And that is what is seen then in the presence of the clouds. We know the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam describing to us from the signs of Laylatul Qadr, that there is a tranquil night to the signs that we see within our own hearts when we are in the presence of the Quran for an extended period of time.
在另一段传述中，有人诵读《山洞》章时经历了完全相同的神迹。当他向先知ﷺ禀报时，先知解释道：“那是因诵读古兰而降下的安宁。”《布哈里圣训集》权威注释家伊本·哈杰尔（愿主ﷻ慈悯之）指出：此处的“安宁”实指天使——因为天使所至之处必降临安宁。无论是晨礼与晡礼时分的高声诵经，还是日常的诵读，当天使们前来见证古兰的诵读时，他们便带来充盈的安宁。艾布·胡莱赖（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）曾提及：常诵经的房屋会变得宽敞，不仅能驱逐恶魔，更因天使的驻留而充满笼罩万物的宁静。这种神圣氛围，正如先知ﷺ描述盖德尔夜的征兆——当我们长久沉浸于古兰经时，心灵便能感知这种超然的宁静。

EP004 天使抚面的恩典
A Face Touched by an Angel

What does it mean when they say an angel wiped your face? Companion Jarir ibn Abdullah (r) was the receiver of this unique experience, leading to a slew of blessings throughout his life. What was most incredible about him was that he used what Allah blessed him with to benefit others.
当人们说“天使擦拭了你的面容”时意味着什么？圣门弟子贾里尔·本·阿卜杜拉（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）正是这一独特恩典的获得者，这份殊荣为他的一生带来了源源不断的吉庆。最令人惊叹的是，他始终将真主ﷻ赐予的恩典转化为造福他人的善行。
The title for this companion in the collection of Imam Al-Dhahabi Rahimahullah Ta'ala says it all about him. Al-Amir, Al-Nabil, Al-Jamil, Abu Amr Jarir Ibn Abdullah. Al-Amir, who is the chief, Al-Nabil, the noble one, Al-Jamil, the handsome one, Abu Amr Jarir Ibn Abdullah, radiAllahu ta'ala alaihi wa sallam.
伊玛目·扎哈比（愿主ﷻ慈悯他）在其圣门弟子传记中为这位圣伴所立的称号已说明一切：领袖、尊贵者、俊美者——艾布·阿慕尔·贾里尔·本·阿卜杜拉。
Now, usually the name Jarir Ibn Abdullah, radiAllahu ta'ala anhu, is a name that is kind of lost because he's mistaken often for Jabir Ibn Abdullah, who's a much more famous companion, radiAllahu anhu, and he has a story in this series as well. But Jarir is a very special man and someone who had a very special trait. Now, he was a man from Yemen and he was the chief of his people. And he does not come to embrace Islam until what's known as Amul Wafood, the year of the delegation. So we're talking about nine years after the hijrah of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam from Mecca to Medina. And he hears the message of Islam from Yemen and he brings with him 150 people from Yemen to meet the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam in Medina and to embrace Islam. So Jarir radiAllahu ta'ala anhu says about the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam famously, this is probably the most famous narration about him. He says, ma ra'ani RasoolAllahi SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam mundhu aslamtu illa tabassama fee wajhi. The Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam never saw me from the day that I embraced Islam, except that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam smiled at me. [Sahih Muslim #2475b] Now, of course, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam always would smile in general, but Jarir says, I actually cannot remember a single time that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam looked at me without a smile.
贾里尔·本·阿卜杜拉常被误认为更著名的圣门弟子贾比尔·本·阿卜杜拉（本系列亦有他的故事）。但这位来自也门的部落首领确有其独特之处——他在先知ﷺ迁徙麦地那九年后（史称“代表团之年”），才率领150名族人前来皈依伊斯兰。关于先知ﷺ，贾里尔有一段著名传述：“自从我归信之日起，真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ每次见到我必定展露笑颜。”（《穆斯林圣训集》#2475）虽然先知ﷺ本就常带微笑，但贾里尔强调：我确实记不起先知ﷺ有哪次看我不是面带笑容的。
Now, of course, that's a trait of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, but there's also a story to it with Jarir radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. So what's his story? RasoolAllah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is sitting with his companions in the masjid in Medina, Masjid Nabawi. And in the middle of his speaking, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam stops and he says, yadkhulu min hadhal baab rajun min khayri fee yaman. That someone is going to enter from that door who is from the best of the people of Yemen. [Al-Adab Al-Mufrad #250] And he described him SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, he says, ala wajhihi mashatu malak. And his face has been touched by an angel. His face has been touched by an angel. What an interesting description of him, right? So you're going to see this man that's about to enter into the masjid and his face has been wiped or touched by an angel. So Jarir radiAllahu ta'ala anhu, he comes into the masjid and everybody stops and in complete silence is staring at him. So Jarir walks in, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is all the way at the front of the masjid. Jarir is coming and he's at the back of the masjid and everyone's staring at him. So he says to the person that's nearest to him, he says, ya abdullah, O servant of Allah, right? That's the way they'd call someone they didn't know. He says, ya abdullah, aqala nabiyus SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam fiya shay'a? Did the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam say something about me? You know, he either said something really bad or he said something really good because it's clear that everyone is staring at me because of something the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said. So the man told me what the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said. So I said, alhamdulillah for that. And he went on to embrace Islam and to become this companion of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam in the final year of his life, alayhi salatu wasalam.
诚然，这是先知ﷺ的品格使然，但贾里尔的故事另有深意。当时，真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ正与圣伴们在麦地那圣寺中座谈。讲学中途，先知ﷺ突然停下说道：“将有人从这扇门进来，他是也门最优秀的人。”（《礼节大全》#250）继而描述道：“其面容曾被天使抚拭。”贾里尔入寺时，众人皆屏息凝视——先知ﷺ在寺前端坐，而他自后门而入，所到之处人们无不注目。他询问身旁之人：“真主ﷻ的仆人啊，先知ﷺ是否提及了我？”因他明白，众人如此注视，必是因先知ﷺ或褒或贬的评价。当得知先知ﷺ所言之后，他当即诵念“一切赞颂全归真主ﷻ”，随后归信伊斯兰，成为先知ﷺ生平最后岁月中的圣伴。
Now, what does it mean to say that an angel wiped your face? You might remember the hadith where the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam mentions three men, a man who was bald, a man who was a leopard, and a man who was blind. And an angel comes to each one of those three men and gives them all a different set of rizq, a different set of sustenance. But in the process of that, when the angel wipes the head of the bald man, the man has hair now. When the angel wipes the eyes of the blind man, the blind man can now see. And when the angel wiped the skin of the leopard, the leopard no longer had leprosy. So the ulama say that in the case of Jarir radiAllahu ta'ala an angel wiped his face in such a way that there was no defect. It just beautified him tremendously. He had an extreme beauty to him radiAllahu ta'ala And so anywhere he went, he attracted attention to himself because of the wiping of that angel on his face. And Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala knew what he was giving to this companion of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam
“天使抚面”的奥义可参照另一则圣训：先知ﷺ曾讲述三位受考验者——秃顶者、麻风病人与盲人。天使分别赐予他们给养时，抚其头顶者生出秀发，拭其双目者重见光明，抹其肌肤者痼疾痊愈。学者们阐释：天使抚拭贾里尔面容时，不仅消除了所有缺陷，更赋予他惊人的俊美。无论行至何处，这份来自天使的恩典都使他成为焦点——全知的主ﷻ确已为这位圣门弟子预备了特殊的恩典。
Some of the narrations, he says, I told the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam one time that I had a hard time keeping stable on my horse. You know, he's the chief of his people and he would go out and battle. And he said, I'm having a hard time staying on my horse. He said, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam struck my chest and he said, Allahumma thabbithu waj'alhu haadiyan mahdiyya He said, Oh Allah, keep him firm, meaning on his horse and make him one who is guided and who others are guided through. [Sahih al-Bukhari #4356] So SubhanAllah, the dua of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam for a person is always better than what they seek from the Messenger SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam And he says, wallahi, I never fell off of my horse again after that dua of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam What was his nickname?
据部分传述记载，贾里尔曾向先知ﷺ倾诉：“我骑马时总是难以保持平衡。”作为部落首领，他常需出征作战。先知ﷺ闻言便抚其胸膛祈祷：“主啊！使他稳固（于马背），使他成为得正道者，并成为指引他人者。”（《布哈里圣训集》#4356）赞主ﷻ超绝！先知ﷺ的祈祷远胜人们所求。贾里尔见证道：“以真主ﷻ起誓，自那日后我再未坠马。”
Umar bin al-Khattab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu called him Yusufu haadihil umma The Yusuf of this umma, SubhanAllah So he is the Yusuf of this umma And Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu praised him, he said, ni'ma sayyid kuntu fil jahiliya wa ni'ma sayyid fil Islam What a great chief of your people you were in the days of ignorance and what a great chief of your people you are in Islam [Al-Bidaya Wal-Nihaya]
欧麦尔·本·哈塔卜（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）曾以“优素福”赞誉他，称其为“这个稳麦的优素福”。这位哈里发赞叹道：“你在蒙昧时代是族人杰出的领袖，在伊斯兰时代依然是族人杰出的领袖。”（《始与终》）
Now we don't find much from Jarir radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu in terms of, you know, interactions with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam In fact, most of it surrounds his initial embracing Islam He said, bay'atu RasulAllah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam I gave my allegiance to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam ala shahadati alla ilaha illallah wa anna Muhammadun RasulAllah Of course, upon the shahadah that there is only one God and that Muhammad is the messenger of Allah wa iqam as-salah wa ita'i zaka'a and to establish the prayers and to pay the zakah wa sam'i wa ta'a and that I would hear and that I would obey wa nushi li kulli muslim This is where it gets interesting and that I would be a sincere advisor to every Muslim that I would treat the Muslims with sincerity
关于贾里尔与先知ﷺ的互动记载虽不多见，但大多围绕其归信之初的情形。他传述：“我向真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ宣誓效忠：作证除真主外，绝无应受崇拜者，穆罕默德ﷺ是主的使者；履行拜功，完纳天课；听从并服从；且真诚劝谏每个穆斯林。”
And so SubhanAllah, what we find with Jarir radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu who had this power of persuasion because of the unique way that Allah Subhanahu wa ta'ala blessed him and that an angel wiped his face and he had such beauty like Imran Ibn Hussain radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu when the fitna broke out, when trials and tribulation broke out and people started to fight and power struggles arose he was a person who said, laa uqatilu man qal la ilaha illallah He said, listen, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam sent me back to Yemen with very clear instructions. He says, I'm not gonna fight people that say la ilaha illallah And the ulama say, that's the blessing of Jarir radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu He had the persuasion of his beauty. He had the persuasion of his leadership. He had the persuasion of so many different things. How did he use his persuasion? He used it in da'wah And that's why the ulama say the greatest virtue of him is that on the day of Qadisiyya which was one of the most important battles in the history of Islam, a third of the Muslims that were fighting on the day of Qadisiyya were from the people of Jarir Ibn Abdullah radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu
赞主ﷻ超绝！这位因天使抚面而获非凡俊美与说服力的圣伴，在人们兵刃相见的动乱时期依然坚守原则：“我绝不与诵念‘除真主外，绝无应受崇拜者’的人作战”——学者们指出这正是他的吉庆所在。他运用与生俱来的领袖魅力、出众仪表等天赋进行宣教。最卓越的功绩体现在卡迪西亚战役（伊斯兰史上最重要战役之一）：当日参战穆斯林中有三分之一是贾里尔·本·阿卜杜拉的族人。

EP005 海天之间的天籁
A Voice in the Seas

Distinguished in his eloquence, in battle, and in his recitation of the Qur’an, Abu Musa al-Ash’ari (r) (also known as Abdullah ibn Qais (r)) was a beloved Companion of the Prophet ﷺ from Yemen. On a ship out in the expansive sea, Abu Musa (r) heard the resounding voice of an angel in an unforgettable moment that elevated his worship and taught us about the virtues—and the beautiful reward—of fasting on a long, hot day.
来自也门的先知ﷺ挚友——以雄辩、战功和古兰诵读著称的艾布·穆萨·艾什阿里（愿主ﷻ喜悦之），（也被称为阿卜杜拉·本·盖斯），曾在浩瀚海面的一艘船上亲历天使之声的奇迹。那个永恒瞬间不仅提升了他的崇拜，更向我们揭示了在漫长炎日中封斋的美德与回赐。
So we covered one chief from Yemen that came to Medina to embrace Islam, Jarir ibn Abdullah radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. The most famous companion, arguably though, from Yemen is Abu Musa al-Ash'ari radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. His name was actually Abdullah ibn Qais radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu.
我们已讲述过也门领袖贾里尔·本·阿卜杜拉（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）在麦地那皈依伊斯兰的故事，而也门圣伴中最负盛名的当属艾布·穆萨·艾什阿里。他的名字实际上是阿卜杜拉·本·盖斯。
This was a man who came to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam early on, before Amul Wafud, before the year of the delegation to embrace Islam and went back to his people and brought a bunch of them back to Medina with him and accompanies the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and becomes not only a beloved companion to him, but distinguishes himself in his fiqh, meaning his scholarship and his knowledge. So becomes a person who is looked to by the companions of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam for his fiqh, becomes distinguished in his eloquence, becomes distinguished in his ability in battle. So he was a Mujahid fi sabiLillah and that's something that he would carry forward. And he was also a diplomat, a general, and also becomes distinguished for his recitation of the Quran. The Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam praised the recitation of Abu Musa radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu in many different ways, said to him, he has been given the Zabur of Dawud alayhi salam. So he is someone who the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam loves deeply and who excels in recitation, excels in remembrance and excels in knowledge and in so many different ways.
他早在“代表团之年”前就归信伊斯兰，并带领族人前往麦地那追随先知ﷺ，不仅成为先知钟爱的圣伴，更以法学造诣著称——圣伴们常向他请教教律。他兼具雄辩之才、军事才能，是为主道奋斗的战士，同时还是外交家、将领，更以优美的古兰诵读闻名。先知ﷺ曾多次称赞他的诵经：“他确已获得达乌德先知的《宰逋尔》经典。”这位在诵读、记主ﷻ、学识等多方面出类拔萃的弟子，深得先知ﷺ的喜爱。
The story of Abu Musa radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu though, when it comes to an angel, is a story that he narrates about himself and a group of companions. And he says that we were traveling by sea and they were clearly out in jihad fi sabiLillah. So this was for the sake of battle. And as the ship was sailing, it was going fast towards its destination. He said, suddenly there was a voice that was heard by all of us. And the voice said, Ya Ahlul Safina, Oh people of the ship, Qifu, ukhbirukum bi qadha in qadha hullahu ala nafsihi Stop and let me tell you of a judgment that Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la or a decree that Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la has placed on himself. So he said, I looked to the right, I looked to the left. I did not find anyone and the voice repeated the exact same thing consecutively seven times. So subhanAllah, imagine the people on the ship are like, where is this voice coming from? That let me tell you of a decree that Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la has decreed. So he said, finally, when I looked around and I couldn't find where the voice was coming from, I called out and I said, man ant, ayna ant, who are you? Where are you? And he said, the answer came, inna allaha qadha ala nafsihi innahu man ta'atasha lillahi fiyawmin haar saqaahullahu yawmil a'tash SubhanAllah, beautiful. He said that the voice responded and said that Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la has decreed upon himself that whoever fasts for the sake of Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la on a hot day, Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la will nourish him on the day of great thirst, which is the day of judgment. So SubhanAllah, if a person fasts for the sake of Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la on a difficult day, a day where it is hot, a day where people will be thirsty, Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la will make sure that that person is nourished on the day of judgment, a day of great thirst. Abu Musa radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu said, ever since the day that that angel shouted that, I never missed fasting on a hot day.
艾布·穆萨亲述的这段天使奇遇发生在海上征程中。当时他与同伴们乘船为主道出征，船只正快速航行时，突然所有人都听见一个声音反复呼喊七次：“船上的人们啊！请停下，我要传达真主ﷻ为自己制定的判决。”他们四处寻找声源，而四周空无一人，于是艾布·穆萨喊道：“你是谁？你在何处？”那个声音回应道：“真主ﷻ确已为自己规定：谁为真主ﷻ在炎日封斋，审判日那天，真主ﷻ必使他免于干渴。”赞主ﷻ超绝，若有人为真主ﷻ在炎炎烈日下封斋，在那人们最易口渴的日子封斋，真主ﷻ会在审判日时滋养他。自此，艾布·穆萨再未错过任何炎热的斋戒日。
Now I want to contextualize this a bit because it's very interesting because you find many narrations from the companions praising the easy fasts. Fasting in the winter, for example, it's the spoils of the believer. Why? Because it's so easy to fast in the winter, right? The days are short for fasting. The nights are long for prayer, but there is a unique reward as well to fasting on a long, hot day. Now the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said in general, Man saama yawman fee sabili Allah ba'daAllahu wajhuhu AAan alnaari sabAAeena khareefa. The Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said whoever fasts one day for the sake of Allah, Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la will distance that person's face from the fire. The distance of a journey of 70 years. [Sahih al-Bukhari #2840]
这段圣训需要特别阐释其背景：圣伴们常赞美冬季斋戒的易行——因其白昼短暂、黑夜漫长，封斋和礼拜皆更为容易，故被称为“信士的战利品”。但与此同时，在炎热长日中的斋戒却蕴含独特的回赐。先知ﷺ曾概括性地宣告：“谁为主道封斋一日，真主ﷻ就使他的面容远离火狱，其距离如七十年的旅途。”（《布哈里圣训集》#2840）
And another narration, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, bayna al-mashriqi wal-maghrib, the distance between the east and the west. And another narration, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, the distance bayna al-sama'i wal-arw, the distance between the heavens and the earth. So fasting one day for the sake of Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la is a great deed that brings about a great reward. But here you find the sweetness of struggle. The sweetness of doing the hard deeds, doing the deeds that will require a greater amount of effort and leaving that gap of the struggle between the easy deed and the hard deed to Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la. And you're not encountering a deed just because it's hard. You're not encountering a deed because you think that, you know, there should be unnecessary difficulty that's placed upon yourself. But you are consistently acting upon deeds, even when there will be difficulty because of the circumstances. And that is the blessing of fasting on a long, hot day.
在另一段传述中，先知ﷺ说：“（其距离）如东西方之遥。”还有传述提到：“如天地之距。”为主道封斋一日确能获得巨大回赐。但这里更彰显了“艰难功修的甜美”——不是刻意追求苦修，亦非强加无谓的困难，而是为真主ﷻ欣然接受环境带来的考验。当你在酷暑长日坚持斋戒时，正是将易行与艰难之间的差距交付于真主ﷻ，这种不因环境而坚持的功修，恰是炎热斋戒日的特殊恩典。
EP006 至仁主ﷻ应答之时
When the Most Merciful Is Invoked

Has a du’a ever saved your life? Many of us have experienced life-changing du’as, but few so literally as Zayd ibn Harithah (r), a Companion so steeped in his conviction in Islam that even an attempt on his life could not stop him from prayer and supplication. And when an angel armed with a spear came to his rescue, the benefit of his conviction in the Most Merciful became crystal clear.
可曾有一段祈祷拯救过你的生命？我们大多经历过改变人生的祈祷，但如宰德·本·哈里斯（愿主ﷻ喜悦之）这般直接的奇迹却属罕见。这位信念坚定的圣伴，即便遭遇生命威胁也未曾中断拜功与祈祷——当持矛天使现身相救时，他对至仁主ﷻ的虔信得到了最清晰的印证。
I think people forget sometimes that though the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam was alone in Ta'if for many of the moments of Ta'if, there was one companion that was with him and that was Zayd ibn Haritha radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. So imagine being there for the worst moments of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam's life.
人们常遗忘：虽然先知ﷺ在塔伊夫大多时刻形单影只，但宰德·本·哈里斯始终相伴左右。试想，他亲历了先知ﷺ生命中最艰难的时光。
When the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam is being mocked in the worst way, when the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam is being humiliated, when the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam is being hit with stones and shoes, Zayd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was the one who was with the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam in Ta'if and was actually trying to stop the stones as they were coming towards the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and also took a beating himself. He's also that one companion that was entrusted with accompanying the family of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam on the hijrah. So Fatima radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, Um Kulthum radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, Saudah radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, they all went with Zayd ibn Haritha to Al-Madinah and of course he himself, his position with the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam, he was Hib Rasulillahi Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam. He was the loved one of the Messenger of Allah Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and that's a lofty position to have because what it tells us is that loving Zayd is a sunnah. If the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam loves him so much that he is the beloved one of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam, then to love him is a sunnah and it's a great way of enjoying closeness to the Messenger Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam.
当先知ﷺ在塔伊夫遭受最恶毒的嘲弄、羞辱，被投掷石块与鞋子时，宰德始终守护在侧，甚至以身阻挡飞向先知ﷺ的石头而遍体鳞伤。这位被先知ﷺ称为“真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ挚爱”的圣伴，还肩负着护送先知家眷迁徙的重任——法蒂玛、乌姆·库勒苏姆和索黛（愿主ﷻ喜悦她们）都由他护送至麦地那。因此它告诉我们，喜爱宰德本身就是一项圣行，如果先知ﷺ如此爱他，以至于他是先知挚爱之一，那么爱他就是一种圣行。因这能使人临近先知ﷺ的品级。
So this story that I'm going to share with you, it's a narration from Al-Laith ibn Sa'd, it's recorded as well by Ibn Abdul Barr and Ibn Kathir and Al-Bidai wa-Nihaya, that Zayd ibn Haritha radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu once rented a mule from a man in Ta'if. So he's out on this journey and he asks to rent this mule to ride. And the man tells him that, okay, I'll rent it to you for such and such price, but at the same time, I have a shalt, I have a condition. He said, what is the shalt? He said, I need you to take me to a particular place. After we get to this place, you can drop me off there and then we'll agree upon a time that you'll be able to ride and you come back and you'll pay me and that'll be the end of that. So it seems like a pretty normal transaction, right? So Zayd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu says, so I agree to the condition and this man from Ta'if tells me, go this way, go this way, go this way until we ended up in a very strange alleyway, right? This is something that you might actually be able to relate to in that sense. We end up in a very strange alleyway. And when we got to this alleyway, I see a bunch of dead bodies. This is a very dark and scary situation. So Zayd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu said, I realized as soon as we got there, what the man was about, right? That basically the man led me to this place. He was going to rob me. He was going to kill me and he was going to throw me with the rest of the dead bodies. So Zayd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu looks at him and he said, the man, you know, they got off the mule and the man was walking up to him to kill him. And Zayd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu said, I said to him, da'ani usalli rak'ateen Look, can you let me pray two rak'ahs before you kill me? SubhanAllah, this becomes a sunnah by the way of sorts. When you see many scholars in history that would ask as they were about to be killed by their tyrants, give me a chance just to pray a couple of rak'ahs. Let me pray two rak'ahs to Allah subhanAllah. Let the last action be salah. Okay, if you're going to do this anyway. So the man says to him, he mocks him. He says, you think the prayer did any good for these people, right? Did salah do any of these people good? He said, go ahead and pray. Okay, fine. Go ahead and pray. So Zayd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu said, so I started to pray. And he said, when I started to pray, the man was not patient with me finishing my prayer. He walked up to me to kill me. So as he walked up to me to kill me, I said, Ya arham ar-raahimin O most merciful of those who show mercy.
所以我要和你们分享的这个故事——据莱斯·本·赛尔德、伊本·阿卜杜·巴尔、伊本·凯西尔传述，在《始与终》中也有记载。宰德曾在塔伊夫租用一头骡子。双方约定：宰德以特定价格租用骡子，但需先载出租者至某地。抵达后，宰德可继续骑行，按时归还并支付费用。看似寻常的交易，却暗藏杀机——出租者指引宰德穿行于曲折巷道，最终来到一条堆满尸骸的恐怖巷弄。宰德顿悟对方意图谋财害命，欲将其弃尸于此。凶手逼近时，他请求：“让我先礼两拜再动手吧！”赞主ﷻ超绝，这后来成为许多学者临刑前的圣行式请求。当你看到历史上许多学者在他们即将被暴君杀害时会问，“请给我一个机会，让我礼几拜。让我向真主ﷻ礼两拜。让我最后的行为是礼拜。”凶手讥讽道：“你以为礼拜能救这些人吗？”应允后却未待宰德礼完就举刀相向。千钧一发之际，宰德疾呼：“至仁至慈的主ﷻ啊！”
And suddenly we heard a voice, la taqtulhu Don't kill him. So he said, the man was surprised by the voice. So he went out to look and see where the voice was coming from. Is there someone that is approaching and that's going to stop him or attack him? But he found nothing. So Zayd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu said, I continued in my salah and he approached me again to kill me. And I said, Ya arham ar-raahimin O most merciful of those who show mercy. And he said, the same thing happened. He went out. He looked to see where that voice was coming from. He couldn't figure it out. And he said, the third time he comes to me again to do it. And I say, Ya arham ar-raahimin O most merciful of those who show mercy. And he said, when he did that, and I said that, Suddenly I saw a man coming forward and he had a spear. And at the tip of that steel spear was fire. And this horseman throws the spear directly through that man and it kills him. And then the horseman turns to me. And he says, when you called out, Ya arham ar-raahimin O most merciful of those who show mercy. Qala kuntu fissamaa ssabiaa I was in the seventh heaven. So he's an angel. He's making it clear to him that I'm an angel. I was in the seventh heaven when you called out, Ya arham ar-raahimin And when you called out the second time, Ya arham ar-raahimin He said, by the time you called out the second time, he said, kuntu fissamaa dunya I had just come down to the heaven directly above the earth. And he said, when you called out and said, Ya arham ar-raahimin The third time, he said, I made it to you. By that time, I made it to you. [Al-Isti'ab fi ma'rifat al-ashab]
突然，听到一个声音，“不要杀他”。凶手被这个声音吓了一跳，他惊惶四顾，却未见人影。所以宰德继续他的礼拜。凶手再次逼近，宰德二次呼求：“至仁至慈的主ﷻ啊！”同样喝止声再现，凶手仍无所获。（就这样）第三次，宰德呼求时，但见一位手持燃焰长矛的天使策马而来，将凶手刺穿毙命。天使转身道：“当你首次呼求‘至仁至慈的主ﷻ啊’时，我尚在第七重天；第二次呼求时，我已降至近天；第三次呼求时，我便抵达此处。"（《圣门弟子认知大全》）
And subhanAllah, that is when he actually killed the man who intended to kill Zayd ibn Harithah radiAllahu ta'ala anhu Who would live of course to become a martyr in the battle of Al-Mu'tah. So this is a powerful example, subhanAllah, not just for Zayd ibn Harithah radiAllahu ta'ala anhu But in general, the way that Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala protected these companions And it's not just in the battles which we'll talk about But even the idea that when they called out to Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala with such conviction That the heavens would be moved by the decree of Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala To come to the aid of that person, to where this angel comes from the highest of the heavens To support this man who is one of the highest of the companions And the most beloved of Rasulullah sallAllahu alayhi wa sallam
赞主ﷻ超绝！这位后来在穆尔塔战役荣膺烈士的圣伴，以亲身经历诠释了至仁主ﷻ的护佑。这不仅彰显宰德的崇高地位，更昭示着：当仆人以坚定信念向主ﷻ呼求时，诸天必将因真主ﷻ的旨意而动——最高处的天使穿越七重苍穹，只为援救先知ﷺ挚爱的弟子。
EP007 一段搞砸的抢劫
A Botched Robbery

Du’a is the most powerful tool of the believer, granting relief in even the most hopeless situations. As Abu Mi’laq al-Ansari (r) witnessed for himself when his life was threatened, a sincere du’a made in need calls down angels from the heavens. His experience embodied the hadith, “Beware of the supplication of the oppressed, even if he is a disbeliever, for there is nothing to veil it.”
祈祷是信士最强大的武器，即便在最绝望的处境中也能带来解救。正如艾布·米拉格·安萨里（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）亲身经历的生死威胁所示，真诚的需求祈祷能召唤天界的天使。他的经历完美诠释了圣训：“当警惕被压迫者的祈祷，即便是非信士，因其祈祷绝无遮蔽。”
So this next narration is actually almost identical to the narration of Zaid ibn Harith radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, but there's something special to it as well. It's narrated by Al-Hafidh ibn Hajar rahimAllah al-Isaba, Imam ibn al-Athir. It's also mentioned by the likes of Imam ibn al-Qayyim rahimAllah ta'ala. However, it doesn't have an authentic chain to the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam. So it can't be attributed to the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam. So the story has great benefit in it, primarily because of the lesson of du'a which we will find in it. But at the same time, we don't trace it back to the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam.
这个传述与宰德·本·哈里斯（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）的故事几乎完全相同，但有其独特之处。它是由哈菲兹·伊本·哈哲尔（愿主ﷻ慈悯他）和伊玛目伊本·艾西尔（愿主ﷻ慈悯他）传述。伊本·盖伊姆（愿主ﷻ慈悯他）也曾提及。然而其传述链无法可靠追溯至先知ﷺ，因此不能归为圣训。这个故事蕴含重大教益，核心在于教导祈祷的力量，但不可将其归为先知ﷺ言行。
So this narration is about a man by the name of Abu Mi'laq al-Ansari radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. And Abu Mi'laq radiAllahu anhu was known as a merchant who was distinguished with his ibadah and distinguished with his integrity. So he's known for his worship. He is known for his integrity in business. And Anas radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu says that he was out on business one day when an armed robber comes up to him and confronts him. The armed robber says to him, سلم متاعك فإني قاتلك He said go ahead and give me everything you have because I'm going to kill you. Okay, or else I'm going to kill you. Now Abu Mi'laq radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu responds, He says خذ مالي ما شأنك وقتلي He says take all of my money. I'll give you everything that you asked for but why do you want to kill me? You know, subhanAllah one of the signs of the day of judgment is what that neither the murderer nor the one who is being murdered understand why the murder is taking place, right?
故事主角艾布·米拉格·安萨里以虔诚礼拜和诚信经商闻名。据艾纳斯（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）传述：某日他外出经商时遭遇持械劫匪。劫匪威胁：“交出财物！我要杀你！”艾布·米拉格回应：“拿走钱财便是，何必害命？”这印证了末日征兆之一——杀人者与被杀者皆不知杀戮缘由。
Just killing becomes so prevalent that people kill just because, right? So he's saying to him, why do you want to kill me? I'm going to give you everything that I have and there is no need for that. And the robber says the money is mine anyway, but I want to kill you anyway, subhanAllah with no enmity and this is of course the hardness of the heart, you know, where you see a serial killer who finds absolutely no hesitation anymore and just shedding blood for the sake of it. So Abu Mi'laq radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu similar to Zayd Ibn Harith radiAllahu Anhu, he says to him, listen, if you're going to kill me anyway, will you give me permission to pray a few rak'ahs and in his narration he says four rak'ahs. So let me let me just pray as you kill me. So the man laughs at him and he says, as-salah, he says prayer, really? He says, go ahead and pray as much as you want. I'll let you pray as much as you want, but I'm going to kill you anyway.
他对劫匪说：“你为什么想杀我？我会把我所有的东西都给你，没有必要这样做。”劫匪宣称：“钱财终归我手，但我仍要取你性命。”这显露出心坚硬如磐石——嗜杀成性者视流血如儿戏。艾布·米拉格如宰德·本·哈里塞一样，请求道：“既如此，容我礼几拜如何？”在他的传述中，他说的是四拜。劫匪嗤笑：“礼拜？尽管礼！你终将殒命。”
So Abu Mi'laq radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, he makes wudu, the man gives him the ability to make wudu and to perform salah and then in his sajda, and this is why the scholars really narrate this story in his sajda, he calls out to Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala on his prostration and he says, ya wadood, ya wadood, ya wadood, ya wadood, ya dhal arshil majeed, ya fa'alan lima yureed, as'aluka bi izzika alladhi la yuram, wa mulkika alladhi la yudham, wa bi noorika alladhi mala arkan arshik, an takfiyani sharra hadha lis, ya mughith aghithni, ya mughith aghithni, ya mughith aghithni. So ya wadood, almost loving and he calls out to Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala with this name three times and he says, the one who possesses the mighty throne and he calls out to Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala, fa'alan lima yureed, the one who does as he pleases and he says, I ask you by your honor that none can hope to have, no one can have the izzah that you have O Allah, and by your kingdom that no one can take away from, and by your light that fills the arkan, that fills the pillars of your throne, subhanAllah, what a beautiful dua, the light that is within the pillars, the foundations of your arsh. He said, I ask you O Allah to protect me from this liss, from this robber and he says, ya mughith aghithni, ya mughith aghithni, ya mughith aghithni O helper, do help me, O helper, help me, O helper, help me, save me O Allah, subhanAllah, this dua has all the meanings. I mean things that are taken from the sunnah of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam but it comes from the heart and it's a very beautifully constructed dua in a moment that is very traumatic. So when he made this dua, a rider, a horseman suddenly appears with a spear held high above his head and just like in the case of Zayd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, he throws his spear at this robber and he kills him. So Abu Mi'laq radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu says to him, who are you? Fana ant, and he says, ana malakun minas sama'i rabia, I am an angel from the fourth heaven.
艾布·米拉格做完小净开始礼拜。在叩头中，他发出震撼人心的祈祷：“至仁主ﷻ啊！至仁主ﷻ啊！至仁主ﷻ啊！拥有尊贵宝座的主ﷻ啊！为所欲为的主ﷻ啊！我以祢不可企及的尊荣，永不消逝的国权，充满阿尔什支柱的圣光祈求：使我免遭此盗之害！拯救者啊，拯救我吧！拯救者啊，拯救我吧！拯救者啊，拯救我吧！”赞主ﷻ超绝！这段祈祷涵盖了一切，它从先知ﷺ的圣行中提取，但发自内心，是在危难之际脱口而出的一段非常美丽的祈祷。所以当他做这个祈祷时，一个骑手（天使）突然出现，他的长矛高高举过头顶，就像宰德的情况一样，他把长矛扔向这个强盗，然后杀了他。所以艾布·米拉格对他说，“你是谁？”他说，“我是来自第四层天的天使。”
He said when you made the first dua, he said the doors of the heavens rattled, subhanAllah, qaqa'a, like there was a rattling of the gates of the heavens and he said when you called out to Allah with the same dua the second time, he said I heard the inhabitants of the heavens cry out and they said that this is a person who's calling upon Allah subhanAllah, the third time it was announced, dua'u makroob, the dua of someone that is in distress. So I asked permission from Allah subhanAllah ta'ala to grant me the ability to kill the robber and Allah subhanAllah ta'ala sent me to you.
他说，“当你第一次祈祷时，天门震动了，赞主ﷻ超绝，咔咔作响，就像天门的铰链发出震动声。当你第二次以同样的祈祷向真主ﷻ呼求时，我听见天上的居民们呼喊，他们说这是正在祈求真主ﷻ的人，赞主ﷻ超绝。第三次宣告说这是受困者的祈祷，于是我向真主ﷻ祈求赐予我击杀强盗的能力，真主ﷻ便派遣我来找你。
SubhanAllah, amman yujeebu al-mutarra idha daAAa wa yakshifu al-su as Allah subhanAllah ta'ala says, who answers the caller when they call out to Allah and removes that distress. Now, there's so much to talk about here. It's very similar to Zayd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, but of course, there's the dua. However, I want to actually make a point about the dua itself. As beautiful as it sounds, a person could take that dua just like any dua from the Sunnah of the Prophet sallAllahu alaihi wasallam and this is of course even of a lesser status, right? Then a dua authentically narrated from the Prophet sallAllahu alaihi wasallam and say this is the one that's going to save me. But the reality is what?
赞主ﷻ超绝，当他们向真主ﷻ呼求时，祂ﷻ应答受困者的祈求，并解除苦难。正如真主ﷻ所说：“当他们向真主ﷻ呼求，并解除苦难，谁应答受难者的祈祷？”这里有很多值得探讨的地方。这很像宰德的经历，但当然这里涉及的是祈祷。不过我想重点谈谈这个祈祷本身。虽然它听起来很美好，但人们可能会像对待先知ﷺ圣训中传述的任何祈祷词那样对待这个祈祷，而它当然属于更低一等的地位，对吗？毕竟比起先知ﷺ真实传述的祈祷词，人们会说“这才是能拯救我的”。但事实究竟如何呢？
That when this person who we don't know much about, right? They're not of the status of being like Zayd ibn Harith radiAllahu ta'ala anhu, but when this person called upon Allah sincerely, then the sincerity of that dua manifested in such a beautiful way, a way that we can still admire today. However, what is the reality of the status of this dua? It's what the Prophet sallAllahu alaihi wasallam said that the dua of a mazloom person, the dua of someone who is being wronged, there is no hijab. There is no veil between that supplication and Allah subhanaw wa ta'ala, even if that person is a disbeliever. [Musnad Ahmad #12549]
这个人的背景我们并不清楚。他没有达到像宰德·本·哈里斯那样的地位，但当他真诚地向真主ﷻ祈求时，那份祈祷的真诚以如此美妙的方式显现出来，至今仍令我们赞叹。然而，这个祈祷的真实地位究竟如何？正如先知ﷺ所说：被压迫者的祈祷、受不公之人的祈求是没有屏障的。这份祈求与真主ﷻ之间绝无阻隔，即使那人是个不信者。（《艾亥迈得圣训集》#12549）
If Allah hears the dua of a person who is a disbeliever, but they call upon Allah and they are actually wronged and they're affirming the oneness of God in the capacity of that supplication, what then of a mu'min, what then of a believer who is in the midst of being wrong and that believer calls out to Allah subhanaw ta'ala with the most sincere of duas and in words that are not scripted but are laced with the sincerity that causes one's supplication to be answered.
倘若真主ﷻ应答一个不信者的祈祷，当他向真主ﷻ呼求且确实遭受不公，并在祈祷中承认独一主宰的存在。那么，那信士的祈祷又如何呢？当一个正遭受不义的信士，以最真诚的祈祷向真主ﷻ呼求，用未经编排却充满真诚、足以使祈求得以应答的言辞倾诉时，他的处境又将如何？

EP008 默默无闻的辅士
The Unknown Ansari

A believer who is sick is surrounded by angels all the time. What status do the sick hold with Allah and why do angels stay with them? A beautiful instance of this comes in the form of a noble visitor to the home of an Ansari man.
患病的信士时刻被天使环绕。生病的信士在真主ﷻ跟前拥有怎样的地位？天使为何常伴他们左右？一位尊贵的访客来到某位辅士家中的故事体现了这一美好的例证。
Abdullah ibn Abbas radiAllahu anhuma, he tells this story. It's an authentic narration at Tabarani of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam going to visit one of the people of the Ansar. And similar to the last narration, this is a man that's relatively unknown. In fact, we don't even have his name in the narration. He's just Rajulan min al-Ansar, a man from amongst the Ansar.
阿卜杜拉·本·阿巴斯（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）讲述了这个故事。这是《塔巴拉尼圣训集》中记载的先知ﷺ去探望某位辅士族人的真实传述。与先前那个故事类似，这位辅士也相对默默无闻。事实上，传述中甚至没有记载他的名字，仅以“一位辅士族人”代称。
So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam went to visit him because he heard he was sick. So Abdullah ibn Abbas radiAllahu anhuma, he says, Falamma dana min manzilihi, when the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam came close to his home, Samia'ahu yatakallamu fiddaakhil, he heard him speaking to someone inside the home. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is drawing near to the house and it seems like he's speaking to someone on the inside. However, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, he knocks on the door and he asks permission to enter. And when the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam comes in, he sees that there is no one with the Ansari. So he says to him, Samia'atuka tukallimu ghayrak, he said, I heard you speaking to someone else. So the Ansari man responds and he says to him that, Ya Rasulullah, I had gone out and I came back home. Ikhtimaman bikalam an-nas mimma bi minal humma, because I was grieved by what people were saying about my illness. So that could mean that either there was some sort of appearance that came as a result of the illness, he looked sick or people treated him in a certain way because he was contagious. So even though the narration says humma, which is a fever, it can mean any type of illness that made him repulsive to the people. So he continues with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and he says, Fadakhala AAalayya daakhilun, so someone came with me to my house. Someone entered into the house after me. And listen to how he describes and he says, Wallahi ma ra'aytu rajulan qattu ba'daka akraman majlisan wa la ahsana hadithan minhum. He said that I swear by Allah, I have never seen a man after you, Ya Rasulullah, who is more noble of a companion nor more beautiful in their words. So he's saying to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, this mysterious person entered behind me and he sat with me and he kept me company and I've never seen anyone more beautiful than him except for you, Ya Rasulullah. Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam says, Do you know who that was? He says, No. He says, Thalika Jibreel, that was Jibreel AAalayhi As-Salam. And he says, Wa innahu minkum la rijal, law anna ahadahum yuqsimu AAala Allah la abarrahu. He said, I swear by Allah, there are people amongst you that if they were to take oaths upon Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala, Allah would honor those oaths. [Majma' al-Zawaaid 276:9]
先知ﷺ因听闻他患病而前往探望。阿卜杜拉·本·阿巴斯传述：当先知ﷺ临近他的居所时，听见他在屋内与人交谈。然而当先知ﷺ敲门请求进入后，发现屋内并无他人。于是先知ﷺ问道：“我听见你在与他人交谈。”这位辅士回答：“真主ﷻ的使者啊！我外出归来后，因人们对我病情的议论而倍感苦闷。（可能指因患病导致容貌改变，或因传染性遭人避讳。虽然圣训原文用‘胡玛’指发烧，但可泛指任何令人避之不及的病症）此时有人随我进入家中，与我共处。”接着他如此描述：“以真主ﷻ发誓！真主ﷻ的使者啊，除您之外，我从未见过比他更尊贵更俊美的陪伴者，也从未听过比他更优美的言辞。”先知ﷺ问："可知那人是谁？"答曰不知。先知ﷺ说：“那是吉卜利勒（愿他平安）。以真主ﷻ起誓，你们中确有这样的人物，若有人以真主ﷻ之名起誓，真主ﷻ必使其誓言成真。"（《珠瓦伊德圣训集》9：276）
Now before I continue with the connection of this narration to other narrations, I want to stop for a moment and connect this to what we learned about a person who is sick in the first place, right? When you read the ahadith about a person who is sick, that person, that believer who is sick is surrounded by angels all the time. There are the angels that come to visit. There are the angels that receive the visitors. You are surrounded by the malaika at all times because you're in a state of constant expiation of sins, right? While the sickness is with you in whatever capacity, even if it's a minor illness, right? The Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, even if it's something as simple as a fever, the angels remain in your company the entire time and 70,000 angels go with the one who goes to visit the sick person. Of course, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam mentioned to us that on the day of judgment as Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala is speaking to the person who neglected the opportunities of khair, the opportunities of good, Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala says, I was sick and you did not visit me. And the person would say, how could I visit you, O Allah, and you are the Lord of the world. And Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala would respond, don't you know that so-and-so was sick and had you visited that person, you would have found me with them. [Sahih Muslim #2569]
在继续探讨这则圣训与其他传述的关联之前，我想暂停一下，将其与我们先前关于患病者的认知联系起来。当你研读关于患病者的圣训时，会明白那位生病的信士时刻被天使环绕。有探病的守护天使，有接待访客的天使，因为此刻你正处于持续赎罪的状态。正如先知ﷺ所言：只要病症存在（即便只是轻微发热），天使便始终相伴，且七万天使随同探病者而来。当然，先知ﷺ也告诉我们：复生日，当真主ﷻ质问那些忽视行善机会之人，“我患病时你为何不来探望”，那人将疑惑“主啊！祢乃养育众世界的主宰，我如何能探望您？”真主ﷻ便回应：“难道你不知道吗？当某人患病时，你若探望他，必能发现我与他同在。”（《穆斯林圣训集》#2569）
And so here you have this random man from the Ansar who not just any angel follows into his home, but Jibreel Alayhi Salaam takes the form of a human being and visits this man. And that's why the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam then even says out of ajab, out of amazement, that there are people amongst you. What does he mean by that? There are people amongst you that are relatively unknown, but at the same time they have such a position with Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala. And that's where you find some of the other narrations. The Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam says, that it might be that there's a person that's covered in dirt, turned away from the homes of the people. And if that person were to take an oath upon Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala, Allah would certainly honor that oath.
而此处这位普通的辅士族人，不仅迎来天使探访，更是吉卜利勒化身人形亲临探视。因此先知ﷺ惊叹道：“你们中确有这般人物。”意指：你们中虽有人默默无闻，却在真主ﷻ跟前享有崇高地位。这也与其他圣训呼应：先知ﷺ曾说“或许有个蓬头垢面、被世人拒之门外者，若他以真主ﷻ之名起誓，祂ﷻ必使其誓言成真。”
And another narration, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, should I tell you who the people of Jannah are? So they said, yes, O Messenger of Allah. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, He said, every da'eef mutada'af, okay? Da'eef is someone who, it means weak technically, but here it's a person that sees humility, a person that is downtrodden. And mutada'af is that that person is made to be in such a situation. And it's because of their praiseworthy characteristics, either that they are inflicted with poverty and put into hardship, or it's because of the hardship that they're in, that praiseworthy characteristics come out of them, right? So they bear the fruit of humility in the process of being people that don't hold any weight in society. But they are so great in the sight of Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala, if they were to take an oath upon Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala, Allah would honor their oath. And that's greater than even the answered supplications, right? So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is teaching us that there are people amongst us that are worthy of being visited by Jibreel Alayhi Salam, but you would not let them into your home. And that's why you never turn away from anyone. So it's instructive to you when you're sick and when you don't have much prominence in society. And it's also instructive to a person that you don't look away from someone else just because they don't have prominence in society.
另一则圣训中，先知ﷺ说：“要我告诉你们天堂的居民是谁吗？”众人答：“当然，真主ﷻ的使者啊！”先知ﷺ道：“就是那些弱势受压迫者（ضَعِيف）（مُتَضَعَّف）。”（ضَعِيف）本指身体羸弱之人，但此处指心怀谦卑、处境困顿者。（مُتَضَعَّف）则指因可嘉美德（或因清贫陷入困境，或因磨难淬炼出美德）而处于此种境遇之人。他们在社会底层默默无闻，却以谦卑之果彰显价值，在真主ﷻ眼中，他们如此尊贵，以至若以真主ﷻ之名起誓，祂ﷻ必使其誓言成真。这甚至比应答祈祷更显殊荣。先知ﷺ以此教导我们：你们中间确有值得吉卜利勒亲临探视之人，而你们却不会让他们踏入家门。因此，永远不要轻视任何人。这启示患病者与社会边缘人，当明白自身价值，也警示世人莫因他人地位卑微而冷眼相待。
EP009 一位非凡女性的非凡归宿
A Special Home for a Special Woman

People with good qualities are only further polished with Islam. In the case of Khadijah bint Khuwaylid (ra), her sacrifices and her grace earned her a special position from Allah in a message delivered to the Prophet ﷺ from Jibreel (as).
品性高洁之人，会在伊斯兰的淬炼中愈发闪耀。赫蒂彻·宾特·胡韦利德（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）正是如此。在吉卜利勒（愿他平安）向先知ﷺ传达的天启中，因赫蒂彻的牺牲与美德，她获得了真主ﷻ赐予的特殊地位。
So we've spoken about the possibility of a person whose status is unknown to be visited by the likes of Jibreel Alayhi Salaam. And of course, for us as believers, when Laylatul Qadr comes around, right? Tanazlil Malaikatu wal roohu fiha. To have the descent of Jibreel Alayhi Salaam is very special. But what about a woman whose status is known by everyone? And that is Khadijah radiAllahu ta'ala Anha. Her status is well established amongst the people of the earth and amongst the inhabitants of the heavens.
此前我们谈及：默默无闻者或能蒙吉卜利勒亲临探访之殊荣。当然我们作为信士，深知盖德尔夜的珍贵——“众天使与灵魂在这一夜奉主命降临。”（97：4）吉卜利勒的降临本已非凡，但若是世人与诸天皆知她尊荣的女性呢？这正是赫蒂彻的写照。她的地位，不仅为大地众生所公认，更铭刻于诸天居民心间。
This is the woman that, aside from just being the first Muslim, the first believer in the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam, she's someone who is the comfort of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam. She finances the da'wah of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam. She sells everything that she has, right? Lived a very comfortable life prior to Islam. She gives that all up for the sake of Allah Subhanahu wa ta'ala.
这位女性，不仅是第一位穆斯林、首位相信先知ﷺ的信士，更是先知心灵的慰藉。她倾尽家财支持先知的宣教事业，变卖所有财产——须知她在归信前本过着极为优渥的生活——她为真主ﷻ奉献了一切。
And of course, you know, we have to note that the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam mentions that people of good qualities, their good qualities were only polished and further shined with Islam. So even before the da'wah of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam, she used to spend on the poor. She used to spend on the orphans, but she was still always comfortable and wealthy until the da'wah of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam starts. Now, the da'wah of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam goes on and the boycott happens. And Khadija radiAllahu anha technically could have excused herself from the boycott, right? She could have stayed back and had the protection of her tribe and her status would have guaranteed that she would be okay. But she chooses to be there with the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and with the Muslims literally as they are starving to death. And she uses her position to try to sneak food into the camp.
先知ﷺ曾言：品性高洁者，其美德必因伊斯兰的锤炼而愈发璀璨。即便在先知ﷺ宣教之前，赫蒂彻早已周济贫弱、抚养孤儿，却仍享富贵安逸。直至先知ﷺ开始传教，形势骤变。当古莱氏人对穆斯林实施经济封锁时，她本可置身事外——凭借家族地位受部落庇护，安然无恙。但她毅然选择与先知ﷺ及忍饥挨饿的穆斯林共患难，甚至利用自身身份暗中运送食物。
Hakim Nuhizam radiAllahu ta'ala anhu, who was the nephew of Khadija radiAllahu ta'ala anha would bring food to her and she would distribute that to the poor Muslims and she would continue to abstain from food herself. So she suffers voluntarily, but so graciously, right? Throughout the life of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam as a prophet. And she never wants complaints. And there are narrations in the seerah, you know, there's a narration from Imam Nusunni rahimullah that Jibreel radiAllahu ta'ala anhu once met her in the street in the form of a man to ask her about the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and she did nothing but say wonderful things about him and praise him and thank Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala for him. She didn't complain about her situation. So when she is tested in ways that we know and in ways that we don't know, because we'll never truly know the extent of that suffering and that hardship and that hurt, especially since much of the Meccan seerah is not known to us. Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala sees it all and Allah elevates her.
赫蒂彻的侄子哈基姆·努海扎姆（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）常为她送来食物，她却将之全部分发给贫困的穆斯林，自己甘愿挨饿。在先知ﷺ为圣的岁月里，她始终优雅地承受苦难，毫无怨言。圣传中记载：吉卜利勒曾化身男子在街巷遇见她，询问先知ﷺ境况。她只以美好言辞赞颂先知ﷺ，感赞真主ﷻ恩赐，对自身所处的困境无半句怨言。她经历了多少我们已知或未知的考验——麦加时期的艰辛大多已湮没于历史——真主ﷻ洞悉一切，并擢升她的品级。
Now she has a very unique incident with the angels and that is that Jibreel alayhi salam does not just come to her in any way, right? But he comes to her with specific salam and specific glad tidings. This is the famous hadith where Jibreel alayhi salam comes to the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and this is close to the end where Khadija radiAllahu anha has now suffered in every way and she's barely hanging on, right? It would, it would eventually be the hardships born through the boycott that would lead to her demise.
