So Jibreel alayhi salam comes to the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and he says Ya Rasulullah, this is Khadija. Oh messenger of Allah, Khadija is about to come to you. She's coming to you right now. Ma'aha ina'un feehi idamun aw ta'amun aw sharaab. And she has a platter. In that platter, there is something to eat or something to drink, some soup, something to eat, something to drink, something very simple. Fa'itha hiya atatka. When she comes to you, faqra alayha salam min rabbiha. First and foremost, tell her that Allah sends her salam. Her Lord sends her salam. Wa minni as salam. And give her salam from me, from Jibreel alayhi salam. So Allah says salam to her and please tell her that I say salam to her as well. Wa bashirha bi baytin fil jannah min qasab la sahaba feehi wala nasab. So this here now, he comes, he says, Ya Rasulullah, this is Khadija coming to you with this dish. It has some soup, some food, something to drink. When she reaches you, give her salam on behalf of her Lord and give her salam from me and give her the glad tidings of a palace in paradise. It's made of qasab, a very specific type of jewel. It's made of these jewels and there will be no noise and no stress in that palace in paradise. Now I'm going to break down some of the wisdom of this because I know this hadith is common, but some of the ways that the ulama extracted blessings and benefits from this very particular bushra, this very particular glad tidings is special to us.
赫蒂彻与天使的相遇尤为独特：吉卜利勒并非寻常造访，而是携特定祝安与喜讯而来。这段著名圣训记载：当赫蒂彻历经万难、生命垂危之际（封锁导致的折磨最终使她归真），吉卜利勒现身，对先知ﷺ说：“真主ﷻ的使者啊！赫蒂彻正携食盘前来。盘中盛着肉汤、食物与饮品。待她走近时，请代她的主ﷻ向她祝安，并转达我的祝安。更要向她报喜：她在乐园中将拥有一座特殊宝石筑就的宫殿，其中绝无喧嚣与烦忧。”现在我要解析这段圣训中蕴含的哲理。虽然这段圣训广为人知，但学者们从这则特殊的喜讯中提炼的吉庆与裨益，对我们而言尤为珍贵。
But first of all, look at the wisdom and the fiqh, the understanding of Khadija radhiAllahu anha and her natural guidance. She says, ask for Allah, إِنَّ اللهَ هُوَ السَّلَامُ Allah himself is the source of peace. So I can't say وَعَلَيْكَ السَّلَامُ to Allah. And that is of course, truly the way some of the sahaba would say السَّلَامُ عَلَى اللهِ and it was changed to, no, you know, التَّحِيَّاتُ لِلَّهِ, you greet Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala, but you can't say salam upon as-salam. He is السَّلَامُ, he's the source of peace. So she says, ask for Allah huwa as-salam. وَعَلَى جِبْرِيلَ السَّلَامُ and Ya Rasulullah tell Jibreel that I send salam.  وَعَلَيْكَ يَا رَسُولَ اللهِ السَّلَامُ وَرَحْمَةُ اللهِ وَبَرَكَاتُهُ And upon you, O messenger of Allah, is as-salam, is the peace and the mercy and the blessings of Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala. Now, this is of course, the beauty of Khadija radhiAllahu anha, her wisdom, her natural grace that even shows in this story.
首先，让我们领略赫蒂彻的智慧与教法洞见。当先知ﷺ转达真主ﷻ的祝安时，她回应道：“真主ﷻ本身就是和平的源泉，我不能以‘愿平安归于祢’来回应祂ﷻ的祝安。”这种精微认知体现了早期圣伴的虔诚——他们最初曾用“愿主ﷻ赐你平安”向真主ﷻ致意，后被启示更正为“一切赞颂全归真主ﷻ”，因为真主ﷻ自身即是终极和平，是平安的源头。  
她继而优雅地回应吉卜利勒的问候：“愿吉卜利勒平安，真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ啊，请转达我对他的祝安。”最后以最完美的问候转向先知ﷺ：“愿真主ﷻ的平安、慈悯与吉庆归于您。”这段对话如明珠般折射出赫蒂彻与生俱来的端庄品性：在最艰难的时刻，依然以法学家的睿智区分神圣位格的本质属性，以贵妇的优雅妥帖回应每一份问候，更以信士的谦卑将最高规格的祝福献给先知ﷺ。
But Imam Suhaili rahimAllah talks about the bushra, the glad tidings. The word qasab actually refers to the finish line in a race. Okay. So the way that they used to race back in the days of ignorance is that they used to put qasab, they used to put a particular prize in the front. And basically the race would be to that prize. And when you get to that prize, you get to take it home. So he says the wisdom of that is that Khadija radiAllahu anha was the first to get to the prize, meaning the prize of Islam, the first to embrace Islam. And so she's the first that gets to keep the reward, right? So she got the qasab of iman first, the qasab of faith first. And therefore she has a home in paradise that's unique. Some of the scholars also say Khadija radiAllahu anha's home before Islam was a distinguished house. It had a very particular decoration to it. So Jibreel alayhi salam is telling her in Jannah, you have this distinct home that no one else has.
但伊玛目苏海利（愿主ﷻ慈悯他）谈到了喜讯。قَصَب一词实际上指的是赛跑中的终点线。在蒙昧时代，他们比赛的方式是：人们会在前方放置一个قَصَب作为奖品，赛跑目标就是争夺这个奖品，最先抵达者即可获得它。他解释说这其中的智慧在于：赫蒂彻是第一个获得这个奖品的人，即伊斯兰信仰的殊荣——她是最先归信伊斯兰的人。因此她率先获得了伊玛尼（信仰）的قَصَب，也就率先获得了相应的回赐。因此她在天园拥有一处独特的居所。一些学者还提到：赫蒂彻在归信前的尘世居所本就与众不同，具有特殊的装饰风格。所以吉卜利勒告知她：在天园里，你将拥有这处独一无二的居所。
Suhaili also says a bait. She's being given the glad tidings of a home in particular because her bait, her home was the first home of Islam. The first house of Islam in this ummah was the house of Khadija radiAllahu anha. And then lastly, laa sakhaba feehi wala nasab. There will be no noise, no stress. Suhaili rahimahullah says that she never raised her voice at the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam or caused him stress. Rather, she was the source of stress relief when others gave the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam noise. And so, yeah, Khadija, you have a home in paradise. No more noise, no more exhaustion, no more stress. No more question, no more persecution. You'll never be tired again. SubhanAllah. And the scholars say, look at the blessing to Khadija radiAllahu anha here with this coming of Jibreel. Jibreel alayhi salam is coming just for this to Khadija, right? To interact just for this purpose, no other purpose. They say that this is the combination of the blessing of Maryam alayhi salam, who Allah sent Jibreel alayhi salam to, and the combination of Asiya alayhi salam, who was given the glad tidings of a home in paradise that Allah sends Jibreel alayhi salam specifically to her to give her salam and the glad tidings of a special palace in paradise.
苏海利还提到“居所”：她获赐特定天园居所的喜讯，正是因为她的尘世之家——赫蒂彻的居所——是伊斯兰的第一个家园。这个乌玛中最早的伊斯兰之家，便是赫蒂彻的居所。最后提到“既无喧嚣，亦无劳苦”：苏海利解释道，她从未对先知ﷺ高声说话或使他烦忧，相反，当他人带给先知纷扰时，她总是他舒心的港湾。因此，吉卜利勒说：“赫蒂彻啊！你在天园将拥有一个再无喧哗、再无疲惫、再无压力、再无质问、再无迫害的居所，永不再受劳苦。”学者们指出：看啊！吉卜利勒专为此事降临赫蒂彻，只为与她互动完成这项使命——这是何等殊荣！他们说，这是融合了麦尔彦（愿她平安）的恩典——真主ﷻ曾派遣吉卜利勒与她沟通，以及阿西娅（愿她平安）的恩典——真主ﷻ曾命吉卜利勒向她传达平安并预告天园中特别的宫殿。
EP010 来自至尊的婚姻安排
A Marriage Proposal From Above

Jibreel (as) approached the Prophet ﷺ in a dream, showing him a green silk cloth with an image of Aishah (ra). Jibreel (as) tells the Prophet ﷺ that she will be his wife in this dunya and the hereafter due to this marriage proposal from above. Aishah (ra) goes on to be the joy of the Prophet’s ﷺ life and gives us more narrations of the angels in the Prophet’s ﷺ presence than any of the Companions.
吉卜利勒（愿他平安）在梦中向先知ﷺ显现，手持一块绿色丝绸布料，上面绘有阿伊莎（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）的画像。吉卜利勒告知先知ﷺ：“因这来自至尊的婚姻安排，她将成为你在今世与后世的妻子。”阿伊莎后来成为先知ﷺ生命的喜悦，她所传述的关于天使在先知ﷺ身旁的圣训，比其他圣伴们（愿主ﷻ喜悦他们）都更为丰富。
So Jibreel alayhis salam has already come to give salam specifically to the woman that the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam loved most, the best woman of this ummah Khadija radiAllahu anha and give her the glad tidings of her home in Paradise. Now after she passes away, we know that this was a period of great sadness for the Messenger sallallahu alayhi wasallam and Rasulullah sallallahu alayhi wasallam did not get married for some time and eventually Khawla radiAllahu anha comes to the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam and suggest to him to get married to two women.
此前，吉卜利勒曾专程向先知ﷺ最挚爱的女子、这个乌玛最卓越的女性——赫蒂彻（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）传达平安，并预告她天园的居所。在她归真后，这段时期是使者ﷺ极度悲伤的岁月。先知ﷺ许久未再婚娶，直到郝拉（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）前来建议他迎娶两位女性。
So she brings up Saudah radiAllahu anha who's old and who can be motherly to his children and she's also particularly distinguished with her laughter and also A'ishah radiAllahu anha who she introduces as the daughter of the most beloved person to you. Okay, so it was Khawla radiAllahu anha who first came to the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam and suggested A'ishah radiAllahu anha. However, it would be some time before the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam would marry A'ishah radiAllahu anha because it would be until she would reach her age.
因此，郝拉向先知ﷺ推荐了年长、能如母亲般照料他子女的索黛（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）——她以爽朗的笑声著称；以及阿伊莎，称其为“你最挚爱之人的女儿”。正是郝拉最先向先知ﷺ提议迎娶阿伊莎。但因需待其成年，先知ﷺ与阿伊莎的婚姻延后了数年。
However, there is another dream that the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam says and in that dream the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam sees Jibreel alayhis salam once again. And Jibreel alayhis salam approaches the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam in this dream and he shows the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam a green cloth made of silk and on that green cloth made of silk is an image of A'ishah radiAllahu anha and Jibreel alayhis salam says This here is going to be your spouse in this dunya and in the hereafter. So subhanAllah Jibreel alayhis salam who just gave Khadija radiAllahu anha the glad tidings of what she's going to is now telling the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam what comes next for him and that would be A'ishah radiAllahu anha who would spend so much time with the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam and of course would be his joy and would document the life of the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam in such detail for us and this would be the woman that would be around the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam in his moments of privacy in his moments of worship in so many different moments where he's interacting with Jibreel alayhis salam.
此后，先知ﷺ又见证一个梦境：吉卜利勒再度显现，手持绿色丝绸布料，其上绘有阿伊莎的画像，并宣告：“她将成为你在今世与后世的伴侣。”赞主ﷻ超绝！此前刚为赫蒂彻传达天园喜讯的吉卜利勒，此刻又在为先知ﷺ预告未来的伴侣——那个将长久陪伴先知ﷺ、成为他生命喜悦、详尽记录先知生活细节的女子。她将见证先知ﷺ在礼拜等私密时刻与吉卜利勒互动的神圣场景。
So you just think about the amount of times she would have interacted with the Angels in the presence of the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam during his qiyam which she narrates more than anyone else and there's a specific narration where Abu Salam ibn Abdur Rahman. He said I asked A'ishah radiAllahu anha how would the Prophet sallallahu alayhi wasallam start his prayer. So she said when he used to get up and he would pray at night. He would start his prayer with the words Allahumma rabba Jibreel wa Mika'il wa Israfil Fatir al-samawati wal-ard, alim al-ghaybi wa shahada, anta tahkumu bayna ibadika fee maa kano feehi yaqhtarifoon, ihdini li maa qhturifa feehi minal haqq, innaka tahdi man ta shaa ila siratil mustaqeem So O Allah the Lord of Jibreel and Mika'il and Israfil creator of the heavens and the earth, knower of the unseen and the seen you are the one who judges between your slaves concerning that which they differ over. O Allah guide me to the disputed matters of truth or through those differences to the truth for you are the one who guides to the straight path. [Sunan Abi Dawud #767]
试想她在先知ﷺ夜间礼拜时与天使的互动次数——她对此的传述远超他人。如艾布·赛莱迈·本·阿卜杜·拉赫曼所述：“我曾问阿伊莎：‘先知ﷺ如何开始夜间拜？’她答：‘当他起身礼拜时，会先念诵：主ﷻ啊！吉卜利勒、米卡伊勒、伊斯拉菲勒的主，天地万物的创造者，彻知幽玄与显迹的主ﷻ啊！你是裁决仆人分歧的主ﷻ。主ﷻ啊！求你在众说纷纭的真理中引导我，唯你能指引通往正道者。’”（《艾布·达乌德圣训集》#767）
So A'ishah radiAllahu anha is narrating the way that the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasallam is calling upon Allah and invoking Allah first and foremost before the Lord of the heavens and the earth the Lord of the angels and naming these angels in particular. Now did Jibreel alayhi salam ever himself interact with A'ishah radiAllahu anha? A'ishah radiAllahu ta'ala anha She says that the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasallam once said to me Ya A'isha, O A'isha, hatha Jibreel yaqra'u alayka salam Jibreel alayhi salam is here and he sends you salam. So Jibreel alayhi salam specifically names A'isha radiAllahu anha to send salam to her and A'isha radiAllahu ta'ala anha responds and she says wa alayhi salam wa rahmatullahi wa barakatuh and upon him is salam and Allah's mercy and his blessings. And she says Ya RasoolAllah tara ma la ara O Messenger of Allah, you see things that I don't see SubhanAllah, you are seeing this and I don't see it.
阿伊莎在此传述了先知ﷺ如何首先向主ﷻ——天地的主宰ﷻ、众天使的主ﷻ——祈祷，并特别提及这些天使的名字。  
吉卜利勒是否曾亲自与阿伊莎互动？阿伊莎说：“先知曾对我说：‘阿伊莎啊！吉卜利勒在此向你问安。’”吉卜利勒特意指名向阿伊莎传达平安，阿伊莎回应道：“愿主ﷻ赐他平安、慈悯与吉庆。”她接着说：“真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ啊！您能看见我看不见的景象——赞主ﷻ超绝！您目睹此景而我却未能得见。”
This was Jibreel alayhi salam coming in his angelic form. There are also times that A'isha radiAllahu anha would see Jibreel alayhi salam in human form, which we will talk about from a distance, however. So this is the woman who Jibreel alayhi salam would come to the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasallam most in her presence. And this is something the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasallam mentioned as one of the fadhal, one of the virtues of A'isha radiAllahu anha is that there is no person who Jibreel alayhi salam came to him in her presence, in any human beings presence like A'isha radiAllahu anha and of course, even to the very moment of his death when the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasallam's body and soul was perched against the chest of A'isha radiAllahu anha in his last moments. While the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasallam was having that conversation with Jibreel alayhi salam, where he was given the option to stay amongst his followers on earth or to join the highest companion. It would be in the hands, in the arms of A'isha radiAllahu ta'ala anha with the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasallam responding and his soul leaving his body.
这是吉卜利勒以天使形态显现的场合。阿伊莎也曾见过吉卜利勒以人类形象出现，此事我们稍后再述。  
吉卜利勒最常在阿伊莎面前降临先知ﷺ身旁，先知ﷺ曾将此列为阿伊莎的殊荣之一：“吉卜利勒从未在其他人面前如在她面前这般频繁地降临于我。”甚至先知ﷺ归真之际——当他的身体与灵魂依偎在阿伊莎怀中时，吉卜利勒仍与之对话，给予他选择：留驻世间与追随者同在，或归向至高同伴。最终，先知ﷺ的灵魂在阿伊莎臂弯中回应召唤，离开了尘世之躯。
So A'isha radiAllahu anha has experienced a mighty journey of interactions with Jibreel alayhi salam and she used to mention this as well from her fadhal, from her virtues that Jibreel alayhi salam was the one who proposed on her behalf that Allah sent Jibreel alayhi salam with the declaration of her innocence that Jibreel alayhi salam used to come to her with salam, that Jibreel alayhi salam used to come to the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasallam with revelation frequently while she was in his presence.
阿伊莎经历了与吉卜利勒互动的神圣的历程。她常提及这些作为她的殊荣——吉卜利勒奉真主ﷻ之命代表她的请求，即真主ﷻ派遣吉卜利勒降示经文昭雪她的清白；吉卜利勒常向她传达平安；吉卜利勒频繁在先知ﷺ面前——尤其当她陪伴先知ﷺ时——降示启示。
However, she also notes that there's one person that surpassed her in this regard. And so in one of the narrations where she says, Wallahi, I was never jealous of any woman the way I was of Khadija. [Sahih al-Bukhari #3817] And she starts to mention that the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasallam used to mention Khadija radiAllahu anha so much and the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasallam would not sacrifice except that he sent something to her friends. And then she mentions at the end of this narration and Allah sent Jibreel alayhi salam to give her the good news of having a palace of Qasr bin Paradise. [Sahih al-Bukhari #3817] So even though Aisha radiAllahu anha encountered Jibreel alayhi salam and had the experience of having salam delivered to her from Jibreel alayhi salam, she did not have what Khadija radiAllahu anha had of the Bushra, the glide tiding of that particular palace of Qasr in Paradise.
阿伊莎也指出，有一人在这方面超越了她。在一则圣训中，她坦言：“以真主ﷻ发誓，我从未像嫉妒赫蒂彻那样嫉妒过任何女子。”（《布哈里圣训集》#3817）她解释道：先知ﷺ时常提及赫蒂彻，每当他宰牲时总会馈赠她的挚友们。这段圣训结尾提到：“真主派遣吉卜利勒向她预告天园中一座宫殿的喜讯。”（《布哈里圣训集》#3817）  
因此，尽管阿伊莎曾与吉卜利勒互动并获其致安，她却未曾拥有赫蒂彻所获的殊荣——天园特定宫殿的喜讯。
EP011 被祈祷废止的离婚
A Divorce Nullified by Prayer

When the Prophet ﷺ reached a point where he wanted to divorce Hafsa (ra), it was ultimately the convincing of an angel that kept them together. Jibreel (as) did not invoke her family name or reputation, but her dedication to prayer as a reason why she would remain his wife in paradise.
当先知ﷺ决意要与哈芙赛（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）离婚时，最终因一位天使的劝说而维系了他们的婚姻。吉卜利勒天使（愿他平安）并非以她的家世或名誉为由，而是以她坚持礼拜的功修作为依据，证明她将在乐园中继续作为先知ﷺ的妻子。
So there is one more wife of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam who has this unique interaction with the angels, specifically Jibreel Alayhi Salam, but it has a serious twist to it. It's not the same as Khadijah radiAllahu Anha or as A'ishah radiAllahu Anha. And that is Hafsa bint Umar. May Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la be pleased with her and her father.
因此，先知ﷺ的妻室中又出现了一位与天使——吉卜利勒存在特别互动的女子，但这次事件具有重大转折。这与赫蒂彻（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）或阿伊莎（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）的情况都不同，这位女子正是欧麦尔之女哈芙赛。愿真主ﷻ喜悦她与她的父亲。
Hafsa radiAllahu Anha has a very interesting story and interaction with Jibreel Alayhi Salam. But before I get there, I actually want to talk about her brother first. And this will give us an understanding of what is going to take place with her.
哈芙赛·宾特·欧麦尔与吉卜利勒天使之间有着耐人寻味的互动故事。但在讲述这段之前，我们需要先了解她的弟弟阿卜杜拉·本·欧麦尔的经历，这将为我们理解后续事件提供背景。
Abdullah bin Umar radiAllahu Anhuma was of course a great follower of the sunnah of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and was almost identical to Umar bin Khattab radiAllahu Anhu. And he was very young when he was around the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. And he said that he wanted to have a dream that he could share with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam Why? Because when the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam would ask people in the mornings to share their good dreams, that was a way of connecting to the Messenger SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. So he asked Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la to give him a dream so that he could share it with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam the next day. Instead, he goes to sleep and he says, I saw this dream of two angels that came to me and they took me to the edge of hellfire. And Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la has a terrifying dream because he says, I saw people in there that I recognized. So imagine he's looking at hellfire for enough of a time that he sees people that he knows in the hellfire. And it obviously scares him in the dream. And just as he is looking at it, another angel comes and says to him, do not be afraid and commands that the angels take him instead to the right because this is not your place.
阿卜杜拉·本·欧麦尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）是先知ﷺ圣行的忠实追随者，其性格几乎与父亲欧麦尔·本·哈塔卜（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）如出一辙。他在先知ﷺ身边度过少年时期，曾渴望通过梦境与先知ﷺ建立联系。因为每当清晨，先知ﷺ询问众人梦境时，这正是与使者建立精神连接的契机。为此他祈求真主ﷻ赐予他值得分享的美梦。然而那夜他经历的却是骇人景象：两位天使带他来到火狱边缘，目睹了熟识之人在其中受刑。正当惊惧之时，第三位天使出现安抚他，并指引天使们带他转向右侧——这预示着天堂才是他的归宿。
So the dream is scary in that, he saw hellfire and he saw people that he knows in hellfire and he was in front of it for that time. But it's also a good dream in the sense that he was spared from it. So it's an interesting dream, but it's not the type of dream that he had in mind to share with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam after fajr the way that other companions would share their dreams. So instead he goes to Hafsah radiAllahu anha, his sister, who's also the wife of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and says to Hafsah radiAllahu anha, can you speak to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam about this dream? Hafsah approaches the messenger SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam shares the dream. Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam in his wisdom, knowing that whatever he says is gonna get back to Abdullah, he starts off by saying, ni'ma rajulu Abdullah. What a good young man Abdullah is. What an excellent young man he is. Which is a statement of praise, right? So that he's put at ease, that he's a good young man. But then he says, law kana yusalli minal layl if only he pray a little bit more at night. So Abdullah is an excellent young man, but Abdullah should also pray some more at night. He needs to pray qiyamul layl. Now you can imagine what the nights of Abdullah ibn Umar radiAllahu ta'ala anhuma looked like after he heard that from the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam as a very young man. So his life is going to be distinguished by his qiyamul layl as it is in many other ways.
这个梦境具有双重性：既展现了火狱以及阿卜杜拉熟识之人在其中受刑的恐怖，又昭示了他被拯救的恩典。然而这并非阿卜杜拉期待与先知ﷺ分享的美好梦境。于是他通过姐姐哈芙赛（她也是先知之妻）向使者ﷺ转述。先知ﷺ知道他的话语最终会转达给阿卜杜拉，他ﷺ便以智慧回应，首先称赞“阿卜杜拉真是为优秀的青年”，继而指出：“倘若他能增加夜间拜功就更好了。”这句话从此塑造了阿卜杜拉的人生轨迹，使其以坚持夜间拜著称。
Now, what does this have to do with Hafsa radiAllahu anha? She's obviously the middle woman in this regard. She's the messenger of the Messenger SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam to her brother to say, look, if you don't want the angels to take you to that place in the hereafter, then you should increase in your qiyamul layl. And that will assure you that the angels will instead take you by the permission of Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la to the other destination of al-jannah. So she was the messenger between the Messenger SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and her brother.
这与哈芙赛有何关联？显然她在此事中担任了中间人的角色。作为先知ﷺ与弟弟之间的信使，她传达着这样的启示：“若你不愿天使在后世将你带往火狱，就当增加夜间拜功。这将确保天使奉真主ﷻ之命引领你前往乐园。”由此，她成为连接使者ﷺ与她弟弟之间的精神桥梁。
Now, what makes this so different? Hafsa radiAllahu ta'ala anha and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam actually separated. Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam actually reached a point of divorce with Hafsa radiAllahu ta'ala anha. He was not cruel with her. He did not abuse her. He did not harm her, but the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam had reached the point where he was going to separate from her. And when the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam reaches that point, how is this in praise of Hafsa radiAllahu anha? Of course, we know Umar radiAllahu anhu came and, you know, asked the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam to reconsider and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam was gracious with Umar radiAllahu anhu. But the one who came to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam to actually convince him was not Umar radiAllahu anhu. It was Jibreel alaihi salam. And Jibreel alaihi salam comes to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and says, Ya Muhammad SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam Raja Hafsa, take her back. Now, is he going to say, take her back because she is the daughter of your companion or take her back because of this, that, take her back because of what people will say? No, he says, Ya Muhammad SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam Raja Hafsa, take her back. Fa innaha sawwama qawwama Because she is a woman who fasts a lot and who prays a lot of qiyam. Wahiya zawjatuka fil jannah And I have seen she is your wife in paradise as well. [Mustadrak al-Hikam ]
这如何体现哈芙赛的特别之处？事实上，先知ﷺ曾与她经历情感危机，一度决定离婚。须知先知ﷺ从未苛待或伤害她，只是双方关系达到了需暂时分离的境地。当此之时，欧麦尔虽曾恳请先知ﷺ三思，先知ﷺ也向欧麦尔表示感激。但真正促成和解的关键调解者并非凡人，而是吉卜利勒天使，他亲临并对先知ﷺ说：“穆罕默德ﷺ啊，请收回与哈芙赛的离婚决定。”天使的劝诫不涉世俗考量，既非因其父辈情谊，亦非顾念世人眼光，而是基于一个神圣理由：“因她是常守斋戒、勤修夜功之人，且我在天园中确见她仍为你的伴侣。”（《两大部圣训补遗》）
SubhanAllah, Hafsa radiAllahu anha has the testimony of Jibreel alaihi salam that you should take this woman back, not because of who she is related to, not because of anything else, but she is sawwama qawwama. She has distinguished herself in the sight of Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la and by the testimony of the angels, by the amount of fasting that she does and by the amount of qiyam that she does. SubhanAllah, now connect that back to Abdullah ibn Umar, right? Who Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la in his wisdom and planning made her the one that would hear the dream of Abdullah and then deliver the message of the messenger SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam to him that you should pray more qiyam ul-layl and the angels won't take you to that destination. And now you have the greatest of the angels coming to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam to testify on her behalf that by the way, this is a woman who is distinguished by her qiyam and her siyam.
赞主ﷻ超绝！哈芙赛竟获得吉卜利勒天使的见证——先知ﷺ应当收回离婚决定，并非因她家世显赫，或任何其他理由，而是基于她“常守斋戒、勤修夜功”的卓越功修。她在真主ﷻ阙前以斋功夜拜显扬，更获天使亲证其虔敬。深思其妙：真主ﷻ的智慧安排使哈芙赛成为连接神圣启示的纽带——既作为传达者将先知“当增夜功以避火狱”的教诲带给胞弟阿卜杜拉·本·欧麦尔，又作为受启者获天使长亲自说情。最尊贵的天使亲临见证：此女子正以夜拜与斋戒在众仆中卓然而立。
And if you think about what comes after that, I want you to think about the blessings of that. When we go to say salam to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, of course the salam reaches him wherever you give it, but when you do ziyarah, when you're in the masjid of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, specifically from the man's side, and you stand in front of the grave of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and Abu Bakr and Umar radiAllahu anhuma, that place that you are standing is actually the house of Hafsa radiAllahu anha. That is the exact location of where Hafsa radiAllahu anha had her home. That is the home that Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la blessed and distinguished by having the Quran gathered in that home. That's not by accident. The mushaf, the papers, the bones, all of the scrolls were gathered in the house of Hafsa radiAllahu anha, and that was the home that by the testimony of Jibreel alayhi salam, a righteous woman used to fast daily and pray nightly. So it shows you that these ranks are not achieved with Allah or with the angels randomly, or because of who you're related to or who you know. These ranks are achieved by the deeds that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam taught us to elevate ourselves until the end of times.
细思其中吉庆：当我们向先知ﷺ致祝安词时，祝安确能达至圣ﷺ听。但若亲临麦地那圣寺进行瞻礼，伫立于先知ﷺ与两位哈里发艾布·伯克尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）、欧麦尔陵寝前时——此处正是哈芙赛的故居所在。真主ﷻ特选此地彰显吉庆：首部古兰经标准本（穆斯哈夫）正是在此汇集编纂，经文骨板与羊皮卷轴皆珍藏于哈芙赛的居所。这绝非偶然，恰是吉卜利勒天使见证的虔敬女子之居所——她昼夜以斋功夜拜滋养灵性。这昭示：在真主ﷻ与天使面前的品级，绝非偶然或倚仗门第，唯凭先知ﷺ教导的功修方能擢升，此理亘古长存。
EP012 天使吉卜利勒（愿他平安）的人间化身
The Man Who Resembled Jibreel (as)

Jibreel (as) was known to take the form of the most beautiful human from a group of people. Amongst the Companions of the Prophet ﷺ, Jibreel (as) chose the form of Dihyah al-Kalbi (ra). What’s the story of the companion Dihyah (ra) aside from his beauty?
吉卜利勒天使（愿他平安）常以人群中最俊美的形象示人。在先知ﷺ的圣伴中，他选择了迪赫亚·卡尔比（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）的容貌。这位以俊美著称的圣门弟子背后有何非凡故事？
Imagine being the man that Jibreel Alayhi Salaam takes the form of when he comes to your people. Imagine being married to the man that Jibreel Alayhi Salaam takes the form of when he comes to a people. Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu is a very interesting companion of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam because he's practically only known for this. Now, no one ever made the accusation that Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was just playing the role of Jibreel Alayhi Salaam, not even the enemies of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam. Because when Jibreel Alayhi Salaam would come, he would take a form that resembled Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu deeply. But if you looked closely, it wasn't exactly him.
试想：你不仅是被吉卜利勒选为化身的男子，你的妻子更是与这位天使化身者共度余生之人。迪赫亚是一位十分有趣的圣伴，他仅以与天使相似而闻名。没人曾指控他假扮天使——纵使是先知ﷺ的敌人们，也从未妄言。每当降临尘世，天使会幻化出与迪赫亚极为相似却更臻完美的容颜，但吉卜利勒虽取其形却非其神。
Imam Zarkashi rahimAllah ta'ala, he explains this. He says that Jibreel Alayhi Salaam when he came, he would take the most beautiful form. And so when he came to the Arabs, he took the form, in human form, of the most beautiful amongst the men of the Arabs. And when Jibreel Alayhi Salaam went to Maryam Alayhi Salaam, in the form of a human being first, he went in the form of a very handsome man from Bani Israel. And anytime he went to a prophet or went to a people in the form of a human being, he took the form of who was noted to be the most handsome amongst them. So this happens to be amongst the Arabs, this man by the name of Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. And there are several encounters where, you know, you looked at the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam talking to Jibreel Alayhi Salaam, and if you were blessed to be able to see the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam speaking with Jibreel Alayhi Salaam, when he came to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam in human form, you would have thought it was Dihya.
据伊玛目·赞卡希（愿主ﷻ慈悯之）阐释：吉卜利勒在向不同民族显现时，总会选取该族公认的至美男子形象。如向麦尔彦（愿她平安）显圣时，他以以色列美男子的形象降临；面对阿拉伯人时，则化身为该族公认的俊杰——迪赫亚。历史记载中，当吉卜利勒以人身与先知ﷺ交谈时，有幸目睹此景者皆恍然以为迪赫亚亲临。
And Umm Salama radiAllahu ta'ala Anha, she actually narrates that one time, you know, in her living room, suddenly she sees the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is sitting with who she thinks is Dihya radiAllahu Anhu. So she looks and then she immediately sort of walks away and distances herself from the sitting of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and who she thinks is Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. And then the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam goes to her and says, man hadha, who do you think this is? And she said, Dihya. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, he doesn't say anything. And Umm Salama radiAllahu Anha says, by Allah, I thought it was Dihya until the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam stood up on the minbar and he said, atani Jibreel, Jibreel alaihi salam came to me. [Sahih al-Bukhari #3634] And he said this, this, this and that.
乌姆·赛莱麦（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）曾见证：某日在其居所，忽见先知ﷺ正与"迪赫亚"交谈。她见状即悄然退避。事后先知询问道：“你以为那是何人？”她答：“迪赫亚。”先知未作解释。直至某日先知ﷺ登上宣讲台宣告：“吉卜利勒曾降临于我...”（《布哈里圣训集》#3634）乌姆·赛莱麦方知真相：“以真主ﷻ为誓，我曾坚信那是迪赫亚！”
Aisha radiAllahu Anha has a similar story. She says that one time, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam heard a man's voice outside and he got up quickly and he went outside to meet that man. So she said, I followed him out to see who he was talking to. And I saw a man from the distance and he was leaning against a very fine horse. And as far as I could see, it looked like Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu wearing his turban and the ends of the turban were behind his back. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam came back to the home and I said, Ya Rasulullah, I saw you get up quickly with a sense of urgency to respond to that voice and go speak to that man. But when I looked in the distance, it looked like Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, Ya Aisha, did you see him? So she said, yes, I saw him. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, that was Jibreel alaihi salam.
阿伊莎亦述：某夜先知ﷺ闻声疾步而出，她跟上去，见远处有男子倚骏马而立，缠头巾垂于背后，形貌酷似迪赫亚。归家后她询问道：“使者ﷺ啊，我看见你为回复一人匆忙而起，远远望去，那人看似迪赫亚？”先知ﷺ反问：“阿伊莎，你见到他了？”确认后，先知ﷺ揭示真相：“那正是吉卜利勒。”
Now Aisha radiAllahu Anha mentioned that when she saw him, he was wearing clothes that he'd never worn before. So he was wearing a white turban. He had a white burd, a white scarf. So white garment. He had white sandals. And she said, I've never seen Dihya radiAllahu Anhu dressed in that way. So who is this man, Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu? What's his story beyond Jibreel alaihi salam looking like him when he came down in human form?
阿伊莎曾提到，当她见到他时，他穿着从未穿过的装束——头戴白色缠巾，身披白色长巾，脚穿白色凉鞋，一身洁白。她说：“我从未见过迪赫亚如此打扮。”那么，这位迪赫亚究竟是谁？除了吉卜利勒天使曾以他的形象降临人世外，他还有怎样的故事？
First and foremost, they say about Dihya kana yudhrabu bihi al-mathab. That people used to write poetry and he would be the example, the standard of beauty amongst the Arabs. So when they would talk about someone and measure someone's handsomeness, they would measure them against Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. And Ibn Abbas radiAllahu Anhuma, he says that Dihya was from Medina. So Yathrib prior to Medina, obviously. And when he used to return home or when Dihya radiAllahu Anhu was out, there wasn't a single woman except that they would go out to see what Dihya was doing. And they would stare at him as he was walking through the street. So it was hard to lower your gaze from a person of that level of beauty, that level of handsomeness.
首先，据传述，人们常以迪赫亚为典范创作诗歌，他是阿拉伯人眼中俊美的标准。当人们谈论某人或衡量其英俊程度时，总会以迪赫亚作为参照。伊本·阿巴斯（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）说，迪赫亚来自麦地那（即之前的叶斯里布）。每当他外出或归家时，城中所有女子都会出门围观，目不转睛地望着他走过街道——他的俊美令人难以移开视线。
So who is he? Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was from Medina as we said. And he would become Muslim sometime after Badr. And he would witness every battle with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam after Badr. And the role that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam chose for him is significant. And it shows you the wisdom of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam chose Dihya radiAllahu Anhu to be an ambassador to emperors around the world. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam sends him to world leaders with handwritten letters, calling them to Islam. And so the very famous story of Caesar, Heraclius, in Rome, receiving the message of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was the one who went out to Asham, to greater Syria, and hand delivered the message of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam to Heraclius. And this was the wisdom of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam because when Dihya showed up to a place, it attracted attention. And Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu used that attention, once again, to call people to Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la, as he did, as the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam commanded him.
他的生平如何？如前所述，迪赫亚来自麦地那，在白德尔战役后归信伊斯兰，此后参与了先知ﷺ指挥的每一场战役。先知ﷺ为他安排的职责极具深意：任命他担任面向各国君主的使节。先知ﷺ派遣他带着亲笔书信前往世界各地，向统治者传达伊斯兰的召唤。其中最著名的便是罗马皇帝希拉克略收到先知书信的故事——正是迪赫亚亲赴大叙利亚地区，将先知ﷺ的书信呈递希拉克略。这体现了先知ﷺ的智慧：迪赫亚的出现总能引发关注，而他正是利用这种关注力，如先知ﷺ所命，将人们引向真主ﷻ。
However, he also was a warrior. And we find Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was someone that fought in numerous battles and fought in the famous battle of Yarmouk. So he wasn't just someone that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam kept there just to go send here or there. He was actually someone that was taking part in a heavy way in some of the most notable battles in Islamic history.
然而，他还是一位战士。迪赫亚参与过多次重要战役，包括著名的雅穆克之战。先知ﷺ并非仅派遣他到某地去担任外交使命，而是使他深度参与了伊斯兰历史上多次最重大的战役。
Now I said, imagine being the woman that's married to that man. And this is probably my favorite part of the story of Dihya, by the way, is who his wife was. Now, before I say who his wife was, a lot of times people, I think, fail to grasp the significance of Ikramah ibn Abi Jahl, of all people becoming Muslim and becoming a great warrior and eventually becoming a Shaheed, eventually becoming a martyr. And the way the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam treated Ikramah radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu and said, you know, don't abuse him in regards to his father. Don't mention his father in a bad way. Even though his father was the Fir'aun of this Ummah, don't hurt him with Abu Jahl, in his presence at least. Right? We know who Abu Jahl is, but the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam took his feelings into consideration. But even Ikramah became Muslim much, much, much later.
我曾说：想象一下成为这个男人的妻子是怎样的体验——而这正是迪赫亚故事中最打动我的部分。在揭示他妻子的身份前，我们需要先理解一个现象：像伊克里马·本·阿比·贾赫勒这样的人物最终归信伊斯兰、成为英勇战士直至殉道，其意义常被世人低估。先知ﷺ曾特别嘱咐众人：“不要因他父亲而羞辱他，即使他的父亲是这个时代的法老，至少在他面前不要提及艾布·贾赫勒的恶行。”须知艾布·贾赫勒虽是这稳麦的法老，但先知ﷺ仍顾及了伊克里马的情感——而伊克里马的归信已是非常后期的事。
Now, who is the wife of Dihya? It's actually the daughter of Abu Lahab. And she is from the first to embrace Islam. Her name is Durrah bint Abi Lahab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. I mean, imagine her reciting the Quran and she recites, tabbat yada Abi Lahab bin Wattab. She recites the punishment of her father and her mother. The only person who has a whole Surah revealed about their punishment and fire. She is the one reciting that. She became Muslim early on with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. And of course, she's the first cousin of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. And she was married already to a man by the name of Al-Harith ibn Naufal, who not only was a disbeliever, but who died fighting against the Muslims in Badr.
那么迪赫亚的妻子是谁？她正是艾布·莱海布的女儿——杜拉·宾特·阿比·莱海布（愿主ﷻ喜悦她），最早归信伊斯兰的先锋之一。试想她诵读古兰时念到“愿艾布·莱海布双手受伤！”（111：1）的场景：她亲口诵读着关于父母将受火刑的经文，而她的父亲是整部古兰中唯一被专门用一章宣告永久火狱惩罚的人。她早年就随先知ﷺ归信，身为先知的堂姐，最初却嫁给了哈里斯·本·脑法勒——这个不仅顽固不信教，更在白德尔战役中与穆斯林作战阵亡的男人。
Okay. So Durrah bint Abi Lahab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. I mean, you have to be a righteous woman to overcome all of those psychological hurdles, right? And spiritual hurdles. She overcame those hurdles. Her husband remained a disbeliever. He fought against the Muslims in Badr. She had children from him. And she made hijrah with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. So she actually was part of the extended family of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam that was protected, but was in a very tricky situation. Now, when Al-Harith died fighting against the Muslims, that kind of seals all hope for her, right? She has kids and she's the daughter of Abu Lahab. And yes, in Medina, she had a hard time. There is an incident that's documented where some people mocked her for her father. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam became very upset.
杜拉·宾特·阿比·莱海布需要何等坚贞的信仰才能跨越这些心理与精神的障碍？丈夫至死未信且在白德尔战役中与穆斯林为敌，她独自抚养子女，却仍坚持随先知ﷺ迁徙。作为受先知ﷺ庇护的亲属，她的处境可谓如履薄冰。当哈里斯战死的消息传来，似乎断绝了她所有希望——一个带着孩子的“艾布·莱海布之女”在麦地那的艰难可想而知。史料记载有人曾因她父亲嘲讽她，引得先知ﷺ震怒。
Look what Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la gave to her. After the battle of Badr, when her husband passed away fighting against the Muslims, Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la made it so that Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu would marry Durra bint Abi Lahab. The great wisdom of this, by the way, as well, you know, remember Um Jameel, the wife of Abu Lahab said to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam that I think your devil has forsaken you, which is prior to Surat Al-Duha. Instead, SubhanAllah, we find that her daughter became one of the people of Iman and was married to a man who was so handsome. And of course, righteous that Jibreel Alayhi Salam would take his form.
但真主ﷻ的恩赐何等奇妙：白德尔战役后，当她的丈夫因与穆斯林作战而阵亡时，真主ﷻ竟让迪赫亚迎娶了杜拉。这其中蕴含的深意令人惊叹——还记得艾布·莱海布之妻乌姆·贾米勒曾对先知ﷺ说，“我觉得你的魔鬼抛弃你了。”这是在《上午》章降示前。而如今，她的女儿却成为信士，更嫁给了俊美无双的义士——甚至吉卜利勒天使都以他的形象显现。
EP013 吉卜利勒圣训
The Hadith of Jibreel (as)

The most important and famous hadith in Islam is the Hadith of Jibreel (as), in which Jibreel (as) comes to the Prophet ﷺ to ask him questions about Islam, Iman, the Hour, and its Signs. What can we learn from this hadith and Jibreel’s (as) line of questioning?
伊斯兰中最重要且著名的圣训当属：吉卜利勒圣训，其中吉卜利勒天使（愿他平安）前来向先知ﷺ询问关于伊斯兰、信仰、末日及其征兆的问题。我们能从这段圣训及吉卜利勒的提问方式中学到什么？
There are two major incidents in the Sira where a large number of companions encountered either the angel being Jibril A.S. or the angels as a whole. The angels as a whole is the incident of Badr, of course, and we'll talk about the battle of Badr. But the most famous hadith in Islam and the most important hadith in Islam is probably Hadith Jibril. According to many of the scholars, this hadith, which is known as the Hadith of Jibril A.S.
在先知ﷺ传记中，有两次重大事件使得多位圣伴遇见吉卜利勒天使或众天使。众天使降临的事件自然是白德尔战役——我们后续会详述。但伊斯兰中最著名且最重要的圣训，或许当属：吉卜利勒圣训。许多学者认为，这个圣训被称为“吉卜利勒圣训”。
This hadith has been narrated by Umar bin Al-Khattab radiAllahu ta'ala anhu, but there are numerous other narrations that give us the full context. So I want you to imagine that you're in the masjid of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and according to the narrations of Abu Dharr radiAllahu ta'ala anhu as well as Abu Huraira radiAllahu anhu, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, when he would sit with his companions, we wouldn't be able to distinguish the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam from his companions in regards to where he was sitting. Meaning that he would sit at the same level as them. It's not that he would sit on a chair and they would all sit around him. And so when people would come from outside, sometimes they wouldn't be able to know which one of the companions or who amongst the people was the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam.
这段圣训由欧麦尔·本·哈塔卜（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）传述，但其他众多传述为我们提供了完整背景。想象你正身处先知ﷺ的清真寺中，根据艾布·赞尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）和艾布·胡莱赖（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）的传述：当先知ﷺ与圣伴们同坐时，我们无法通过座位位置来区分先知ﷺ与弟子们。这意味着他ﷺ与众人平起平坐，并非高坐椅上让众人环绕。因此当外来者进入时，有时竟无法辨认人群中哪位是先知ﷺ。
So we said to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, let's, you know, make something for you so that if people come, at least if a stranger comes, then they know who you are. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, while he refused anything that would resemble like a throne or something that was too pompous, they said to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, how about we make a small bench for you by just putting the dirt together? And it's like a little bench of clay. It's slightly elevated so that at least if someone was to come, they would recognize your position, right? So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam accepted that.
于是我们对先知ﷺ说：“让我们为您准备一个座位吧，这样当陌生人到来时，至少能认出您。”先知ﷺ虽拒绝任何类似宝座或过于华丽的摆设，但圣伴们提议：“我们为您用泥土堆个矮凳如何？那只是个略高于地面的黏土小台——只为让来访者能辨认您的身份。”先知ﷺ便接受了这个建议。
So one day we're sitting with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and he's sitting in a spot and suddenly a man comes amongst us and he is the most handsome and the most good smelling. They said he had a scent of musk that they had ever smelled. I mean, a beautiful man comes to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam in this Majlis amongst the people. Now they were used to being interrupted by Bedouins, right? This man looked very different. He had a different aura to him. His clothes were exceedingly white. His hair was exceedingly black. And so he had a very, very white thawb and it looked like no dirt ever touched his garments. On top of that, we could see no signs of safar, no signs of travel on him. But at the same time, none of us knew who he was. So it's very mysterious, right? I mean, if he's someone from far away, then his clothes should show it. And he would look like he just traveled a long journey. And if he's someone that lives in the city, then we would have known him, especially when he looks like this. So we were very taken aback by his coming. And this shows you, by the way, that Jabir al-Islam would not just come in the form of Dihya, right? Because if it was only Dihya and if he was limited to that in any way, then that would have been the giveaway there, right?
有天我们正与先知ﷺ同坐，他坐在那个位置时，突然有位男子来到我们中间——他是我们见过最俊美、最芬芳的人。据传述，他散发着前所未有的麝香气味。这位俊美的男子就这样来到人群环绕的先知ﷺ面前。当时他们已习惯被贝都因人打扰，但此人截然不同：他周身散发着独特气质，衣袍洁白得耀眼，乌发漆黑如墨，长衫纤尘不染，身上更无任何旅途痕迹。然而在场的所有人都不认识他。这实在是非常神秘——若他来自远方，衣袍应有风尘；若是本地居民，如此出众的容貌我们理应相识。他的出现令众人惊愕不已。这段传述也表明，吉卜利勒天使并非总是以迪赫亚的形象降临，因为若仅限于此形象，当时便会立即被认出。
So he comes and he enters into the Majlis of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and he stops at the edge and he says, As-Salamu Alaikum Ya Muhammad, peace be unto you o Muhammad SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam responds with Salam, and he says can I come closer to you, Ya Muhammad? And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam says come close. He asks three times. Can I come closer? Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam says come close, until eventually he comes right in front of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, and he is sitting directly in front of him, his knees in the knees of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and he puts his hands on his knees, so he's looking directly at the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, very close to him. close to him and he says, Ya Muhammad, tell me what Islam is.
只见他来到先知ﷺ的座席前，停在边缘问候道：“愿平安降临于你，穆罕默德啊！”先知ﷺ回以祝安词后，他请求道：“穆罕默德啊，我可以再靠近些吗？”先知ﷺ应允了。他连续三次请求靠近，先知ﷺ每次都允许，直到他最终跪坐在先知面前，双膝抵着先知的膝盖，双手覆于先知膝上，目光直望先知说道：“穆罕默德啊，请为我阐释伊斯兰是什么。”
Khbirni a'an al-Islam So the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam says what Islam is. He says Islam means to worship Allah and not associate anything with him in that worship, to establish salah, to establish the zakah, to fast the month of Ramadan and to perform the Hajj when you are able to do so. And he then says, Sadaqt You've told the truth. And so the Sahaba say, Ajibna minhu, yas'aluhu wa yusaddiquhu Like we were confused by that because he asked the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam the question and then he was the one that said, you have spoken the truth. As if he's quizzing him, as if he is testing him. So we found that odd as well.
先知ﷺ回答道：“伊斯兰，即崇拜独一的真主ﷻ，不以物配主；履行拜功，完纳天课，封莱月的斋，并有能力时朝觐天房。”那人立即说：“你说得对。”圣伴们见状暗自诧异：“这人既提问又自行肯定答案，仿佛在考问先知ﷺ？”
Then he says, tell me about Iman. Tell me about faith. So the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam says to believe in Allah, to believe in the angels, to believe in the books, to believe in the prophets, to believe in the day of judgment, to believe in divine decree. And then he says, Sadaqt once again, you've told the truth. And then he says, and tell me what Ihsan is. Tell me what excellence is. And he said, it is to worship Allah as if you can see him. And even if you can't see him, then you know that he sees you. And he says, you have spoken the truth.
接着他又问：“请为我阐释信仰。”先知ﷺ答道：“信仰是确信真主ﷻ、天使、经典、众先知、末日，以及前定。”他再次肯定：“你说得对。”随后追问：“请阐释至善是什么？”先知ﷺ说：“是如同亲眼见证般崇拜真主ﷻ，即便你未见祂ﷻ，但确信祂ﷻ在监察着你。”他又肯定：“你说得对。”
Then he says, Ya Muhammad Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam Tell me about the hour. So the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam did not respond. He lowered his head, he didn't respond. So he said, again, tell me about the hour. The Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam did not respond. Then he asked him a third time and he still didn't respond. The Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam then said, the one who is being asked does not know any more than the one who's asking the question.
最后他问：“穆罕默德啊，请告诉我末日何时来临？”先知ﷺ垂首不语。他重复追问两次，先知ﷺ始终沉默。最终先知ﷺ抬头说道：“被问者并不比提问者更知此事。”
Okay. So, you know, this Sa'a, this hour has been concealed even from Jibreel Alayhi Salam. And the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam knows who he is. Right? So the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam is taken aback by the question. And he says, neither the one who is asking nor the one who's being asked knows. Neither of us know when the hour is. So he says, tell me about its signs. And so the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam mentions when you see barefoot Bedouins competing to build tall buildings. And when you see a woman giving birth to her master, right? So he gives some of the signs, particularly of Fasaat, of corruption, of the day of judgment. And Jibreel Alayhi Salam, this man, who we know is Jibreel Alayhi Salam, says, Sadaqt, you've told the truth. [Sahih al-Bukhari #50]
你知道，这个时刻甚至对吉卜利勒都隐藏了。先知ﷺ知道他是谁。所以先知ﷺ被这个问题吓了一跳。他说，提问的人和被提问的人都不知道。我们都不知道这个时刻是什么时候。于是吉卜利勒追问：“那么，请告诉我末日的征兆。”先知ﷺ便提及：“当你看见赤足的贝都因人争相建造高楼，当你目睹女奴生下自己的主人时...”——他ﷺ列举了末日腐败的若干征兆。这位我们已知是吉卜利勒的男子再次肯定：“你说得对。”（《布哈里圣训集》#50）
And then he gets up and he goes away. And, you know, they looked in every direction and he was completely out of sight. He rapidly got up from the majlis of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and he disappeared. And we looked in every direction and we couldn't find him. So anyway, this first and foremost, you know, shows once again that Jibreel Alayhi Salam could take the form of others, not just Dihya radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu when he came in human form. Secondly, that this was a great blessing to the sahabah of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam because anyone that was in the majlis that day got to encounter Jibreel Alayhi Salam. And the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam confirmed that to them. He said, do you know who that is? And he even challenged them, Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam, he said, go look and see if you can find him. No one could find him. And the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam says, do you know who that is? And he said, that is Jibreel Alayhi Salam coming to teach you your religion, coming to teach you your religion.
随后他突然起身离去。众人环顾四周，却见他已彻底消失——他以惊人速度从先知ﷺ的座席间抽身，转眼无影无踪。先知ﷺ随即考验圣伴们：“你们可知道那是谁？”甚至让他们四处寻找，自然无人寻得。最终先知ﷺ揭示：“那正是吉卜利勒，他为教授你们宗教而来。”
And what the ulama say is that there are four types of questions, right? So this was the questioning of Jibreel Alayhi Salam. They say there are questions that are tiham, it's accusations, right? Did you really do this, did you really do that? Then there are questions that are called istifham, questions where you're trying to understand something yourself. Raf'ul jahli an nafsik, that you're trying to remove ignorance from yourself. And then there are questions that are ta'lim, education in general, right? So to increase your knowledge of something. And then there are questions that are called sualat jibrailiya, questions like the questioning of Jibreel Alayhi Salam, which is raf'ul jahli an ghayrik, to remove ignorance of someone else. You ask a question that you know the answer to in hopes that those who are listening will understand and they will be educated as a result.
学者们指出，提问分四种类型：
1.指控式提问——“你真的做了此事吗？”
2.求知式提问——为消除自身无知
3.教学式提问——旨在普及知识
4.吉卜利勒式提问——为消除他人无知，提问者已知答案，只为启迪听众
So SubhanAllah, Jibreel Alayhi Salam, and of course Allah sends Jibreel Alayhi Salam, Jibreel Alayhi Salam is making a scene here, right? And this is going to be an unforgettable incident to the people. And the beauty of that is that what did Allah send him to in this unforgettable scene? Was it just some miracle that takes place? No, it was the fundamentals of the religion that would then be solidified in the memory of the people. And SubhanAllah, this goes on to become as a result of that incident, the most important hadith in Islam. And so mission was certainly accomplished.
赞主ﷻ超绝！吉卜利勒奉真主ﷻ之命刻意营造这场戏剧性场景，让在场者永生难忘。而最精妙之处在于：真主ﷻ让他以什么内容造就这难忘场景？并非奇迹展示，而是夯实宗教根基的核心教义——这段圣训由此成为伊斯兰最重要的圣训之一，使命圆满达成。
EP014 卓越的学子
The Spectacular Student

Angels continuously shower their blessings upon seekers of knowledge. Known for his pursuit of knowledge, Abdullah ibn Abbas (ra) was perpetually in the presence of angels while also being blessed to see Jibreel (as) twice.
天使们持续降福于求知者。以追求知识著称的阿卜杜拉·本·阿巴斯（愿主ﷻ喜悦他），不仅天使常伴其左右，更蒙恩两度亲见吉卜利勒天使（愿他平安）。
Abdullah ibn Abbas radiAllahu ta'ala anhuma only spent three years with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam between the age of 10 and 13. But subhanAllah was so dedicated to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam that what he was able to grasp in those three years sustains us until now. Narrates over a thousand hadith from the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. Becomes Habrul Ummah, the scholar of this Ummah. Becomes someone that we look to in tafsir, someone we look to in fiqh, someone we look to in hadith. I mean, in every way you look to Abdullah ibn Abbas radiAllahu ta'ala anhuma.
阿卜杜拉·本·阿巴斯在10至13岁期间，陪伴先知ﷺ仅三年光阴。然而，赞主ﷻ超绝！他对先知ﷺ的虔诚追随使得这三年间所领悟的智慧至今仍在滋养着我们。他传述了先知ﷺ超过一千段圣训，成为“乌玛的学者”——这个乌玛的智识灯塔。我们在古兰经注中追寻他的见解，在教法实践中遵循他的判断，在圣训研究中仰赖他的传述——无论从何种维度观察，阿卜杜拉·本·阿巴斯都是指引方向的明灯。
The sahaba could have embraced Islam at the same time, but Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala chooses to bless certain companions with certain things. And when you look at Abdullah ibn Abbas radiAllahu ta'ala anhuma, the fact that he's distinguished by knowledge and when a person is in pursuit of knowledge and doing the work of knowledge, not only is it that the inhabitants of the earth are benefiting and the animals and the insects in the environment, but the angels continue to shower their blessings upon that seeker of knowledge. And so just think about ibn Abbas radiAllahu ta'ala anhuma. I mean, he basically was in the presence of angels without any pause, right? From the age of 10 years old, his pursuit of knowledge did not stop when the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam passed away. In fact, it continued all the way until the day of his death. So he's basically someone who's perpetually in the presence of the angels. Now Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala blessed him with something else. And that is that he was blessed with seeing Jibreel alayhi salam two times. Okay, so the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam made dua for him at least two times and he saw Jibreel alayhi salam twice.
圣伴们或许同时归信了伊斯兰，但真主ﷻ以特定恩典拣选某些圣伴。当我们凝视阿卜杜拉·本·阿巴斯时，会发现他被知识所标识的特殊性——当一个人追求知识并践行知识事业时，不仅大地的居民、动物及环境中的昆虫皆蒙其益，更有天使持续向这位求知者倾注吉庆。试想伊本·阿巴斯，可以说自十岁起，即便在先知ﷺ归真后，他求知的步履从未停歇，直至生命最后一刻，始终与天使同在。真主ﷻ更赐予他另一项殊荣：两度亲见吉卜利勒。先知ﷺ至少为他祈祷两次，使他得见吉卜利勒两次。
In one of these incidents, he goes out with his father to meet the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, al-Abbas radiAllahu anhum. And when they get to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is seemingly not paying attention to al-Abbas. So al-Abbas is addressing him and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is seemingly ignoring him. And so we left. Ibn Abbas is talking about himself and his father saying we walked away and al-Abbas was upset by that, right? I mean, it seemed like the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam was ignoring him. So he said, oh, my son, have you seen that your cousin turned away from me? And Abdullah says, I said to my father, I said, oh, my father, didn't you see that there was a person sitting right next to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and he was speaking to him? And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam was giving him attention. Al-Abbas says, what are you talking about? He says there was a man that was sitting with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam wa kana yuhadithohu means he was speaking to him. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam was being attentive to that man. And when you were speaking, you were interrupting. And so the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam remained focused on what that man was saying. So al-Abbas says that let's go back to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and ask him about this.
其中一次事件中，他随父亲阿巴斯（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）前去拜见先知ﷺ。当他们抵达时，先知ﷺ似乎未留意阿巴斯——当阿巴斯向先知致意时，先知仿佛在无视他。伊本·阿巴斯传述道：“我们转身离去时，父亲为此感到不悦。他问道：‘孩子啊，你可曾见你堂兄（指先知ﷺ）回避我？’我回答：‘父亲啊，难道您没看见先知ﷺ身旁坐着一位正在与他交谈的人吗？先知ﷺ正专注于聆听那人讲话，而您的发言打断了他们。’阿巴斯追问：‘你说什么？’我解释道：‘确有男子坐于先知ﷺ身侧与之交谈，先知正全神贯注倾听。您开口时打断了他们的对话，因此先知ﷺ始终专注于那人的话语。’”阿巴斯遂决定：“我们当重返先知ﷺ处求证此事。”
So he takes Abdullah back to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam this time immediately receives him the way that he would typically receive his uncle. And he says, Ya Rasulullah, O Messenger of Allah, Abdullah says that when we came to you, there was a man that was speaking to you. And I'm wondering whether or not that is true. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam looks at Abdullah and he says, Ya Abdullah, did you really see him? And Abdullah says, yes, I did. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, that was Jibreel Alayhis Salaam, and he was the one that kept my attention from you, O my uncle.
父子二人重返先知ﷺ居所时，先知ﷺ此次如常热情接待了叔父。阿巴斯询问：“真主ﷻ的使者啊，阿卜杜拉称先前觐见时您身旁有位交谈者，此事当真？”先知ﷺ凝视阿卜杜拉问道：“阿卜杜拉啊，你果真看见他了？”少年坚定回答：“确然如此。”先知ﷺ揭示真相：“那正是吉卜利勒，叔父啊，正是他使我未能及时回应你。”
SubhanAllah. So this situation here where Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala allowed Abdullah to see Jibreel Alayhis Salaam with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, but not necessarily Al-Abbas radiAllahu Anhu, of course, is still a very righteous man and someone that is beloved to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and beloved to the Ummah.
赞主ﷻ超绝！真主ﷻ使少年阿卜杜拉得见先知ﷺ与吉卜利勒相会的场景，而阿巴斯虽未得见此景——他仍是先知ﷺ所钟爱、稳麦所敬重的虔敬之士。
Another incident is when the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam had Abdullah in his home. And Abdullah, of course, is one of those who narrates the tahajjud of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam while being in his home, because Maymunah radiAllahu Anha was the aunt of Abdullah, so his maternal aunt, and Abdullah got to spend the night there during the time when the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam was with her. In another incident, Al-Abbas sends Abdullah to the home of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, and Abdullah goes to the house and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam was sleeping. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam wakes up and he says, mataj'ita ya habibi, when did you come, O beloved one? When did you come to my home? And he said, about an hour ago, I've been here for about an hour. He says, har ra'ayta ndi ahad? Did you see someone with me? And he said, yes. He said, I saw a particular man. So there's someone that, it's as if he was standing guard for the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, that was Jibreel Alayhi Salatu Wasallam. That Abdullah actually saw Jibreel Alayhi Salam once again, standing in the protection of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam in that moment.
另一段轶事发生在先知ﷺ让阿卜杜拉留宿家中时。这位少年正是通过借宿姨妈梅蒙娜（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）的居所，得以记录先知ﷺ夜功拜的细节。某次，阿巴斯差遣阿卜杜拉前往先知ﷺ住所。少年抵达时，先知ﷺ正在安睡。待先知ﷺ醒来时，慈爱问道：“你何时到来，我的挚爱啊？何时入我室中？”少年答：“约莫一小时前。”先知ﷺ追问：“你可见我身旁有人？”少年如实禀告：“确见一位非凡之人。”原来有人仿佛守护者般立于先知ﷺ身侧。先知ﷺ揭示道：“此乃吉卜利勒。”这是阿卜杜拉第二次得见吉卜利勒——彼时他正护卫着安眠的先知ﷺ。
And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, you know, in one particular narration, he mentions that no one other than a Prophet sees Jibreel Alayhi Salam this way, except that at some point they lose their vision. And I hope that that would be towards the end of your life. And indeed, Abdullah would lose his vision, actually. He would go blind at the end of his life. But we know that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam made dua for him in this instance. He said, Allahumma a'allimuhu at-ta'weel, wa faqihuhu fad-deen, wa ja'aluhu min ahlil iman. Oh Allah, teach him proper interpretation. [Musnad Ahmad #2874] Teach him proper understanding of the deen, and make him from the people of iman. So Abdullah ibn Abbas radiAllahu ta'ala anhuma was blessed with seeing Jibreel Alayhi Salam. And that was not because of his age. That was not because of anything, except for his dedication to the knowledge that Jibreel Alayhi Salam was bringing to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam.
据一则圣训记载，先知ﷺ曾明示：“除先知ﷺ外，凡得见吉卜利勒真容者，终将失明——唯愿此失明发生在你生命末期。”而历史确证，阿卜杜拉晚年双目失明。但正是在这段经历中，先知ﷺ为他献上珍贵祈祷：“主ﷻ啊！求祢赐予他经典诠释之智，授予他教门精微之识，使他成为笃信者中的一员。”（《艾亥迈得圣训集》#2874）真主ﷻ应答了这份祈祷——阿卜杜拉·本·阿巴斯蒙赐亲见吉卜利勒的殊荣。这非凡恩典并非因其年少，全然源于他对吉卜利勒传达给先知ﷺ之知识的虔诚追求。
EP015 先知诗人
The Poet of the Prophet

“Your poetry hurts them more than arrows.” These were the words of Rasulullah ﷺ to Hassan ibn Thabit (ra), the Companion who had a gift for satire and wordplay. He used his talents to craft poetry in defense of the Prophet ﷺ, and Jibreel (as) himself stood by him in support when he recited.
“你的诗句比箭矢更刺痛他们。”这是真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ对哈桑·本·萨比特（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）的赞誉。这位以讽刺诗与文字游戏见长的圣伴，用天赋创作捍卫先知ﷺ的诗篇。当他吟诵时，吉卜利勒（愿他平安）曾亲自降临护持。
Hassan ibn Thabit radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, who is known as the poet of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam has a very interesting story. And he's actually a lot older than what might be suggested when you're reading about him. He was actually older than the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam. So Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was about seven years older than the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam. And he grew up in Yathrib, which would of course become Medina. And he actually could recall when the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam was born, some of the rabbis in Medina expressing objection or speaking about the coming of Ahmed, that this prophet had been born, that they had a feeling that this prophet had been born. So he remembered quite a bit and he was quite traveled.
被尊称为“先知ﷺ诗人”的哈桑·本·萨比特有着非凡的人生轨迹。鲜为人知的是，他的实际年龄远比表面记载更为年长——他较先知ﷺ年长七岁，成长于日后改称麦地那的叶斯里卜。游历四方的他清晰记得先知ﷺ降生时的异象：当时麦地那的犹太学者们激烈争论，预言“艾哈迈德”（即先知穆罕默德ﷺ）已诞生于世。
Now his role, even as a young person, was to be a poet and Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu would do nothing but poetry. And he had a very unique genre of poetry, right? And this is going to span by the way, what he's believed to have lived of a total of 120 years. Okay, 60 years before Islam and 60 years after radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu and his whole life is poetry. So growing up, all he wanted to do was read poetry, attend poetry festivals, write poetry. And eventually he got so good at it that when he's traveling the world, whether it is Damascus or it is the rest of Ash-Sham, greater Syria, or it is Yemen, he is meeting these empires and he's meeting these powerful people and they start to pay him to author poetry on behalf of the rulers of those areas. And so what he does is he authors these poems, but as we said, it was a unique genre. All he did was satire. So if you wanted to ruin your enemies, then you would bring Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu and he would speak on behalf of the emperors in different places.
哈桑专注于诗词歌赋，他的文风独特，并且流传千古。这位长寿的诗人，据传享寿120岁，前60年处于蒙昧时期，后60年归信伊斯兰。早年即以诗才闻名，毕生与诗歌为伴。少年时期，哈桑便显露天资，沉浸于诗歌的海洋——研读诗篇、参与诗会、创作不辍。随着诗艺精进，他的足迹遍及大马士革、沙姆地区乃至也门，为各地君主创作应制诗。其独特之处在于专攻讽刺诗体：若君王欲使敌酋蒙羞，必延请哈桑代笔，以犀利的诗句瓦解对手的威严。
And that was enough to make him a very rich man. In fact, he would be invited to poetry festivals around the world. And if you really wanted to put someone down, you hired Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu to come and to author a poem on your behalf. So he was actually hired by some of the hypocrites in Medina to go out and see the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and he was older than the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and to, you know, author poems against the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. Instead, what ends up happening is he comes out, he sees the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and he's the most beautiful person he's ever seen. He is the most noble person he's ever seen. And he falls in love with the Messenger SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and dedicates himself to being the sha'ir of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, to being the poet of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. Now we know when we look at the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam did not use to respond in like manner to people, but Hassan radiAllahu anhu could, right?
这份才华使他跻身富豪之列——各地诗会竞相邀约，权贵们争相聘请他撰写讽刺诗贬损对手。麦地那的伪信者曾重金聘请他创作攻讦先知ﷺ的诗篇。然而当这位比先知ﷺ年长的诗人亲眼见到真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ时，立即被其超凡的俊美与高贵所折服，从此将诗才全然奉献给捍卫先知的事业。
现在我们知道，当我们看先知ﷺ时，真主ﷻ的使者过去并不以同样的方式回应人们，但哈桑可以，对吧？
So if someone came and they started to speak ill of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala anhu stood up and he would basically run them out of the room. So that was Hassan's job, was to stand up and that was his natural talent. He didn't fight in battles. He could not throw a spear. That's what they said about Hassan. You couldn't give the man a spear. All he could do was write poetry, but he used that talent to defend the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. And one time he had this conversation, interesting conversation with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, which was about the way that he would do poetry. Usually he insults the qawm, usually he insults the people. So he will either insult, you know, your tribe, or he will say that you are not worthy of your tribe. And in the case of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is, you know, fighting against his own people, right?
所以，如果有人来并开始说先知ﷺ的坏话，哈桑就会站起来，把他们赶出房间。所以那就是哈桑的职责——挺身而出，那是他的天赋。据传他从未在战场上战斗，他不会投掷长矛，你不会给他一支长矛。他只会写诗，但他用那份才能来保卫先知ﷺ。有一次，他和先知ﷺ有一段有趣的对话，关于他创作诗歌的方式。通常他会侮辱整个族群，所以他要么侮辱你的部落，要么他会说你不配属于你的部落。而在先知ﷺ的情况下，（这是）在反对他自己的族人，对吧？
Or rather his own people are fighting against him. This is Quraysh versus Quraysh. So this is an interesting situation because you can't insult Abu Sufyan. He's my cousin. You can't insult Abu Lahab. He's my uncle. You can't go after the qawm and start to assign certain things towards the qawm, towards their tribes. And so Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, he says to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, what he says, Ya Rasulullah, I'm going to extract you the way that a person would take hair out of dough, right? So if someone's preparing the dough and a piece of hair is in there, he's saying that, Ya Rasulullah, I'm going to take you out so carefully to make sure that I never insult you when insulting Quraysh on your behalf because of what they throw towards you.
或者更准确地说，是他的族人在与他作战。这是古莱什内部的对抗。所以这是一个有趣的情况，因为你不能侮辱艾布·苏富扬，他是先知ﷺ的堂兄；你不能侮辱艾布·拉哈布，他是先知ﷺ的叔叔。你不能针对整个民族，开始把某些事情归咎于整个民族，归咎于他们的部落。于是哈桑对先知ﷺ说，真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ啊，我会把你小心翼翼地提取出来，就像一个人小心翼翼地从面团里取出一根头发一样，对吧？所以如果有人正在揉面，里面有一根头发，他说：真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ啊，我会如此小心地把你‘取出来’，因古莱什人对你的所作所为，当我在代表你侮辱古莱什人时，绝不会侮辱到你。
So what does he do? He studies the lineage of the people from Mecca through Abu Bakr as-Siddiq radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. So he goes to Abu Bakr radiAllahu Anhu, who was the most knowledgeable of the companions of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam about the various lineages that existed in the tribes and where they came from. And he learns all of those tribes so that he makes sure he never says anything about the tribe of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam or anyone even remotely related to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam in a direct way. So he has the help of Abu Bakr radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, but he also has the help of who? He has the help of Jibreel Alayhi Salam. And that is a notable distinction for him. There were three poets that were notable amongst the companions of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. There's Ka'b ibn Malik radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, Abdullah ibn Rawaha radiAllahu Anhu, and Hassan ibn Thabit radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. But Hassan was the one who was so skilled that they literally put a minbar up for him in the masjid of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam.
那他做了什么？他向艾布·伯克尔·斯迪克（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）请教麦加人的族谱。于是他去找艾布·伯克尔，他是先知ﷺ的圣伴中最了解各部族现存各种族谱及其来源的人。他学习了所有这些部族，以确保他绝不会以直接的方式对任何关于先知ﷺ的部落，或甚至与先知ﷺ有远亲关系的人不敬。所以他得到了艾布·伯克尔的帮助，但他还得到了谁的帮助？他得到了吉卜利勒的帮助，这便是与其他诗人的区别所在。在先知ﷺ的圣伴中，有三位著名的诗人：卡阿布·本·马立克（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）、阿卜杜拉·本·拉瓦哈（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）和哈桑·本·萨比特。但哈桑技艺如此高超，以至于他们确实在先知ﷺ的清真寺里为他设置了一个宣讲台。
So you had the minbar of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and then you had the minbar of Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, where if someone came forth, he would stand up and he would praise the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. And if someone tried to insult the believers, Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu responded with satire towards the disbelievers and those that were hurting the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and hurting the believers. And Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam looked up at Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu one time and he said to Hassan that Jibreel Alayhi Salaam is standing next to you and he is supporting you. And Jibreel Alayhi Salaam supports Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu so long as he defends the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. As long as he is arguing on behalf of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, Jibreel Alayhi Salaam is standing with him. And so we find that Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu on the way to battle, he is the one who is chanting out Rasulullah Fina wa roohul Qudusi laysa lahu kifau that we are marching and the messenger of Allah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is amongst us and roohul Qudus, the Holy Spirit, Jibreel Alayhi Salaam has no match. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam would encourage him and say, Uhjul mushrikeen fa inna Jibreel maak that respond to the disbelievers and Jibreel Alayhi Salaam is with you.
所以先知ﷺ有一个宣讲台，然后哈桑也有一个宣讲台，如果有人前来，他会站起来赞美先知ﷺ。如果有人试图侮辱信士，哈桑就会用讽刺诗回击那些不信道者，以及那些伤害先知ﷺ和伤害信士的人。有一次，真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ抬头看着哈桑，他ﷺ对哈桑说：吉卜利勒正站在你身边，他在支持你。只要哈桑保卫先知ﷺ，吉卜利勒就支持他。只要他是在代表先知ﷺ争辩，吉卜利勒就与他同在。因此我们发现，在前往战场的路上，哈桑就是那个高喊“真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ与我们同在，圣灵（指吉卜利勒）无可匹敌”的人，先知ﷺ会鼓励他说：“回击那些不信道者，吉卜利勒与你同在”。
And something very powerful about this is that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said that while Hassan radiAllahu Anhu was authoring this poetry, that the poetry of Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was more impactful than any arrows that would be thrown towards them. Okay. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam told Hassan radiAllahu Anhu that your poetry hurts them more than arrows. And that is certainly due to the support that would come from Jibreel Alayhi Salaam. And that should be a lesson for us as well. That when you defend the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam intellectually, when you speak on behalf of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, that you can be more impactful than any other way of defending the Messenger SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. However, and I hesitated to mention this, but I will mention it because I think it's important. That Hassan radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was aided by Jibreel Alayhi Salaam so long as he defended the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. However, Hassan radiAllahu Anhu fell into a major sin. And that was that he was one of the people whose tongues passed the slander of Aisha radiAllahu Anhu.
于此，一个非常有力的事实是，先知ﷺ说：当哈桑创作这些诗歌时，哈桑的诗歌比任何射向他们的箭矢都更具杀伤力。所以先知ﷺ告诉哈桑，你的诗歌对他们的伤害超过了箭矢。这无疑是由于来自吉卜利勒的支持。这也应该是给我们的一个启示：当你用理智捍卫先知ﷺ，当你代表先知ﷺ发言时，这可能比任何其他捍卫使者ﷺ的方式都更具影响力。然而，我犹豫是否要提及接下来这一点，但我还是要提，因为这一点很重要：哈桑得到吉卜利勒的帮助，当他捍卫先知ﷺ时。然而，哈桑也犯下了一项大罪。那就是，他用口舌传播了诽谤阿伊莎（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）之言。
So can you imagine, SubhanAllah, this was the man that would defend the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and Jibreel Alayhi Salaam would be there on his behalf. But Hassan radiAllahu Anhu slipped and that same tongue that would defend the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam hurt the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam by passing the slander of Aisha radiAllahu Anhu. And of course he repented and he was punished and he was forgiven. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam did not hold that against him, even though that was very hurtful to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. And this part is really to the virtue of Aisha radiAllahu Anhu. That Aisha radiAllahu Anhu was the one that would defend Hassan when other people put him down because of that mistake that he made, even though she was the primary victim of that. And so the virtues of Hassan, some of them are narrated by Aisha radiAllahu Anhu. She's the one that will speak about the virtues of Hassan bin Thabit radiAllahu Anhu. And that is from the heart and the forgiveness of Aisha radiAllahu Anhu. And there was a time where Hassan visits her at the age of 120 years old and Aisha radiAllahu Anhu honored him, even though SubhanAllah one time he slipped and he said of Aisha what he should not have said about her. And she honored him and she had a chair placed for him and she treated him with respect.
那么，你能想象吗，赞主ﷻ超绝，这就是那个曾保卫先知ﷺ，吉卜利勒曾为他站台的人。但是哈桑失足了，那同一根曾捍卫先知ﷺ的舌头，却通过传播诽谤阿伊莎的言论而伤害了先知ﷺ。当然，他忏悔了，他受到了惩罚，同时也被宽恕了。先知ﷺ并没有因此记恨他，尽管这对先知ﷺ的伤害很深。而这一事件确实彰显了阿伊莎的美德。当其他人因他所犯的那个错误而贬低他时，阿伊莎却是那个维护哈桑的人，尽管她本人是那次事件的主要受害者。因此，关于哈桑的一些美德，正是由阿伊莎传述的。正是她讲述了哈桑·本·萨比特的美德，而这正是源于阿伊莎的宽宏大量。曾经有一次，哈桑在120岁高龄时去拜访她，阿伊莎依然尊敬他，尽管赞主ﷻ超绝，他曾失足并说了关于她不该说的话。而她尊敬他，将他奉为上座，并以礼相待。
And Abdur Rahman, the brother of Aisha found out about that because people talked, they said, you know, Hassan visited Aisha, she let him in the house and she honored him despite the fact that he was one of those that fell into the slander of Aisha at one point. And Abdur Rahman came to Aisha and said, is it true? Did you let him into your home? Did you honor him in this way? And what does she say? She says, you know, may Allah forgive him. First of all, he's blind, he's old. But secondly, kani udafi AAana nabis salallahu alayhi wa sallam He used to defend the Prophet salallahu alayhi wa sallam and Rasulullah salallahu alayhi wa sallam was pleased with that defense. Urwa ibn Zubair radiAllahu Anhu who captures much of the seerah for us, he once was sitting with Aisha radiAllahu Anhu, she was his maternal aunt. And he said to his son that I once started to say some bad things about Hassan and Aisha radiAllahu Anhu objected and she said, la tasubbuhu fa innahu kana yunafihu AAan Rasulullah salallahu alayhi wa sallam Do not say anything bad about Hassan because he used to defend the Prophet salallahu alayhi wa sallam. So subhanAllah in it is, you know, a lesson for us of the blessing of a man when he's doing right, but also that a person can get caught up in their own talent and that a tongue that was, you know, frequent in defending the Prophet salallahu alayhi wa sallam and studying environments and, you know, putting satire forward and things of that sort also slipped for a moment. However, because of the redemption of Islam, he's held by the standard of which he was supporting the Prophet salallahu alayhi wa sallam with Jabir al-Islam aiding him, not the time that he fell to the temptation of Shaitan and used his tongue to hurt the Prophet salallahu alayhi wa sallam.
而阿伊莎的兄弟阿卜杜·拉赫曼得知了此事，因为人们议论纷纷，他们说，你知道，哈桑拜访了阿伊莎，她让他进了屋，并且以礼相待，尽管他曾传播诽谤阿伊莎的言论。阿卜杜·拉赫曼来问阿伊莎：是真的吗？你让他进了你的家？你这样尊敬他？他怎么说？她说：愿真主ﷻ宽恕他。首先，他已年老失明，其次，他过去常常捍卫我们的先知ﷺ，而真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ对那份捍卫感到喜悦。乌尔沃·本·祖拜尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）——他为我们记录了大量圣传——他曾有一次和姨妈阿伊莎坐在一起。他对自己的儿子说，有一次我开始说哈桑的一些坏话，阿伊莎反对了，她说：不要说任何关于哈桑的坏话，因为他过去常常捍卫先知ﷺ。所以赞主ﷻ超绝，你知道，这给了我们的一个启示：当一个人行善时，其恩典犹在；但一个人也可能沉迷于自己的天赋，而那曾频繁捍卫先知ﷺ、察言观色、施展讽刺诗等的舌头，也曾一度失足。然而，由于伊斯兰的救赎本质，他（的功过）是以其毕生支持先知ﷺ的标准来评判的，有吉卜利勒襄助他，而不是以他受恶魔诱惑、用舌头伤害先知ﷺ的那次失误来衡量。
EP016 吉卜利勒（愿他平安）与米卡伊勒（愿他平安）
With Jibreel and Mika’eel

Abu Bakr (ra) and Ali (ra) held a special status in the early days of Islam. Abu Bakr (ra) was the first man to embrace Islam and a close lifelong friend of the Prophet ﷺ, who declared that Abu Bakr (ra) was “like my hearing.” Ali (ra) was the first boy to accept Islam, and he staunchly bore the banner of Islam in battle. Due to their respected status, they were sometimes accompanied by two similarly respected angels, Jibreel (as) and Mika’eel (as).
艾布·伯克尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）和阿里（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）在伊斯兰启示的早期享有特殊地位。艾布·伯克尔是第一位皈依伊斯兰的男性，也是先知ﷺ终生的挚友，先知曾宣称艾布·伯克尔“如同我的听觉”。阿里是第一个接受伊斯兰的男孩，他在战场上坚定地高举伊斯兰的旗帜。由于他们备受尊崇的地位，有时会有两位同样受尊崇的天使——吉卜利勒（愿他平安）和米卡伊勒（愿他平安）——陪伴他们。
So before we get to the general amazing incident of Badr, where you have Malaika everywhere, angels everywhere. I want to talk about some of the wisdoms of the people that were specifically honored. All of the veterans of Badr are honored and that will just be, you know, a chance for us to marvel at the amount of angels that showed up on the day of Badr and the way that Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala honored the Muslims on the day of Badr with all those angels. But people that Allah chose to honor specifically.
在我们谈到白德尔战役那次惊人的事件之前——当时四处都是天使，我想先谈谈那些被特别尊荣的人所蕴含的智慧。所有白德尔战役的老兵都受到尊荣，让我们有机会惊叹那天出现的天使数量之多，以及真主ﷻ如何以那些天使在白德尔日尊荣穆斯林们。但真主ﷻ选择特别尊荣的人……
And it starts off with Abu Bakr and Ali radiAllahu ta'ala anhuma. Abu Bakr radiAllahu anhu, the first man to embrace Islam. Ali radiAllahu ta'ala anhu, the first boy to embrace Islam. And of course, two of the greatest companions of the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam. And Rasulullah salAllahu alayhi wa sallam looks at them on the day of Badr and he says ma ahadikumma Jibreel, one of you has with you right now Jibreel, wa ma'al akhar Mika'il, and the other one has Mika'il, and Israfil malakun azeem. Israfil is a great angel. yashhadu alqital, aw yashhadu al-saf. He is present in the lines amongst the Muslims right now. So before we even start the battle, I want you to know that one of you has Jibreel alayhi salam, the other one has Mika'il alayhi salam, and Israfil alayhi salam is standing amongst the Muslims. [Musnad Ahmad #1257]
首先就是艾布·伯克尔和阿里。艾布·伯克尔是第一位皈依伊斯兰的男性；阿里是第一位皈依伊斯兰的男孩。他们当然是先知ﷺ最伟大的两位圣伴。在白德尔日，先知ﷺ看着他们说：“你们两人中，一人身边有吉卜利勒，另一人身边有米卡伊勒，而伊斯拉菲勒——一位伟大的天使——正见证着战斗，或见证着阵列。此刻他就站在穆斯林的队伍中。”（《艾亥迈得圣训集》 #1257）
So even these three angels are not missing in any way the battle of Badr. They are the best of the angels and the best of the angels as a whole are the angels that participated on the day of Badr. Now Abu Bakr and Ali radiAllahu ta'ala anhuma having Jibreel and Mika'il is significant. And we know about Jibreel alayhi salam, but what is the role of Mika'il alayhi salam other than his virtue? And the ulama say that the narrations as a whole suggest that Mika'il alayhi salam is the angel from whom Allah subhanahu wa'ta'ala brings rain and wind and so vegetation. So al-matar, al-reeh and things of that sort, the rain, the wind, and some of the mercy that comes as a result of that.
因此，这三位天使在白德尔战役中从未缺席。他们是众天使中最优秀的，而参与白德尔战役的天使整体上也是最卓越的。艾布·伯克尔和阿里有吉卜利勒和米卡伊勒相伴，意义重大。我们了解吉卜利勒，但米卡伊勒除了其美德外，还有什么职责呢？学者们指出，综合各种传述表明，真主ﷻ通过米卡伊勒降下雨水、风以及由此带来的仁慈——如植被等，米卡伊勒即掌管降雨、风等事务。
And you'll notice in Badr, in the battle of Badr, before the battle started, Allah subhanahu wa'ta'ala destroyed the army or rather he made it very difficult on the other side with what? Al-matar, with rain, right? It was too wet for them. And so they struggled to actually make it to the battlefield of Badr. The Muslims were already settled there before the army on the other side even made it. And then in al-ahzab, right? In the battle of khandaq, al-ahzab, Allah subhanahu wa'ta'ala sent upon them al-reeh, a strong wind. And so you have Jibreel alayhi salam, who is of course the chief of the mala'ika, the imam of the angels, and the strongest of the angels, and you have Mikael alayhi salam, and the apparent presence of both of them is showing in the way that these battles are playing out.
在白德尔战役开始前，真主ﷻ以什么方式摧毁了敌军，或者说使对方陷入困境？是凭借雨水。大雨导致他们寸步难行，难以抵达战场，而穆斯林早已在战场严阵以待。同样，在同盟军战役（即壕沟之战）中，真主ﷻ向他们派遣了狂风。因此，吉卜利勒作为天使的首领、领袖和最强者，与米卡伊勒的显现，通过这些战役的进程得以体现。
So it is the fadl, the virtue, the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasalam was narrated to have said that my two ministers, my two wazirs from ahl al-sama'a, from the heavens, the inhabitants of the heavens are Jibreel and Mikael, wa min ahl al-ard and from the inhabitants of the earth, Abu Bakr and Umar radiyaAllahu ta'ala anhuma. And the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasalam one time said about Abu Bakr and Umar, may Allah be pleased with them, he said, these two are like as-sam'u wal-basr. These two to me are like my hearing and my seeing. [Sunan at-Tirmidhi #3671]
所以这个恩典就是美德。先知ﷺ曾形容：“我的两位来自天上的助手是吉卜利勒和米卡伊勒，而来自地上的助手是艾布·伯克尔和欧麦尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）。”先知ﷺ还曾评价艾布·伯克尔和欧麦尔：“这两人于我而言，如同我的听觉和视觉。”（《提尔密济圣训集》#3671）
So that was the way that the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasalam praised them and compared them in that sense to Jibreel alayhi salam and Mikael alayhi salam. However, I want to go now to the eulogy of al-Hassan ibn Ali radiyaAllahu ta'ala anhuma when Ali radiyaAllahu anhum passed away. Al-Hassan, the grandson of the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasalam, the son of Ali and Fatima, may Allah be pleased with them. Someone very beloved and close to the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasalam is speaking after the martyrdom of Sayyidina Ali radiyaAllahu ta'ala anhum. And listen to what he says. He said, laqad faraqakum rajrun bil-ams lam yasbiqhu al-awwaluna bi-ilmin wala yudrikhu al-akharun. The people that have passed were not able to match him in his knowledge, nor were those who came after able to catch up to him. So he has surpassed those that have come before him and he surpasses those that come after him. And he says, kana rasoolAllahi salallahu alayhi wasalam yaba'athuhu bil-raya. The Prophet salallahu alayhi wasalam used to send Ali radiyaAllahu ta'ala anhu with the banner. Jibreelu an yameenihi wa Mika'ilu an shimalihi. Jibreel was on his right, Mika'il was on his left. [Musnad Ahmad #1721]
先知ﷺ以此方式赞美他们，并将他们与吉卜利勒和米卡伊勒相提并论。然而，现在我想转向哈桑·本·阿里（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）在阿里殉难后的悼词。哈桑是先知ﷺ的孙子、阿里与法蒂玛（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）之子，一位深受先知ﷺ喜爱和亲近的人。他在阿里牺牲后说道：“昨夜离世之人，前人无法在知识上超越他，后人亦无法企及。” 即阿里超越了前人，也令后人望尘莫及。
他接着说：“真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ曾屡次派遣阿里执掌旗帜。吉卜利勒在他右侧，米卡伊勒在他左侧。”（《艾亥迈得圣训集》#1721）
So the imagery here is very powerful. That Ali radiyaAllahu anhu would be the one to go forward with the banner and Jibreel alayhi wasalam would be on his right and Mika'il alayhi wasalam on his left. And some of the ulama say this is Khaibar, right? And the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasalam gave the banner to Ali in Khaibar, you know, he said that I'm going to give the banner to a man who loves Allah and his messenger and who is beloved by Allah and his messenger salallahu alayhi wasalam. [Sahih al-Bukhari #3702]
这一画面极具冲击力——阿里高举旗帜冲锋时，吉卜利勒在其右，米卡伊勒在其左。部分学者认为这指向海巴尔战役。当时先知ﷺ将旗帜交给阿里，并说：“我将把旗帜交给一个喜爱真主ﷻ及其使者ﷺ，并被真主ﷻ及其使者ﷺ所喜爱的人。”（《布哈里圣训集》#3702）
And everyone wanted to be that man. And it was Ali radiyaAllahu ta'ala anhu. So as Ali went forth, imagine Ali radiyaAllahu anhu moving forward and he has on his right Jibreel alayhi wasalam and he has Mika'il alayhi wasalam on his left. And Hasan radiyaAllahu ta'ala anhu says, la yansarifu hatta yuftaha lahu and he would not come back. When the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasalam would send my father out with a banner, Jibreel on his right and Mika'il on his left. My father would not return back until Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala gave him victory. He was absolutely relentless. And part of that was the aid that Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala gave to him with Jibreel alayhi wasalam, the chief of the angels, one of the two ministers of the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasalam, of the inhabitants of the heavens on his right side and Mika'il alayhi wasalam on his left side. The other of the two ministers of the Prophet salallahu alayhi wasalam from the inhabitants of the heavens. And this was from the righteousness of Ali radiyaAllahu anhu and his virtues and also the virtue of his action and seeking victory, not for any personal glory, but only for the sake of Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala.
当时，每个人都渴望成为那个人——而最终成为那个人的正是阿里。想象这一幕：阿里高举旗帜前进时，吉卜利勒在他右侧，米卡伊勒在他左侧。哈桑说：“先知ﷺ每次派遣我父亲执旗出征时，吉卜利勒在他右边，米卡伊勒在他左边。他绝不会撤退，直到真主ﷻ赐予他胜利。”
阿里的冲锋势不可挡，这力量部分源于真主ﷻ通过两位天使赋予的援助：右侧是吉卜利勒——天使的首领、先知ﷺ来自天界的两位助手之一；左侧是米卡伊勒——另一位天界助手。这既源于阿里的虔诚与美德，也因他只为真主ﷻ的胜利而战，而非任何个人荣耀。
EP017 尊贵的缠头巾
The Honored Turbans

“Every prophet had disciples; my disciple is Al-Zubair.” What actions of Al-Zubair ibn Al-Awam (ra) garnered this praise from the Prophet (s)? It was his readiness to defend Rasulullah even in the face of life-threatening situations. At the Battle of Badr, Al-Zubair was the first to draw his sword, and even the angels took notice. As Jibreel (as) and the angels arrived on the battlefield, they donned the turban of Al-Zubair to honor his bravery.
“每位先知都有门徒，而我的门徒是祖拜尔。”祖拜尔·本·阿瓦姆（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）究竟以何种行为赢得了先知ﷺ如此赞誉？正是他甘愿为捍卫使者ﷺ赴汤蹈火的决心。白德尔战役中，祖拜尔第一个拔剑出鞘，连天使们都为之瞩目。当吉卜利勒（愿他平安）与众天使降临战场时，他们特意佩戴与祖拜尔相同的缠头巾，以致敬他的英勇。
When you talk about the battle of Badr, as we said, there are khasas, there are these specific miracles, specific companions that were honored, and then there is the general outpouring of angels that come. Now, we talked about Abu Bakr radiAllahu anhu and Ali radiAllahu ta'ala anhu and the way that Jibreel alayhis salam and Mika'il alayhis salam are attached to those two in specific, but I want to talk about before Badr, another honor that is bestowed upon one man and the entire preparation of Badr, you know, the day of Badr is an encounter with the angels as a whole.
谈及白德尔战役，正如我们所述，既有专属奇迹——特定圣伴获得的殊荣，也有天使大军倾巢而出的宏大场景。我们已探讨过艾布·伯克尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）与阿里（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）如何与吉卜利勒、米卡伊勒（愿他平安）紧密相连，但此刻我想聚焦战役前夕赐予另一人的荣耀，以及整个白德尔之战的筹备——那日实则是与天使军团的全面相遇。
