First of all, the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasallam spent the entire night before crying to Allah subhanahu wa'ta'ala, praying to Allah subhanahu wa'ta'ala, asking Allah subhanahu wa'ta'ala for his support and Allah subhanahu wa'ta'ala mentions when you called upon your Lord, so he answered you that I am going to support you with 1000 of my angels, one after the other （8：9）and so the way that the Uduma explained this is that first it was 1000 and the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasalam continued to pray and Allah subhanahu wa'ta'ala continued to respond. So Allah sent another 2000 and then Muradifin, the rose behind another 2000 behind those 2000. So if you do the math, 1000 first and then another 2000 and behind those 2000, another 2000. So in total, that's where the number 5000 angels total comes from the Quran that 5000 angels came down on the day of Badr. What is 5000 angels look like when that is almost five times the entire population of human beings on the battlefield at that time subhanAllah. And if you've been to Badr, it's not a very large battlefield. So the angels are basically occupying every spot in the battlefield and around the battlefield and they all come out at one time and you can feel the presence of them arriving.
首先，先知ﷺ在战前整夜向真主ﷻ哭泣祈祷，恳求援助。真主ﷻ在经文中提及：当时，你们求援于你们的主，他就答应了你们：“我要陆续降下一千天神去援助你们。”（8：9）
学者们阐释：最初是一千名天使，而先知ﷺ持续祈祷，真主ﷻ便不断回应——又派遣两千名，随后再增派两千名紧随其后。若计算总数，最初一千，加两千，再加两千，共五千名天使降临白德尔日。试想五千天使的景象：几乎是当时战场人类总数的五倍！若你曾亲临白德尔，便知那并非辽阔战场。天使们遍布战场每个角落，他们同时现身时，你定能感知其临近的震撼。
Abu Usaid radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, he was a companion who fought in the battle of Badr and he was blind later on in life and he said, He said, if I was with you, he's talking to his students. If I was with you right now in Badr and I had my eyesight, I would show you, I would show you the exact Valley, the exact place that all of the angels came out from. [Dalaail al-Nubuwwa ] We felt their presence arrive in the skies from the Valley and subhanAllah, they took the form of our companions.
艾布·乌赛德（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）——这位参加过白德尔战役的圣伴晚年失明后曾对学生们说：“若此刻我能与你们同在白德尔且双目清明，我必指给你们看天使军团降临的那道山谷。”（《先知明证》）我们当时真切感受到他们从山谷上空降临的威仪——他们竟以我们同伴的形象显现！
What does that mean? Some of them came up to us in the form of our companions, but we knew that they weren't our companions. We knew they weren't human beings, right? And they were walking up to us and saying to us, abshiru fa innahum laisu bishay wallahu maakum glad tidings to you, that side, they're nothing and Allah is with you. So the angels were coming to us in the form of our human companions and telling us to have thabat, telling us to be firm, telling us to have the glad tidings that this is going to play out in a way that is going to be to the detriment of the other side. You have nothing to worry about that. As soon as the swords start to strike, the malaika are going to be with you because Allah sent the angels to be with you. And that's why the prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasallam says that shaitaan had a really bad day on the day of Badr. In fact, it was the worst day of his existence, right? So you look at shaitaan, the prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasallam mentions that the worst day of the year for shaitaan annually is Arafah. He hates Arafah. Why does he hate Arafah? Because, and we're talking about Iblis, the devil, right? He has sent his troops to work on these believers to destroy them, right, for the entire year, their entire lifetimes. And here they are on the day of Arafah and Allah forgives them all. So undoes all of their work, all of the work of the shayateen. So the prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasallam said that's his worst day, is the day of Arafah except for Badr.
这意味着什么？部分天使化作圣伴的模样走近我们，但我们心知他们并非凡人。他们对我们说：“坚定吧！敌军不堪一击，真主ﷻ与你们同在。”天使们以人类形貌激励我们坚忍，预告胜利必将属于我们。当刀剑交锋时，天使军团必将参战——这正是真主ﷻ派遣他们到来的意义。先知ﷺ因此说：“恶魔在白德尔日遭遇了最惨痛的失败。”事实上，那是恶魔存在以来最黑暗的一天。先知ﷺ曾指出，恶魔每年最痛恨的日子是阿拉法特日，因为全年蛊惑信士的阴谋将在那日被真主ﷻ的全恕粉碎。但白德尔日更甚！
Why? Amma innahu qad ra'a jibreel yazAAu almanaika Shaytaan looked across and he saw jibreel alayhi wasallam putting the angels in the rows. Okay, so jibreel alayhi wasallam was commanding the army of the angels. When shaitaan saw that, shaitaan knew that this was over, right? This was over before it started. And in fact, even Allah speaks to that. Nakasa ala aqibayhi wa qala inni bari'un minkum inni ara ma la tarawun shaitaan who was pumping them up the other side, when he saw jibreel alayhi wasallam, it's like he threw his arms up and he turned back on his heels and he said to the mushrikeen, he said to the disbelievers, I have nothing to do with you people. I see what you don't see, meaning this battle is over. Okay, so shaitaan gave up on the battle before it even started after he was giving them the hirs, right? This determination to actually go forward on the day of Badr. Now, how did this happen? Right?
为何？当时恶魔亲眼目睹吉卜利勒正在列阵天使军团。当恶魔看见这一幕，他明白大势已去。真主ﷻ在经文中描述：“恶魔见势便逃窜，并对多神教徒说：‘我与你们划清界限，我确已看见你们看不见的天兵！’”原本鼓动敌军的恶魔在开战前临阵脱逃，这场战争还没开始就已结束！这一切是如何发生的呢？
Some of the narrations say that shaitaan took the form of a warrior, particularly the form of suraq ibn Malik and he's calling out to them from behind and saying you might as well turn back because this army is going to defeat you. Abu Jahl responded and Abu Jahl shouted out and said he's lying. This is all just the plan of deception from Muhammad Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and his companions to stop you from going forward. And that's one of the reasons why the ulama say that Abu Jahl is the fir'aun of this ummah. He's the fir'aun of this ummah. He's the pharaoh of this ummah because that's what fir'aun said to his people when they saw the clear domination of Musa alayhis salam over the magicians and they had a chance to believe. He said that this is just the plan of deception, right? makartumuhu fil madina, you plotted this before you came here so that you could destroy our people so that you could take the people out of their place. So Abu Jahl was truly being a pharaoh until the last moment where he was killed in that battle of Badr.
据传述，恶魔当时化身成战士苏拉格·本·马立克的模样，在后方呼喊：“趁早撤退吧！你们必败无疑。”但艾布·贾赫勒厉声呵斥这是穆罕默德ﷺ和他同伴的诡计。学者们因此称艾布·贾赫勒是“这个乌玛的法老”——正如当年法老污蔑穆萨（愿他平安）的奇迹是“精心策划的骗局”一样，艾布·贾赫勒至死都在扮演顽固者的角色，最终殒命于白德尔战场。
Okay, so this is all happening before the battle even starts. This interaction of the angels and the believers, this sight, this vision of shaitaan, of the angels on the other side, the sakinah, the tranquility that is descending into the hearts of the believers. All of this is happening before this even starts. Abu Bakr and Ali. But there's one person who's going to be distinguished in the greatest way subhanAllah before this battle even starts and that man is Az-Zubair radiAllahu ta'ala anhum.
这一切都发生在战役尚未打响之际——天使与信士的互动、恶魔窥见天兵降临的异象、安宁注入信士心灵的奇迹。艾布·伯克尔与阿里已获殊荣，但还有一人将在战前获得更非凡的尊荣——祖拜尔·本·阿瓦姆。
Az-Zubair, the first person to draw his sword in defense of the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasalam. In Mecca when he had heard that the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasalam was attacked he immediately took his sword to defend the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasalam and he was known as the first person to draw his sword in defense of the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasalam. And Rasulullah sallAllahu alayhi wasalam said, li kulli nabiyyin hawariyun, that every single Prophet had disciples. My hawari, my disciples Az-Zubair. [Sahih al-Bukhari #3514] Az-Zubair radiAllahu ta'ala anhum istajaba lil rasul sallAllahu alayhi wasalam. He answers the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasalam throughout his entire life. Anything the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasalam calls for, Az-Zubair comes forward. When no one else would answer the call to march forward, Az-Zubair radiAllahu anhum would always come forward.
祖拜尔是最早为捍卫先知ﷺ的拔剑之人。当麦加传来先知ﷺ遇袭的消息时，他当即执剑奔赴护卫，因而被誉为“首位为主圣挥剑者”。先知ﷺ曾说：“每位先知都有门徒，而我的门徒是祖拜尔。”（《布哈里圣训集》#3514）
终其一生，祖拜尔始终响应先知号召。当众人踌躇不前时，唯有他毅然挺身。
So what is the honor? Az-Zubair radiAllahu ta'ala anhum was wearing a yellow turban on the day of Badr. And when Jibreel and the angels arrive, the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasalam looks at Az-Zubair radiAllahu ta'ala anhum and he says, inna al-malaika nazalat ala simaa az-Zubair. The angels came down dressed with the turban of Az-Zubair. So imagine subhanAllah the feeling of Az-Zubair radiAllahu ta'ala anhum, the honor to Az-Zubair that Jibreel alayhi wasalam and Mikail alayhi wasalam and all of those angels came down wearing the same turban that you are wearing as a sign of honoring you, not just because you're someone beloved to the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wasalam, but because like the angels respond to Allah, you responded to the Prophet of Allah sallAllahu alayhi wasalam in every single situation.
这份殊荣究竟为何？白德尔当日，祖拜尔佩戴黄色缠头巾。当吉卜利勒与天使军团降临时，先知ﷺ注视着他说：“众天使皆以祖拜尔的缠头巾形貌降临。”试想这份震撼——吉卜利勒、米卡伊勒与万千天使，全都佩戴与你相同的缠头巾降临！这不仅因你是先知ﷺ挚爱之人，更因你如同天使响应真主ﷻ般，在每一个时刻都坚定响应真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ的召唤。
EP018 白德尔老兵
The Veterans of Badr

The Battle of Badr brought down angels from the heavens in great numbers to stand alongside those who experienced persecution for their faith. Described as “men as large as mountains,” the angels fought in battle, captured enemies, and even lined up rows of soldiers for prayer. And just as the Companions who participated in Badr were seen as the best of Companions, the angels who fought on that day gained the status of the best of angels.
白德尔战役召来了大批天兵与为主道受迫害者并肩作战。这些天使被描述为“壮如山岳的战士”——他们参与厮杀、俘虏敌军，甚至为穆斯林战士排列礼拜队列。正如参战的圣伴成为圣伴中的精英，当日参战的天使也获得了天使中的至高品级。
Wabi yawmi badrin hina tumha wujuhahum, Jibreel tahta riwa'ina wa muhammadu The famous verses of poetry from Ka'b ibn Malik radiAllahu ta'ala anhu as he looked out at the battlefield of Badr and he said the day of Badr, the day that their faces were disgraced, Jibreel alayhi salam and Muhammad sallAllahu alayhi wa sallam are standing under our banner. Who's going to defeat this army? With Jibreel alayhi salam amongst us and Muhammad sallAllahu alayhi wa sallam amongst us.
“白德尔之日，当敌军颜面扫地之时，吉卜利勒（愿他平安）与穆罕默德ﷺ屹立在我们的旗帜之下。”这是卡阿布·本·马立克（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）的著名诗句。试问：有吉卜利勒与先知ﷺ同在的军队，谁能战胜？
Before the battle starts, the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wa sallam says Hatha Jibreel aakhidun biraasi farasihi alayhi adatul harb He says here's Jibreel alayhi salam holding the head of his horse and he is ready for battle. [Sahih al-Bukhari #3995] He's ready to go forward. This is going to now proceed and subhanAllah the miracles that the sahaba will witness on the day of Badr from the angels which is the time that more companions witnessed angels than any other moment right so the scholars mention more people saw the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wa sallam on Hajjatul Wada' on the farewell Hajj than any other time in the Prophet sallAllahu alayhi wa sallam's life. More people saw Jibreel alayhi salam in human form on the day of Hadith Jibreel alayhi salam than any other day and this is the day of Badr where more companions experienced the mala'ika than all of the other moments of the seerah combined.
战役伊始，先知ﷺ宣告：“看哪！吉卜利勒正执马辔，全副武装准备冲锋。”（《布哈里圣训集》#3995）随后发生的奇迹令圣伴们目眩神驰——学者们指出：这是圣伴目睹天使数量最多的时刻，如同辞朝时，朝觐者是目睹先知ﷺ的人数最多的时刻，“吉卜利勒圣训日”是见证吉卜利勒人形显现最多的一天。而白德尔日，圣伴遇见天使的规模，远超先知ﷺ生平所有事件的总和。
So Abdullah ibn Abbas radiAllahu anhuma describes the scene that as soon as the battle proceeds basically the Muslims are seeing their enemies get pulled off of their horses and flying right and left and they don't even have to do anything. Umar radiAllahu ta'ala anhuma describes that moment where you're going after someone and then you hear a whip from the sky and then the person falls off the horse so the enemies were falling without the Muslims in many cases even having to strike them okay so in one situation there was a Muslim that was chasing someone from the other side as Ibn Abbas radiAllahu anhuma says and just as he was going to him he heard the sound of a whip over that over that man and he heard this voice this voice that was so loud that said aqdim hayzum go forward hayzum [Sahih Muslim #1763] so he said that when he heard that sound he looked up to see who it was and then he looked back at the man and he saw that the man had fallen off of his horse and he had the scar from the lashing on his nose and his face and subhanAllah it had turned green because of the severity of that strike that came to his face so he came to the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wasalam after that and he asked the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wasalam about what he saw and the Prophet salAllahu alaihi wasalam said hayzum was an angel from the third heaven now one of the things the scholars mention is that even the kuffar even the disbelievers and of course many of them were disbelievers only at the time even they saw the angels on that day so Hayl ibn Amr radiAllahu ta'ala anhu of course would not become Muslim until way later he said there were angels on horses that were gliding between the heavens and the earth. I mean these were men that were gliding between the heavens and the earth effortlessly and they killed us and they captured us however they saw fit there was nothing we could do against them and the only logical explanation they could come to on the other side was they said this was some sort of magic or soothsaying the same way they tried to dismiss the Quran by saying that this is just magic and soothsaying and poetry they tried to dismiss the experience they had at Badr by saying maybe this was just some sort of supernatural force that was summoned that we can't make sense of.
阿卜杜拉·本·阿巴斯（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）描述当时场景：穆斯林们亲眼目睹敌军被无形之力拽下马背，左右抛飞——他们甚至无需动手。欧麦尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）回忆道：“当你追击敌人时，空中突然响起鞭声，那人便坠马而亡。”许多敌人未受穆斯林攻击便已毙命。
据伊本·阿巴斯（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）传述：一位穆斯林正追击敌军时，忽闻空中传来震耳欲聋的喝令：“前进吧，海兹姆！”随后便见敌人面部带着绿色鞭痕坠马身亡。他向先知ﷺ禀报此事时，先知告知：“海兹姆是第三层天的天使。”（《穆斯林圣训集》#1763）
学者们指出：即便当时尚未归信的敌兵也目睹了天使——海勒·本·阿慕尔（后皈依伊斯兰）说：“有天使骑着战马在天地间翱翔，随心所欲地击杀俘虏我们，我们毫无招架之力。”但当时的他们如否认古兰经一般否认天使的存在，认为白德尔之战的经历是常人无法理解的魔术。
there's another incident that took place with Abu Yusr al-Sulami radiAllahu ta'ala anhu Abu Yusr was a very small statured man okay a very small statured man and he goes up to al-Abbas radiAllahu ta'ala anhu on the day of Badr now al-Abbas was on the side of the disbelievers but you know there's a difference of opinion about whether he'd already become Muslim and he was concealing it or whether al-Abbas radiAllahu ta'ala anhu was still not Muslim but he wasn't going to fight against the Prophet ﷺ. so he came out on that day because he was forced to come out but al-Abbas radiAllahu ta'ala anhu did not actually lift up his sword against any of the Muslims so he didn't fight technically speaking the Muslims but al-Abbas was a huge man radiAllahu ta'ala anhu a very large man so what happens is that Abu Yusr goes to him and just the idea of Abu Yusr capturing al-Abbas is something that seems impossible because that's what the variation of size looks like. so Abu Yusr he comes holding al-Abbas radiAllahu ta'ala anhu back to the Muslims and the Muslims are looking at him with just complete shock and awe like there is no way that Abu Yusr was able to capture al-Abbas so Abu Yusr comes and he's excited and he's saying Ya Rasulullah there was this man that came I've never seen him before and he helped me tie him up and he put him together and then he told me go forth and the Prophet ﷺ he smiled and he said laqad a'anaka alayhi malakun kareem it was a noble angel that helped you capture al-Abbas radiAllahu ta'ala anhu and bring him back. [Musnad Ahmad #3140]
另一奇迹发生在矮小的艾布·叶斯尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）身上。他竟擒获了身材魁梧的阿巴斯——当时阿巴斯虽被迫随敌军出征，但未对穆斯林挥剑，也有传闻称当时他已是穆斯林，但隐藏了身份。当艾布·叶斯尔押解阿巴斯归来时，因两人体型的差异，众人震惊不已。艾布·叶斯尔兴奋地向先知ﷺ报告：“有个陌生人帮我捆绑了他。”先知ﷺ微笑答道：“是尊贵的天使助你俘虏了阿巴斯。”（《艾亥迈得圣训集》#3140）
now there's an incident that takes place in Mecca that's also describing what happened in Badr Abu Rafi radiAllahu ta'ala anhu who was the servant of al-Abbas. he says that I was sitting with the wife of al-Abbas who we know was a Muslim Lubaba, Lubaba Umm al-Fadl radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. so I was sitting with Lubaba and Abu Lahab was pacing nervously back and forth waiting for the news from Badr. so he's there he's already a Muslim and he's keeping it to himself Lubaba is a Muslim and she's keeping it to herself or rather she is a Muslim but she's not being threatened uh you know because she's the woman of the house and so no one is really touching her they're leaving her alone and al-Abbas is either a secret Muslim or someone who's not hostile to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam but he's on the other side right he's he's fighting technically against the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam so Abu Rafi says we're sitting in the room Abu Lahab is pacing back and forth right waiting for the news and then Abu Sufyan comes and when Abu Sufyan comes Abu Lahab stands up and says Malhabar what happened so Abu Sufyan says to him we found a people that were small in number but they were not deficient in courage they had courage that made up for their numbers they killed us as they wished they captured us as they wished and they had with them these huge men the size of mountains that literally were filling up the space between the earth and the skies and they were striking at us but we couldn't strike back at them
so Abu Rafi says I'm listening to this and I couldn't contain myself in joy so I stood up and I said tilka wallahi al malaika tilka wallahi al malaika I swear by Allah it was the angels I swear by Allah it was the angels and Abu Lahab punches him in the face and starts to beat him violently and that's when Lubaba radiAllahu ta'ala anha takes a pole and cracks it across the skull of Abu Lahab and Abu Lahab would actually die from that strike from Lubaba radiAllahu ta'ala anha
麦加发生的另一事件同样印证白德尔的奇迹。阿巴斯的仆人艾布·拉菲（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）回忆道（当时他已是穆斯林但是隐藏了身份）：“我正与阿巴斯的妻子——已知的穆斯林鲁芭芭·乌姆·法德勒（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）同坐，艾布·莱海布焦躁地来回踱步等待战报。当艾布·苏富扬归来时，艾布·莱海布急问战况。艾布·苏富扬回答：‘我们遭遇了一支人数稀少却勇猛无比的军队，他们随心所欲地击杀俘虏我们。更有如山岳般巨大的战士充斥天地间，我们根本无法反击。’”
艾布·拉菲说：“我听闻此言喜不自胜，高喊：‘以真主ﷻ发誓，那是天使！以真主ﷻ发誓，那是天使！’艾布·莱海布闻言猛击我的面部，鲁芭芭当即抄起木棍重击其头颅——艾布·莱海布最终因此伤毙命。”
so subhanAllah that's even the way that the enemies of the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam were perceiving the angels and the ease with which the battle would flow now subhanAllah the interaction of the Muslims with the malaika with the angels was not just limited to the battle and not just limited to you know feeling the presence and seeing the the enemies fly but the sahaba even experienced something else when Jibreel alayhi salam was with the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam he was also giving the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam the command and the process of salat al khawf how to pray in the middle of a battle. so this prayer where you know some of the troops go they pray the first rak'ah they come out another group grows and prays the first rak'ah they switch out so it's a very interesting way of doing the salah under those circumstances. and the sahaba could feel the warmth of the hands of Jibreel alayhi salam and the angels as they were arranging their rows for salah. so subhanAllah they could feel the hands of the angels putting them in their rows in prayer because even then the angels were straightening the rows for salah subhanAllah even in the midst of battle even in the midst of salat al khawf.
​赞主ﷻ超绝！穆斯林与天使的互动不仅限于战场。当吉卜利勒伴随先知ﷺ时，他还在传授“恐惧拜”的仪式——部分战士先礼第一拜，另一组接替完成。圣伴们甚至能感受到吉卜利勒和天使们排列拜功行列时手掌的温暖，即便在激战中天使仍在整顿他们的拜功队列。
so they encountered Jibreel alayhi salam and they encountered the malaika in ways on the day of Badr that they would remember for all times now was it only memorable to the sahaba to the people or was it memorable to the angels as well. Jibreel alayhi salam comes to the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam and this is a hadith in Bukhari and he says to the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam Ya Rasulullah how do you the companions see the veterans of Badr? [Sahih al-Bukhari #3992] amongst you like what position do you give them and the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam said khiyaruna they're the best of us we put the veterans of Badr on a pedestal and that pedestal by the way would last throughout the entire time of the sahaba like someone who was from the Badriyun the people of Badr always maintained a position that gave them a status amongst the sahaba and Jibreel alayhi salam says what he says he said you know what Ya Rasulullah even the angels the Badriyun from the angels the angels the angels that came down and fought on the day of Badr we give them a status and they are the best amongst us.
白德尔之战成为圣伴们永恒的回忆，而天使们是如何看待这段经历呢？吉卜利勒曾问先知ﷺ：“使者ﷺ啊！你们如何看待白德尔老兵？”先知ﷺ回答：“他们是我们中最优秀者。”（《布哈里圣训集》#3992）吉卜利勒随即表示：“我们天使也将参与白德尔战役的天使视为最尊贵者。”
EP019 在乌侯德战役的战壕中
In the Trenches of Uhud

During the Battle of Uhud, Jibreel (as) and Mika’eel (as) stood by the Prophet ﷺ. Despite the setbacks experienced on that day, one Companion stood out: Mus’ab ibn Umayr (ra). He bore the banner of Islam and drew the threat of injury away from the Prophet ﷺ. Although he was martyred for his efforts, he was given a final honor in battle: an angel who picked up the banner and carried it while taking on the form of Mus’ab (ra).
在乌侯德战役期间，吉卜利勒（愿他平安）和米卡伊勒（愿他平安）站在先知ﷺ身边。尽管当天困难重重，但有一位圣伴脱颖而出：穆萨布·本·乌迈尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）。他高举着伊斯兰的旗帜，并保护先知ﷺ免受伤害。尽管他为此殉教，但他在战斗中获得了最终的荣耀：一位天使拾起了旗帜，并以穆萨布的形象扛着它。
So how come the angels weren't present in Uhud? Or were they present, but in a certain capacity? Or did they come for some time and then leave after another part of the battle? So where were the mala'ika? And this is actually a very instructive part. SubhanAllah, all of the lessons of Uhud also relate to the mala'ika, they relate to the angels. And we look at the narrations. So for one, you also have a narration that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, hatha jabreel aakhidun birasi farasihi alaihi adatil harb That here's Jabreel Alayhis Salaam holding the head of his horse and he's ready for battle. So Jabreel Alayhis Salaam was present on the day of Badr and on the day of Uhud, right? So he's with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam in battle and even when there is no battle.
那么，天使们为什么没有出现在乌侯德呢？或者说他们出现了，但以某种特定的身份？或者他们来了一段时间，然后在战斗的另一个阶段离开了？那么天使们在哪里？这实际上是非常具有启发性的一系列问题。赞主ﷻ超绝，乌侯德之战的所有教训也与天使们相关。我们来看一些传述。首先，有一个传述说，先知ﷺ说：“这是吉卜利勒，手握着马匹的缰绳，他全副武装，准备战斗。”所以吉卜利勒在白德尔和乌侯德那天都在场，对吧？所以他在战斗中，甚至在非战斗时期，都与先知ﷺ在一起。
Now Sa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu has this narration about the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam specifically as well in the battle of Uhud. He said, ra'aytu an yameeni rasoolillahi sallAllahu alaihi wasallam wa an shimalihi yawma uhud rajulayni alayhima thiyabu bayadin ma ra'aytuhu ma qablu wa la ba'd He said that I saw two men with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam on the day of Uhud and they were defending him from his right and they were defending him on his left. And I had never seen those two before and I'd never seen them after. And we came to know that that was Jabreel Alayhis Salaam and Mikail Alayhis Salaam. And in fact, SubhanAllah, even the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam mentions a moment in Uhud where he said that I looked and it was as if the only two I could see were Jabreel Alayhis Salaam and Talha radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. Right, so there was a point in the battle where everything as it was unfolding, it was as if I could only see Jabreel Alayhis Salaam and Talha and we know that Jabreel and Mikail were present and that was testified to by Sa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. So the perspective here is important before we continue with what else happened in the battle.
现在，萨阿德有一段关于先知ﷺ在乌侯德战役中的具体传述。他说：“在乌侯德那天，我看到在真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ的左右各有一人护卫，身着白衣，我此前从未见过他们，此后也再未见过。我们后来得知那正是吉卜利勒和米卡伊勒。事实上，赞主ﷻ超绝，先知ﷺ甚至提到过乌侯德战役中的一个时刻，他说：“我环顾四周，仿佛我只能看到吉卜利勒和泰勒哈（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）。”是的，所以在战斗进行到某个时刻，局势展开之际，仿佛我只能看到吉卜利勒和泰勒哈。我们知道吉卜利勒和米卡伊勒在场，并且萨阿德证实了这一点。因此，在我们继续讲述战役中发生的其他事情之前，这个视角很重要。
Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam always had the angels himself. Okay, he always had the angels himself, but the Muslims collectively lost them due to the actions of the people on the mountain. Okay, the people on the mountain of the archers. The Muslims collectively lost the support of the mala'ika when they disobeyed the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and this is very important that even in battle, right? When you say Allah is with you, that means Allah's support is with you and that manifests itself in the presence of the mala'ika and the presence of the angels in some capacity, okay? And the Muslims will have Allah with them when they follow the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. So because 40 people disobeyed the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and came down from the mountain of archers, that led to the entire plot unfolding, right? Where Khalid bin al-Urid radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu at that time on the other side is able to take the disbelievers around the mountain and capitalize on the Muslims being distracted by what? Being distracted by dunya, being distracted by the spoils of the battle because they thought that it was already over. They had seen the way that Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la gave them a quick victory, even over a larger army.
真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ自己身边一直有天使。他自己身边一直有天使，但穆斯林大众却失去了他们，这是因为山上一些人的行为——那些山上的弓箭手。当穆斯林违背先知ﷺ的命令时，他们全体失去了天使的支持，这一点非常重要，即使在战斗中也是如此，对吧？当你说真主ﷻ与你们同在时，那意味着真主ﷻ的援助与你们同在，这体现为天使的存在和天使以某种身份的存在，明白吗？当穆斯林追随先知ﷺ时，真主ﷻ才会与他们同在。所以，因为40个人违背了先知ﷺ的命令，从山上下来，导致了整个战局的发展，对吧？那时，在另一边的哈立德·本·瓦利德（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）在穆斯林分心的时刻得以带领不信道者绕山包围，他们在分心什么？分心于今世，分心于战利品，因为他们以为战斗已经结束了。他们看到真主ﷻ是如何迅速赐予他们胜利的，即使是面对一支更庞大的军队。
The Muslims won the battle of Uhud in the beginning, even over a larger army. This seemed to play out exactly like Badr. The difference being that that group of people hastened towards the spoils of battle and what was worse, honestly, is not them hastening towards the spoils, it's that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam explicitly told them not to. Okay, now Allah forgave them and they are companions. They're not from the hypocrites. The hypocrites already left before Uhud started. They're from the Sahaba RidwanAllahi Alayhim. So it's a lesson for us as to what transpired and what unfolded in that regard. But at the same time, this happened. Now, with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, did angels appear in any other fashion?
乌侯德战役刚开战时，穆斯林获胜，甚至战胜了人数更多的敌军。这看起来和白德尔战役如出一辙。不同之处在于，那群人急于奔向战利品，更糟糕的是，并不是因为他们急于奔向战利品这一行为，而是先知ﷺ明确禁止他们这样做，但他们却依然这样做。真主ﷻ原谅了他们，他们是圣伴。他们不是伪信士，伪信士在乌侯德战役开始前就已经离开了。他们是圣伴（愿主ﷻ喜悦他们所有人）。所以这对我们来说是一个教训，关于那时发生了什么以及事情是如何发展的。但与此同时，这件事确实发生了。那么，关于先知ﷺ，天使们是否以其他方式出现过？
There's a narration from Abdullah ibn Thabit, which is very powerful, that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam gave the flag, as we know, to Mus'ab ibn Umair radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu during the battle of Uhud. Now Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu went forth as we know, and Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, when he saw the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam being attacked, Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu attracted attention to himself. Why? So that the army would come to him. So he started to make a scene where he was, he started to kick up with his horse, and he basically led the army away from the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam so that they would descend upon him. And that's exactly what happened. Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was not only murdered and martyred, but he was mutilated, right? I mean, he was made an example out of, in that regard. And this was a huge, you know, morale boost to the disbelievers, and it hurt the Muslims quite a bit. But something happened in the battlefield as well. And that is that, as Abdullah ibn Fadl says, that when Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was killed, that banner was taken by Malakun fee soorati Mus'ab. That the banner was then picked up by an angel in the guise of Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu.
有一段来自阿卜杜拉·本·萨比特的强有力的传述，正如我们所知，先知ﷺ在乌侯德战役期间将旗帜交给了穆萨布·本·乌迈尔。穆萨布向前进发时，他看到先知ﷺ受到攻击时，穆萨布便将敌军注意力吸引到了自己身上。为什么？这样敌军就会冲他来。于是他在自己所在的位置制造骚动，他开始策马扬蹄，他把敌军从先知ﷺ身边引开，让他们都冲向他。结果也正是如此——穆萨布不仅被杀害、殉教，而且尸体还遭到肢解。他被当成了警示穆斯林的例子。这极大的鼓舞了不信道者的士气，也让穆斯林非常痛心。但战场上还发生了另一件事——正如阿卜杜拉·本·法德勒所述，当穆萨布被杀时，那面旗帜被一位以穆萨布形象示人的天使接了过去。
So it's as if he was still there, and that an angel is now holding the flag and moving forward, and he is in the form of Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam sees him and says, taqaddam ya Mus'ab, go ahead, oh Mus'ab. And the angel turns to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and says, last to be Mus'ab, I'm not Mus'ab. SubhanAllah. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam realized, annahu malakun uyyida bihi, that he was an angel that Allah Subhanahu wa ta'ala sent to assist him. So one of the honors of Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu is not just that Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu died in such a brave way and was martyred on that day, and is spoken about the way that he was spoken about, gave everything to bring Islam to Medina and gave everything for the sake of Allah Subhanahu wa ta'ala and lost his life that day, but that Allah Subhanahu wa ta'ala even honored him immediately after his death, while he is a shaheed, and he is already taken by the angels because when the shaheed is struck, the malaika take that person, they already see their spot in Jannah. So while he was already enjoying the na'im of Jannah, while his body was mutilated in dunya, as the angels are taking him up, Allah Subhanahu wa ta'ala sends an angel that looks like Mus'ab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu to continue to carry forward the banner of the Muslims on that day of Uhud.
所以，仿佛他仍在场，现在是一位天使举着旗帜前进，他以穆萨布的形象出现，先知ﷺ看到他并说：“前进吧，穆萨布啊”。天使转向先知ﷺ说：“我不是穆萨布”。赞主ﷻ超绝。于是先知ﷺ意识到——他是一位被派来援助他的天使。因此，穆萨布的荣誉之一，不仅在于他以如此英勇的方式牺牲、在那天殉教，以及被后人如此传颂——他将舍弃一切将伊斯兰带到麦地那，为真主ﷻ奉献了一切，并在那天失去了生命——而且真主ﷻ甚至在他死后立即给予了他荣誉，当时他已是烈士，已被天使接走，因为当烈士被击中时，天使会带走那个人，他们已看到自己在天园的位置。所以，当他在今世的躯体被肢解时，他已在享受天园的恩典，与此同时，真主ﷻ派遣了一位长得像穆萨布的天使，继续在乌侯德那天高举穆斯林的旗帜前进。
EP020 真主ﷻ直接与他交谈
Allah Spoke Directly to Him

What happens to those who die in the cause of their Lord? For Abdullah (ra), the father of Jabir (as), it was a beautiful death; he was surrounded by angels, and they remained at his side, shading him with their wings as he was carried off the battlefield. It was Abdullah (ra) who spoke to Allah without a veil between them, and about whom the verse was revealed, “Do not say of those who have been killed in the way of Allah, ‘They are dead.’ Rather, they are with their Lord, being sustained.”
为主道牺牲的人会怎样？对于贾比尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）的父亲阿卜杜拉（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）来说，那是一个美好的死亡；他被天使环绕，天使们留在他身边，当他被抬离战场时，用他们的翅膀为他遮荫。正是阿卜杜拉曾与真主ﷻ直接交谈，中间毫无帷幕相隔，关于他的经文被降示：“你们不要称被杀在真主之道中的人是死的；其实，他们是活的，但你们不知道。”（2：154）
When the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam came to Medina, Jabir ibn Abdillah radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, who was one of the most famous of the Ansar, was a very young man. And he would go on to be one of the most instrumental people in Islam. I mean, the Hajj of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is narrated primarily by Jabir radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. There are so many narrations. There is so much closeness that Jabir radiAllahu Anhu would enjoy with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam throughout his entire life in Medina. But it starts with his father. And what's very interesting about his father is what we know about his father basically pertains to his death.
当先知ﷺ来到麦地那时，杰出的辅士贾比尔·本·阿卜杜拉还非常年轻。他后来成为伊斯兰中最具影响力的人物之一——先知ﷺ的朝觐主要由贾比尔传述，有众多传述。贾比尔在麦地那的整段人生，都享有与先知ﷺ非常亲近的关系，这始于他的父亲。关于他父亲，非常有趣的一点是，我们所知的关于他父亲的事迹基本上都与他的牺牲有关。
His father, Abdullah radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, who embraced Islam, dedicated himself immediately to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. And he had Jabir radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu and then nine daughters. So he has Jabir and he has nine daughters. And because of that, he didn't want Jabir radiAllahu Anhu to fight with him in Badr or in Uhud because he wanted Jabir to take care of his nine sisters in case something happens to him. So on top of Jabir being young, his father asks him to stay back and instead he can serve in another capacity, but he doesn't want him to go forth and actually participate in the battle in case something happens to him. So Jabir radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu was serving the thirsty soldiers. He was doing what he could, but at the same time, he wasn't fighting in the battle because his father directly told him not to.
他的父亲阿卜杜拉归信了伊斯兰，立即将自己奉献给了先知ﷺ。他育有贾比尔一个儿子和九个女儿。正因如此，他不希望贾比尔与他一起参加白德尔或乌侯德战役，万一自己遭遇不测，贾比尔能照顾他的九个姐妹。因此，除了贾比尔年纪尚小之外，他的父亲要求他留下来，他可以用其他方式服务，但父亲不希望他上前线、实地参与战斗，以免他发生意外。所以贾比尔在后方服务口渴的士兵。他尽己所能地做事，但与此同时，他并未参加战斗，因为他的父亲直接告诉他不许去。
Now, before Uhud starts, Jabir radiAllahu Anhu narrates this heart-to-heart conversation between him and his father. Abdullah tells him, waking up on the morning of Uhud, he says, listen, my son, I had a dream last night and the way I interpreted that dream is that I'm not going to make it past this battle, [Sahih al-Bukhari #1351] that, you know, inshAllah, I'm gonna be blessed with martyrdom. I'm gonna be blessed with shahada on this day of Uhud. So he gives him this wasiyya, this will on how to take care of his sisters, the nine daughters he's going to leave behind, as well as some of the debts. He had a lot of debt and he had nine daughters. So it's gonna be difficult on Jabir radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu and his father is telling him, listen, this is what I want you to do after Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la grants me martyrdom because that's what I saw in this dream and that's what I think is gonna happen on the day of Uhud.
在乌侯德战役开始前，贾比尔传述了他和父亲之间的一次倾心交谈。阿卜杜拉告诉他，在乌侯德战役的那天早晨醒来后说：“听着，儿啊，我昨晚做了一个梦，我对这个梦的解读是，我无法在这场战役后生还了。（《布哈里圣训集》#1351）即，若主ﷻ意欲，我将蒙赐殉教的福分，我将在乌侯德战役这一天蒙赐成为烈士的福分。”因此，他给了他一份遗嘱，关于如何照顾他将留下的九个女儿，以及一些债务——他负债累累，还有九个女儿，所以这对贾比尔来说会很艰难。父亲告诉他：听着，当真主ﷻ赐予我殉教的恩典之后，这就是我希望你做的，因为这就是我在梦中看到的，我认为这将在乌侯德战役这天发生。
SubhanAllah, what ends up happening, Jabir radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu mentions that my father was amongst the first that were killed on the day of Uhud [Sahih al-Bukhari #2816] and Jabir was there, but he wasn't allowed to fight. So he then describes the next part of this. He says, Ji'a bi-abi ila nabi salAllahu alayhi wa sallam wa qad muthila bihi They brought the body of my father to the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam wa qad muthila bihi means that he was crucified basically, he was mutilated. He was not simply killed, but his father was basically cut into different pieces. Wa wudhiAA bayni adayhi So he's put in front of the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam and he had a cover on him and the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam was keeping him covered. So he says, dhahabtu akshifu AAan wajhihi that I went to uncover his face. Very powerful and human harrassment. I wanna see my dad's face. And he says, fa nahaani qawmi and everyone held my hand and said, don't do it. They knew what would happen if I saw what was done to my father. And so the women of my family, his sisters and the widow of Abdullah, they started to cry loudly. So they started to wail loudly. And the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam said, la tabki maa zaratil malaika tulilluhu bi ajnihatiha He said, don't cry because I see that until now the angels have not left Abdullah with their wings. They're still following him and they're shading him with their wings.
赞主ﷻ超绝，最终，贾比尔提到：我的父亲是乌侯德战役当天最先牺牲的人之一。（《布哈里圣训集》#2816）贾比尔当时在场，但他不被允许战斗。然后他继续描述当时场景，说：我父亲的遗体被抬到先知ﷺ面前，他如被钉十字架般被肢解了，他的遗体被损毁。他不仅被杀害，而且被砍成了碎块。他被放在先知ﷺ面前，先知ﷺ覆盖了他的残躯。贾比尔说：“我在非常强烈的冲动下去揭开他的脸，我想看看我父亲的脸。但族人们阻止了我，所有人都抓住我的手说，不要这样做。他们知道如果我看到父亲的样子会怎样。接着，我家族的女眷——我的姐妹们和母亲，开始大声哭泣，嚎啕大哭。”先知ﷺ说：“不要哭，因为我看到直到此刻天使们仍未离开阿卜杜拉，仍用翅膀为他遮荫。”
SubhanAllah, what an honor for him. So the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam is seeing the way the malaika are following this procession, even though he has just been killed. They're still keeping their wings over him. Now Jabir radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu says, you know, that obviously this was difficult on the whole family to lose him. They knew that he was blessed. They knew that he had the Bushra. They knew that he was, you know, given the glad tidings and martyred. But at the same time, it hurt them, right? To be missing such an important figure in their family. So he says, the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam came up to me and he said, Ya Jabir ma li araka munkasiran You know, SubhanAllah, it's so beautiful. The Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam even recognizes, Ya Jabir, why do I see you broken? [Ibn Majah #190] Munkasiran, like you're broken. And he says, Ya Rasulullah, my father has been martyred and he left behind, you know, a lot of daughters and a lot of debts. So it's overwhelming. So the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam said, should I not tell you what happened to your father after he passed away? So I said, Bala Ya Rasulullah, Yes, O Messenger of Allah. So we already know the angels were following him around with their wings and shading him throughout that entire process. The Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam says, Ma kalamAllahu ahadan qat illa min warai hijab wa kalama abaka kifahan This is so beautiful. He said, Allah never spoke to anyone except from behind a veil, but he spoke to your father directly. SubhanAllah, like that's a huge blessing, right? That's something that is beyond what anyone could expect. Like Allah spoke to your father directly rather than from behind a veil. And what did he say to him? He said, Ya Abdi, tamanna alayya O my servant, ask me anything, Ya Abdullah, and I'll give it to you. So this is a conversation that the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam is relating to Jabir about a conversation between Allah and his father. So he said, Ya Rabb, he said, O my Lord, tuhyini fa uqtalu fika thaniya O my Lord, my wish is that you send me back to the dunya and I'll be killed in your cause again. SubhanAllah, give me life again, Ya Allah, and let me be martyred for your sake again. Why? Because, you know, the martyr wants to come back, not because the martyr is not enjoying what they have, but subhanAllah, when they see what Allah is giving them, and giving your life for Allah means nothing at that point, right? I mean, it's compared to what Allah is giving to you. So Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala responded, and he said, innahu sabaqa minni annahum ilayha la yarji'oon I have already decreed that the people will not return back to it. Meaning, you're not going to go back to the dunya. And he said, Ya Rabb, fa abligh man warai He said, O my Lord, then convey the news of what is happening with me to those that I left behind. Tell my family what happened with me. So Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala revealed, wala tahsabanna allatheena qutiloo fee sabirillahi amwata bal ahya'un ainda rabbihim yursaqoon Do not say of those who have been killed in the way of Allah that they are dead, rather they are with their Lord being sustained. （2:154）
赞主ﷻ超绝，这对他是何等的荣耀！先知ﷺ看到了天使们如何跟随着这支送葬队伍，尽管他刚被残杀，他们仍然用翅膀为他遮荫。贾比尔说，显然，失去父亲对整个家庭来说都是艰难的。虽然他们知道他蒙福了，他们知道他得到了恩典，他们知道他得到了成为烈士的喜讯，但与此同时，这生离死别也让他们很痛苦，对吧？失去了家庭中如此重要的人物。所以他说，先知ﷺ走到我面前说：“贾比尔啊，为什么我看到你如此沮丧？”赞主ﷻ超绝，这太美好了。先知ﷺ甚至察觉到他的心事了：“贾比尔啊，为什么我看到你心碎了？”（《伊本·玛哲圣训集》#190） 他说：“真主ﷻ的使者啊，我的父亲牺牲了，他留下了九个女儿和许多债务，所以这让我不堪重负。”于是先知ﷺ说：难道我不该告诉你，你父亲去世后发生了什么事吗？我说：“是的，真主ﷻ的使者。”我们已知道天使们曾用翅膀跟随他并为他遮荫。先知ﷺ说：“真主ﷻ从未与任何人交谈过，除非隔着帷幕，但他却直接与你的父亲交谈了。”这太美好了。赞主ﷻ超绝，这是多么巨大的恩典啊，对吧？这超出了任何人的期望。真主ﷻ直接与你的父亲交谈了，而不是隔着帷幕。真主ﷻ对他说了什么？他说：“我的仆人啊，向我祈求吧！ 阿卜杜拉啊，你想要什么，我都会赐予你。”这是先知ﷺ向贾比尔转述的真主ﷻ与他父亲之间的一段对话。于是阿卜杜拉说：“我的主ﷻ啊，他说：“求你使我复活，让我再次为你而战死吧！” 赞主ﷻ超绝，真主ﷻ啊，再赐我生命吧，让我再次为你而殉教吧！为什么？因为，你知道，烈士希望回来，不是因为烈士不享受他们所拥有的恩典，而是赞主ﷻ超绝，当他们看到真主ﷻ赐予他们的恩典时，为主道献出生命在那时已算不得什么了，对吧？我的意思是，与真主ﷻ赐予你的相比，献出生命微不足道。于是真主ﷻ回应说：“我早已注定人们不得复返尘世。”意思是，你不会再回到今世了。阿卜杜拉又说：“我的主啊，那么请将我的消息传达给我身后的人吧，告诉我的家人我的情况。”于是真主ﷻ降示了：“你们不要称被杀在真主之道中的人是死的；其实，他们是活的，他们在真主那里享受给养。”（2：154）
EP021 被天使沐浴
To Be Washed by Them

Handala ibn Abi Amr (ra) fought in the Battle of Uhud, and he died only a day after his wedding night. He was another Companion who received a great blessing in death: not just the presence of angels, but angels who performed his ghusl. For this honor, he was called Ghaseel al Malaa’ika, the one who was washed by the angels.
汉达拉·本·阿比·阿姆尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）参加了吴侯德战役，他在新婚之夜仅一天后就牺牲了。他是另一位在归真时获得巨大恩典的圣伴：不仅有天使在场，而且天使为他洗了大净。因为这一殊荣，他被称为“被天使沐浴者”。
Some of the most wicked people had some of the most righteous children. And we talked about Durra radiAllahu anha, bint Abi Lahab. And what's SubhanAllah, very beautiful when you read about Medina, is that the children of the chief hypocrite, Abdullah ibn Ubaid bin Sarul, his children were righteous Muslims. His children were devoted followers of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. So here you have Abdullah ibn Ubaid bin Sarul, and he is trying to do everything in his power to dismantle the Ummah of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, to undermine the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, to stop Islam, all for his seat of power. Right. And in his own home, Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala brought guidance to his children.
一些最邪恶的人却拥有一些最正直的后代。我们曾谈到杜拉（愿主ﷻ喜悦她），她是艾布·莱海布的女儿。非常奇妙的是，赞主ﷻ超绝，当你阅读麦地那的历史时，会发现伪信士首领阿卜杜拉·本·乌拜·本·萨鲁勒的孩子们是正直的穆斯林，他的孩子们是先知ﷺ虔诚的追随者。所以你看，阿卜杜拉·本·乌拜·本·萨鲁勒本人，他竭尽全力想要瓦解先知ﷺ的乌玛，破坏先知ﷺ的事业，阻止伊斯兰，尽一切努力保住他的权位。然而，在他自己的家中，真主ﷻ将引导赐予了他的孩子们。
And so his daughter is named Jamila, Jamila bint Abdullah ibn Ubaid bin Sarul. And his daughter is married to a man by the name of Handala radiAllahu ta'ala anhu, Ibn Abi Amr, Handala ibn Abi Amr. Now this Handala is not the same as the Handala that is in the famous narration about the angel shaking your hands in the street. Okay, so this is a different Handala radiAllahu ta'ala anhu.
因此，他的女儿名叫贾米拉，（全名是）贾米拉·宾特·阿卜杜拉·本·乌拜·本·萨鲁勒。他的女儿嫁给了一个名叫汉达拉——伊本·阿比·阿姆尔，汉达拉·本·阿比·阿姆尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）的人。注意，这个汉达拉与那段著名传述（关于天使在街上与之握手）中的汉达拉不是同一个人。所以这是另一位汉达拉。
Handala was a young man in Medina who embraced Islam and his father is not like the father of Jabir. Right. So the father of Jabir, righteous companion, elderly companion of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, his father actually went to Mecca to join the enemies of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. So Abi Amr went to Mecca to help the kuffar of Mecca against his own people in Medina because of his hatred of faith. Handala radiAllahu ta'ala anhu instead became a righteous Muslim.
汉达拉是麦地那的一个年轻人，他皈依了伊斯兰。而他的父亲不像贾比尔的父亲。贾比尔的父亲是位正直的圣伴，先知ﷺ年长的同伴，而汉达拉的父亲则去了麦加，加入了先知ﷺ敌人的阵营。阿比·阿姆尔去了麦加帮助麦加的不信道者对抗他自己在麦地那的族人，这是出于他对信仰的仇恨。相反，汉达拉成为了一名正直的穆斯林。
And imagine the situation of this young man. His wedding day was the day before Uhud. He got married the day before the battle of Uhud. To who? To the chief, the daughter of the chief of the hypocrites, who is cutting up the army of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, you know, bringing the hypocrites back to Medina, trying to leave the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and the believers on their own to be killed in the battle of Uhud. The night before he gets married to Jamila, the daughter of Abdullah ibn Ubayy ibn Salul. Now, the day of Uhud comes, he is immediately out the door when he hears the call to come to the battlefield. So he has his wedding night. He comes straight to the battlefield and he lines up where the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam tells him to line up. And Hamdallah is not, you know, doing these calculations in his mind, like, you know, let me, let me sit this one out. I just got married. Let me kind of stay in the back here. Hamdallah is ambitious.
想象一下这个年轻人的处境。他的婚礼日是在吴侯德战役的前一天，他在吴侯德战役的前一天结婚。娶的是谁？娶的是伪信士首领的女儿，而这个首领正在分裂先知ﷺ的军队，他把伪信士们带回麦地那，试图让先知ﷺ和信士们在吴侯德战役中孤立无援地被杀。婚礼前一天晚上，他与阿卜杜拉·本·乌拜·本·萨鲁勒的女儿贾米拉结婚了。现在，吴侯德战役的日子到了，他一听到上战场的召唤就立即出了门。所以他在新婚之夜后，直接奔赴战场，并站在先知ﷺ命令他站的位置上。汉达拉并没有在心里盘算：让我退出这次战斗吧，因为我新婚，让我待在后面这里。汉达拉是雄心勃勃的。
He looks to the heads of the enemies that are coming forward and he sees at the head of them is Abu Sufyan. Right. Abu Sufyan is the most senior person now on the other side. And Hamdallah decides he's going to pursue Abu Sufyan on the day of Uhud. So as the battle starts, Hamdallah goes forward and he's able to even knock Abu Sufyan off of his horse. Now, SubhanAllah, mercy to Abu Sufyan that Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la would instead bless him with guidance later on in his life and he would die fighting on the side of the Muslims. But he was about to die right there. Hamdallah knocks him off of his horse. And as Hamdallah is about to strike him, he had no horse. He was a foot soldier. He got him off of his horse. As he's about to strike him, a man by the name of Shaddad ibn Aswad comes to him and says, comes to him and stabs him from behind. OK, so Hamdallah dies literally the day after his wedding night.
他看向迎面而来的敌人的首领，他看见为首的是艾布·苏富扬。艾布·苏富扬是当时对方阵营中资历最高的人。汉达拉决定在吴侯德战役那天要追击艾布·苏富扬。所以战斗一开始，汉达拉就冲上前去，他甚至能将艾布·苏富扬从马上击落。现在，赞主ﷻ超绝，这是对艾布·苏富扬的仁慈，真主ﷻ后来赐予他引导，他最终在为穆斯林一方作战中牺牲。但他当时差点就在那里死去。汉达拉将他击落马下。艾布·苏富扬没有马了，他当时是步兵，汉达拉把他打下了马。就在汉达拉要攻击他时，一个名叫沙达德·本·阿萨德的人来到他身边，来到他身边，从背后捅刺了他。所以，汉达拉就在新婚之夜的第二天牺牲了。
Now, as the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and the companions are going through the corpses on the day of Uhud, this man has a very interesting situation. He is soaked in water and they're saying to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, Ya Rasulullah, we don't even have water to drink. We have no water. But Hamdallah radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu looks like he just took a bath. This makes no sense to us whatsoever. You know, we're seeing mutilated bodies. We're seeing all these different things. But Hamdallah radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu looks like he just took a bath. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, SubhanAllah, Tughassiluhul malaika ala tibaqin min fidda bayna as-sama'i wal-ardi That he is being washed by the angels between the heavens and the earth. His ghusl is being done by the malaika. The angels are bathing your companion on this special tub between the heavens and the earth. So even the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is amazed by this sight. And Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam says, ask his family what happened.
现在，当先知ﷺ和圣伴们在吴侯德战役那天查看尸体时，他的情况非常奇特。他浑身浸透了水，他们向先知ﷺ报告：“真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ啊，我们连喝的水都没有。我们没有水。但汉达拉看起来像是刚洗过澡一样，这对我们来说完全说不通。你知道，我们看到的是残缺不全的尸体，我们看到的是各种不同的景象，但汉达拉看起来像是刚洗过澡。”先知ﷺ说：“赞主ﷻ超绝！天使们正在天地之间一个银盆中为他沐浴。天使们正在为你们的同伴做大净。”所以连先知ﷺ也对这个景象感到惊奇。真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ说，去问问他的家人发生了什么。
And they go to Jameela and they say that SubhanAllah, it was the wedding night and they were intimate. And when he heard the call in the morning, he was so quick to the battlefield that he didn't even do ghusl. He didn't take his shower in the morning to answer the call of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam quickly. And Allah Subhanahu wa ta'ala instead made it so that the angels were doing his ghusl. And so he becomes known as famously ghaseel al-malaika. His nickname amongst the sahaba is ghaseel al-malaika, the one who was washed by the angels. On top of that, from that one night that he was with his wife, Allah Subhanahu wa ta'ala blessed him with a son. So she realizes sometime after Uhud that she's pregnant and she gives birth to his one son that would survive him. From SubhanAllah, this one night of marriage and she names him Abdullah and Abdullah grows up being known as ibn al-ghaseel, the son of the one who was washed, the son of the one who was washed by the angels.
于是他们去找贾米拉，他们说赞主ﷻ超绝，那是新婚之夜，他们行了房事。当他早晨听到召唤时，他来不及做大净便迅速地奔赴战场。为快速响应先知ﷺ的召唤，他来不及在早晨洗澡。而真主ﷻ却让天使为他做大净。因此他成为了著名的“被天使沐浴者”。他在圣伴们中的称呼就是“被天使沐浴者”。此外，就在他与妻子共度的那一夜，真主ﷻ赐福给他一个儿子。所以贾米拉在吴侯德战役后不久发现自己怀孕了，并生下了他唯一存活的儿子。赞主ﷻ超绝，这个孩子诞生于这仅仅一夜的婚姻，她给儿子起名叫阿卜杜拉。阿卜杜拉长大后被称为“伊本·加西勒”，即“被沐浴者之子”，那位“被天使沐浴者”的儿子。
And SubhanAllah on top of that, actually, if you keep going forward into the history, you'll find that Abdullah lives long enough to also become a mujahid fi sabi'illah. And he fights alongside al-Husayn radiAllahu ta'ala anhu and he was also martyred by Yazid ibn Muawiyah. So his son even goes forward and he dies in a very similar fate. He dies as a martyr. So you see this incident and the scholars say, you know, it's not just the fact that ghusl was done for him as a virtue. It's that Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala sends these angels to take care of things that only Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala knows about. Right. Only Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala knew that he was in a state of janaba still and his wife who was at home. And Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala sends the angels to wash him radiAllahu ta'ala anhu and to honor him. And this is also a part of anything, literally anything that you give up for the sake of Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala in this dunya, Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala will compensate it more generously in the hereafter.
赞主ﷻ超绝，不仅如此，实际上，如果你继续追溯历史，你会发现阿卜杜拉活到成年，也成为了为主道奋斗的战士。他与侯赛因（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）并肩作战，也被叶齐德·本·穆阿维叶杀害而殉教。所以汉达拉的儿子也奋勇直前，并以非常相似的前定死去。他作为烈士牺牲了。所以对于这个事件，学者们说，因为他被洗大净不仅仅是作为一种美德，而是真主ﷻ派遣这些天使来处理只有真主ﷻ知晓的事情。是的。只有真主ﷻ知道他在殉教时仍处于无大净的状态，他的妻子当时在家。真主ﷻ派遣天使来为他沐浴并尊荣他。这也是其中的一部分，任何事物，字面意义上，任何你为真主ﷻ在今世放弃的事物，真主ﷻ必将在后世加倍地补偿你。
EP022 壕沟之战和侯奈因之战：震耳欲聋的声音
Khandaq & Hunayn: Deafening Sounds

What happened during the battles of Hunayn and Khandaq? As the Muslims’ stomachs rolled in fear and nerves, Allah sent help in the most miraculous of ways: through the armies of angels.
侯奈因和壕沟战役期间发生了什么？当穆斯林们因恐惧和紧张而肠胃翻腾时，真主ﷻ以最奇迹的方式派遣了援助：通过天使大军。
While there is never another battle that has the amount of angels that the battle of Badr has, you have to realize that in all of the battles, Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la is pleased with the believers. Uhud has an exception and that's the second half of it. Hunayn, Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la mentions that some of the believers took it for granted because of the large size of their numbers. However, the way the mala'ika play a role in each one of these battles has to be noted.
虽然再没有任何一场战役拥有像白德尔战役那样多的天使，但值得注意的是，在所有战役中，真主ﷻ确是喜悦信士们的。而吴侯德战役的下半场是个例外。在侯奈因战役中，真主ﷻ提到一些信士因为他们人数众多而想当然的得胜而掉以轻心。然而，我们必须注意到天使们在每一场战役中所扮演的角色。
And so I want to actually talk about two incidents and what happens in between. Al-Khandaq and then Hunayn. Al-Khandaq of course was a time when Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la was extremely pleased with the believers, right? They are putting everything on the line and they are surrounded from all directions and they have, you know, the external thing to worry about, which is the largest army they've ever heard of. And they have internal treason to worry about as well. And all the while they're being starved. They are going through a lot of difficulty in Al-Khandaq.
因此，我想谈谈壕沟战役和侯奈因之战这两场战役以及其间发生的事。壕沟战役时，真主ﷻ是极其喜悦信士们的，对吧？他们全力以赴，四面受敌，他们需要担心外部的问题——面对有史以来最庞大的军队，同时他们还要担心内部的叛变，而且他们还一直处于饥荒之中。在壕沟战役中，他们历经千辛万苦。
And Aisha radiAllahu ta'ala Anha describes a scene of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam that when he came home after the battle of Al-Khandaq and he took a bath, Jibreel Alayhi As-Salam came to the door. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam came to the door as well. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam described Jibreel Alayhi As-Salam in his full armor, in his full angelic armor, whatever that would look like. And he was shaking the dust off of his head and he said, qad wadaAAtas silaah wallahi ma wadaAAtu that you have laid down your arms, O Messenger of Allah. I have not laid them down, [Sahih al-Bukhari #4117] meaning there's still something that has to happen here. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said ila ayn, where to? faashara ila bani quraidah Jibreel Alayhi As-Salam pointed towards Bani Quraidah where there was treason that was taking place. So Jibreel Alayhi As-Salam is coming to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and saying we still have to proceed to this direction.
阿依莎（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）描述了一个场景：先知ﷺ在壕沟战役后回到家洗了澡，吉卜利勒（愿他平安）来到门口。先知ﷺ也来到了门口。先知ﷺ描述吉卜利勒穿着他全套的盔甲，他那全套的无法想象的天使盔甲。他正在抖落头上的尘土，并且他说：“你已放下武器了，以真主ﷻ发誓，我还没有放下。”（《布哈里圣训集》#4117）意味着这里还有事情必须处理。先知ﷺ说：“去哪里？” 吉卜利勒指向了白尼·古莱达部落的方向，那里正在发生叛变。所以吉卜利勒来到先知ﷺ这里说：“我们必须向这个方向进发。”
Anas radiAllahu ta'ala anhu describes then when the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam comes out of his home and he starts to make his way towards Bani Quraidah. He says that I could see the dust and the sand just kicking up into the sky because of the marching of Jibreel Alayhi As-Salam's procession when the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam was going out to Bani Quraidah. So you could see in Bani Ghanm, a very particular area in Medina, just all of the earth kicking up because of Jibreel Alayhi As-Salam and that mighty army of the angels making their way towards Bani Quraidah. [Sahih al-Bukhari #4118]
安奈斯（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）描述道：“当先知ﷺ走出家门，开始前往白尼·古莱达部落时，我能看到沙尘直冲云霄，那是因为先知ﷺ前往古莱达部落时，吉卜利勒的队伍行进所致。”（《布哈里圣训集》#4118）所以，在麦地那的某一地区，你会看到整个大地尘土飞扬，那是因为吉卜利勒和他那强大的天使大军正朝着白尼·古莱达部落进发。
Now what's happening in other parts? There's a man by the name of Saeed bin Shuhaym. He was actually part of a tribe from Murrah that was of those that had conspired to come from outside and assist on the inside to attack the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam with Bani Quraidah. So he was a disbeliever at the time and he says that they were coming from the inside to harm the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. They were conspiring and as they were approaching, they heard an announcement to their army that said, Oh people, your families are under attack. And so all of them returned back and while they were returning back, they realized that that voice that they heard was not a human voice. So SubhanAllah, even the enemies were being told to go back and your families are under attack. And that of course deterred them from continuing inwards to harm the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam from the inside.
那么其他地方发生了什么？有一个名叫赛义德·本·舒海姆的人。他来自穆拉部落，是那些密谋从外部前来内部协助白尼·古莱达族攻击先知ﷺ的人之一。所以他当时是不信道者，他说他们正从内部来伤害先知ﷺ。他们正在密谋，当他们接近时，他们听到对他们的军队宣布说：“众人啊，你们的家人正遭受攻击。”于是他们全都返回了。而在他们返回时，他们意识到他们听到的那个声音不是人类的声音。赞主ﷻ超绝，甚至连敌人也被告知“回去，你们的家人正遭受攻击”。而这当然阻止了他们继续向内推进以从内部伤害先知ﷺ。
There's another narration also from someone that was on the other side, Sa'ib bin Yasar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhum. He relates, he said that I asked Yazid bin Amr radiAllahu Anhu, who at that time was again on the other side. He was fighting against the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. I said, what was it like for you during the Battle of Hunayn when you were fighting against the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam? What happened to you and to the disbelievers when you were fighting against us in the Battle of Hunayn, fighting against the Messenger SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam? So he said, what was the feeling? And Yazid said, we felt fear and he took some pebbles, some stones and he started to throw them into a platter. So he's basically shaking a bunch of stones on a platter and he says, this is what our stomachs were like. Kunna najidu fee ajwaafina misal hadha, like we felt this in our stomachs because of what we were encountering on the other side with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam.
还有另一段来自当时在敌方阵营的人的传述，萨伊卜·本·亚萨尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）叙述道，他问过叶齐德·本·阿姆尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他），那时他同样在敌方阵营，当时正在与先知ﷺ作战。萨伊卜问：“在侯奈因战役期间，当你们与先知ﷺ作战时，情况如何？你们和那些不信道者遭遇了什么？”叶齐德说：“我们感到了恐惧。”他拿了一些小石子、大石头，开始把它们扔进一个盘子里，然后他摇晃着盘子里的一堆石头，说：“我们当时在敌方阵营遭遇先知ﷺ时，我们的腹中正如乱石击盘一般手足无措。”
Another man by the same, in the same position, Auf bin Abdur Rahman, he says that when we were fighting the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam on the day of Hunayn, he said as we were about to attack him, now remember in Hunayn, if you go back and you read the details of that battle, I mean, even though it was a large number of people that went out because of the ambushes on the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and his army, it was only a few people that were left in the battlefield. It became a very dangerous situation because of all that had been prepared for them. So he says, as we were about to attack the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, we saw these beautiful men that were huge that came up to us and they said, shahatil wujuh, they said, may your faces be disgraced, farji'u, go back. And he says, hazimna min dhalikal kalam, he said, it was the roar of those words from those huge, handsome men that we had never seen before that caused us to be defeated. So we turned back and we were defeated.
另一个处于同样立场的人——奥夫·本·阿卜杜拉赫曼说当他们在侯奈因那天与先知ﷺ交战时，就在他们要攻击他的时候——现在回想一下侯奈因战役，若回头去读那场战役的细节，尽管（先知ﷺ这边）出动了大批人马，但由于敌军对先知ﷺ及其军队的伏击，战场上的人马只寥寥无几，而敌军的严阵以待使情况变得非常危险。所以他说：“就在我们要攻击先知ﷺ时，我们看到一些极其英俊魁梧的男人出现在我们面前，他们说：‘愿你们蒙羞、回去’，我们因那震耳欲聋之声而溃散四逃。正是那些我们前所未见的、英俊魁梧之人的咆哮声击败乱我们，所以我们转身溃逃了。”
Jubayr ibn Mut'im radiAllahu ta'ala Anwi says when we were with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam in the battle of Hunayn and the battle was raging, he said, I looked up in the sky and I saw what appeared to be a black blanket that was descending from the heavens. And we were all just staring up and looking as it was coming down and it landed right between us and the enemies. And what came out of it? A bunch of ants that scattered about. And he said that caused the enemy to panic. And he said, we had no doubt as the Muslims that these were in fact the mala'ika that were being sent by Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la. So you see incident after incident where Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la sends these angels, sends these troops, and no one knows the junood, no one knows the armies of Allah except for him. And the angels are only one part of that assistance that Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la sends to his believers.
朱拜尔·本·穆特伊姆（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）说，当我们在侯奈因战役中与先知ﷺ在一起，战斗正激烈时，他说，我抬头望向天空，看到似乎有一块黑毯正从天而降。我们都只是抬头凝视着它降落下来，它正好落在了我们和敌人之间。而从里面出来了什么？一群四散开来的蚂蚁，这导致了敌人恐慌。他说：“我们作为穆斯林毫不怀疑，这些实际上就是真主ﷻ派来的天使们。”
所以你看，真主ﷻ派遣这些天使、派遣这些军队的事例一个接一个，除了祂ﷻ，无人知晓真主ﷻ的军队。而天使只是真主ﷻ派遣给其信士的援助的一部分。
EP023 真主ﷻ的宝座为之震动
The Throne of Allah Shook

Companion Sa’ad ibn Mu’adh (ra) had 5 years to spend in service of Allah and in the footsteps of the Messenger ﷺ before a battle wound led to his last breaths. But the miraculous incidents following his death involved 70,000 angels and the Throne of the Most Merciful, serving as clear signs of a life beautifully lived and time used wisely.
圣伴萨阿德·本·穆阿兹（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）在为主道奋斗和追随使者ﷺ的道路上仅有五年时光，随后便因一处战伤而走到了生命的尽头。然而，他去世后发生的种种神迹，包括七万位天使的参与以及至慈主ﷻ的宝座（为之震动），都清晰地印证了他那美好的一生以及被善用的时光。
When you talk about using your time wisely and taking advantage of the years that you have on earth, the companion whose name comes up in my mind is always Sa'ad ibn Mu'adh radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. Now Sa'ad ibn Mu'adh was a chief of Al-Aws in Medina. And if you remember, when Mus'ab radiAllahu anhu went to Medina, he basically got the buy-in of three people and that spread Islam throughout Medina. It was Usaid ibn Hudair, who we've spoken about radiAllahu anhu, and then Sa'ad ibn Mu'adh and Sa'ad ibn Ubadah. May Allah be pleased with them all. So it was these three men at the direction of Mus'ab that brought Islam to and through Medina.
当谈论起善用时间、珍惜在世的光阴时，我脑海中总会浮现出一位圣伴的名字——萨阿德·本·穆阿兹。萨阿德·本·穆阿兹曾是麦地那奥斯部落的首领。如果你还记得，当穆萨卜（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）去到麦地那时，他争取到了三个人的支持，从而将伊斯兰传遍了整个麦地那。这三个人就是乌赛德·本·侯代尔（我们曾提及过他）、萨阿德·本·穆阿兹和萨阿德·本·乌巴达（愿主ﷻ喜悦他们）。正是这三个人在穆萨卜的指引下，将伊斯兰带到了麦地那并使其在麦地那传播开来。
And Sa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala anhu was only 31 years old and he died at the age of 36. So in those five years, the position that he's going to have with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is almost unheard of. It's absolutely stunning how much the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam revered this particular man. And of course that was not just the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam because if the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam loves him that much, then surely that is the love of Allah for him and the love of the angels. Now Sa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala anhu participated in Khandaq and he had a wound that he would not succumb to until sometime afterwards. And of course that was after he would give the judgment on Banu Quraidah as he was looked at as the most trusted person to be able to do so anyway.
萨阿德（皈依时）年仅31岁，去世时也只有36岁。在那五年里，他在先知ﷺ心目中的地位几乎是闻所未闻的。先知ﷺ对他的敬重程度令人惊叹。当然，这不仅仅是先知ﷺ的情感，因为如果先知ﷺ如此喜爱他，那无疑真主ﷻ以及众天使也喜爱他。萨阿德参加了壕沟战役，他受了一处伤，但这伤并未立刻夺去他的生命，而是在之后一段时间才发作。当然，那是在他针对古莱达族人做出判决之后，因为无论如何，他被视为最有资格做出此判决的最受信任之人。
When the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam hears about Sa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala anhu taking his last breaths, Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam prayed Salatul Fajr and he goes quickly towards Sa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. And SubhanAllah as he is making his way towards Sa'ad the Sahaba are walking with him and the straps of their shoes are falling off and their shawls are being lost because of how fast the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is proceeding towards Sa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. And that was not common. So they said to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, they said, Ya Rasulullah, you're walking so fast that we're losing our shoes. We're losing our shawls. What is it Ya Rasulullah? And listen to what he says. He says, Inni akhaafu an tasbiqana almala'ikatu ilayhi kama sabaqatna ila hanbala SubhanAllah. He said, I'm afraid that the angels are going to get to him before we do the way they got to Handala. SubhanAllah. So it's like, I want to get to him before he passes away. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam quickly comes to the bedside of Sa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala anhu and Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is looking around and he is seeing the angels filling the room of Sa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. And he says, Haniyan laka ya Abu Amr. Haniyan laka ya Abu Amr. So he's looking around. He's saying, congratulations to you, O Abu Amr. Congratulations to you. You know, what an amazing life that you lived just five years in your thirties. And what an amazing death that Allah Subhanahu wa ta'ala is blessing you with. The angels are all over Sa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala anhu when he passes away.
当先知ﷺ听说萨阿德即将咽下最后一口气时，他ﷺ礼完晨礼便迅速赶往萨阿德那里。赞主ﷻ超绝，当他赶往萨阿德那里时，圣伴们与他同行，由于先知ﷺ行走得极快，以致于他们的鞋带松脱，缠头巾也掉落，这非比寻常。于是他们对先知ﷺ说：“真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ啊，您走得太快了，以致于我们的鞋子掉了，缠头巾也丢了。这是怎么回事啊，真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ？”请听他是怎么说的。赞主ﷻ超绝，他说：“我担心天使会像他们抢先到达汉达拉那样，在我们之前到达他那里。”赞主ﷻ超绝。这就像是说，我想在他去世前赶到他身边。先知ﷺ迅速来到萨阿德的床边，真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ环顾四周，看到天使挤满了萨阿德的房间。他（对萨阿德）说：“恭喜你啊，艾布·阿慕尔。恭喜你啊。你知道，你在三十多岁的短短五年间，度过了多么非凡的一生。真主ﷻ又赐予了你多么美好的归真。”当萨阿德归真时，天使遍布在他周围。
And in one narration, 70,000 angels came down for the janaza of Sa'ad ibn Mu'adh that had never tread the earth before. 70,000 mala'ika, especially sent for the sake of attending and participating in the janaza of this man, Sa'ad ibn Mu'adh radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. Now, when they took Sa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala anhu to be buried, one of the things that the sahaba said, they said that it was as if his body was carrying us. He was, the body was so light and it was going so fast that it was proceeding and rushing towards the grave. And we couldn't keep up with it. And the hypocrites, they mocked Sa'ad. They said, he's light in a way of mockery. And the prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said what? That that was the angels that were rushing him to his grave. Remember the believing soul says, qadimooni qadimooni, hurry up, hurry up. Take me to my grave. Take me to my grave so that they can enjoy what Allah Subhanahu wa ta'ala has prepared for them.
在一段传述中，七万位天使为了萨阿德·本·穆阿兹的殡礼而降下，这些天使此前从未踏足过大地。七万位天使被特别派遣来参与萨阿德·本·穆阿兹的殡礼。当他们抬着萨阿德去埋葬时，圣伴们提到：感觉好像是遗体在抬着我们走。他（的遗体）是如此的轻，行进得如此之快，仿佛在奔向坟墓，我们几乎跟不上它。伪信者们则讥讽萨阿德，以一种嘲弄的口吻说：“他（的遗体）真轻啊。”先知ﷺ说了什么？他说：“那是天使们在抬着他奔向他的坟墓。”记得吗？有信仰的灵魂会说：快抬我走，快抬我走，带我去我的坟墓，带我去我的坟墓。这样他们就能享受真主ﷻ为他们预备的恩典了。
One of the narrations as well is that the prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam says, ihtazza arshurrahman, the throne of the most merciful shook by the death of Sa'ad ibn Muadh radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. [Sahih al-Bukhari #3803] The man's death shook the throne of ar-Rahman. Now that's a powerful narration and it needs some context because what does that even mean? Did it shake because of tragedy or did it shake out of joy? You might remember there's a hadith that the ulama liken this to that the prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam was walking on Uhud with Abu Bakr, Umar and Uthman, may Allah be pleased with them. And Uhud shook. Why did Uhud shake? The prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, Uhud is a mountain that loves us and we love it. And the prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, he kneeled down and he tapped Uhud and he said to Uhud, uthbut ya Uhud, be firm oh Uhud, because you have upon you a prophet, a siddiq and two martyrs. So the prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, Abu Bakr, Umar and Uthman. So it shook out of love, out of honor, out of haybah for who was on it. And so when we talk about the throne of the most merciful, it shook out of joy for Sa'd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu.
还有一段传述是，先知ﷺ说：“至仁主ﷻ的宝座因萨阿德·本·穆阿兹的去世而震动了。”（《布哈里圣训集》#3803）这个人的去世震动了至仁主ﷻ的宝座。这是一段非常有力的传述，需要一些背景解释，因为这到底是什么意思呢？宝座震动是因为悲伤还是因为喜悦？你可能记得有一段圣训，学者们常将此事与之类比：先知ﷺ曾与艾布·伯克尔、欧麦尔和奥斯曼（愿主ﷻ喜悦他们）一起走在吴侯德山上。吴侯德山震动了。为什么吴侯德山震动了？先知ﷺ说：“吴侯德山是一座爱我们、我们也爱它的山。”先知ﷺ跪下轻拍吴侯德山，对它说：“请坚定呀，乌侯德山啊！”，因为山上有一位先知ﷺ、一位诚实的信士和两位殉道者——先知ﷺ、艾布·伯克尔、欧麦尔和奥斯曼。因此，它（吴侯德山）是因爱、因敬意、因对山上之人的崇敬而震动的。同样，当我们说到至仁主ﷻ的宝座时，它是因喜悦萨阿德而震动的。
And one of the ways that the scholars explained that is what? The prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam mentioned that the souls of the shuhada, the souls of the martyrs are placed in the bodies of green birds. Now these green birds are not like the green birds that we would see or any birds that we would see, something that is heavenly. And those birds roam the gardens and the sustenance of paradise. And they eat from the fruits of paradise as they please. And then they come back to these chandeliers that are nestled from the throne of Ar-Rahman. So their nests, where they come back to are these lamps, these chandeliers that are hanging from the throne of the most merciful. And so the throne of Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la, as the ulama mentioned, the throne of Allah shook in a way of joy from receiving the soul of this blessed man for whom the angels crowded his janaza and accompanied him to his grave. And a man that the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam loved so much, Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la said, even Sa'd radiAllahu Anhu, if anyone was going to be spared from the squeeze of the grape, it would have been Sa'd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. But even Sa'd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu had the squeeze of the grave. So it's a reminder that every human being has to go through certain things. But all of us can aspire to receive what the soul of Sa'd radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu received.
学者们解释这一点的方式之一是引用了什么根据呢？先知ﷺ提到过，殉道者的灵魂被置于绿鸟的身体里。这些绿鸟并非我们所见的那种绿鸟或任何我们可见的鸟，它们是来自天园的。这些鸟儿在天园的花园中飞翔，享用天园的给养。它们随心所欲地享用天园的果实，然后它们飞回到这些悬挂在至仁主ﷻ宝座上的枝形灯架里。所以它们归巢的地方，就是这些悬挂在至仁主ﷻ宝座上的灯盏、枝形灯架。因此，正如学者们所提到的，真主ﷻ的宝座震动，是喜悦地迎接这位蒙福之人灵魂的一种方式。这位蒙福之人的殡礼挤满了天使，并陪伴他走向坟墓。先知ﷺ如此深爱的这个人，真主ﷻ说，即使是萨阿德，如果有人能免遭“葡萄挤压”（指坟坑的拷问），那本该是萨阿德。但即使是萨阿德，同样也经历了坟坑的挤压。所以这是一个提醒，每个人都必须经历某些事情，但我们所有人都可以渴望获得萨阿德的灵魂所获得的恩典。
EP024 当恶魔逃遁时
When the Devils Flee

The Prophet ﷺ once famously told Umar ibn al-Khattab (ra): “Shaytan does not see you take a path, O Umar, except that he takes a path other than yours.” But just as the devils would run from him, the angels had a special connection with him; and he was amongst those so divinely inspired, it was as if the angels were speaking on his tongue.
先知ﷺ曾对欧麦尔·本·哈塔布（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）说过一句名言：“恶魔绝不会看到你选择一条道路，欧麦尔啊，除非他选择一条与你不同的道路。” 但正如恶魔会逃离他，天使们与他也有着特殊的联系；他是那些蒙受神圣启示的人之一，仿佛天使借他之口说话。
There is a very famous incident with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and Abu Bakr As-Siddiq radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu where Abu Bakr radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu responds to an insult and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam leaves and then he informs Abu Bakr radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu that before you responded, there was an angel that was responding on our behalf. And then once you started to respond, the angel left and a shaitan came instead. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, I didn't want to be seated in the presence of the shaitan.
有一个非常著名的事件，发生在先知ﷺ和艾布·伯克尔·西迪克（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）之间。艾布·伯克尔回应了一次侮辱，先知ﷺ离开了，随后他告诉艾布·伯克尔：在你回应之前，有一位天使在代表我们回应。然后一旦你开始回应，天使就离开了，取而代之的是一个恶魔来了。先知ﷺ说，我不愿与恶魔同席而坐。
Now that's when overcoming someone else. What about when you're overcoming your own nafs? And so this is where I want to segue into Umar bin al-Khattab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. There's a general rule that you can extract that anywhere the shaitan are not, there is an extra amount of angels and where the angels are present, the shayateen are not. And so they cannot both be in the same place. And it's ultimately going to be our actions that determine who's where, how many angels are around us or how many shayateen are around us. So when we act like the angels, then we are surrounded by the angels. When we act like shayateen or we give in to their whispering, then we're surrounded by shayateen. So why is this significant to Umar bin al-Khattab radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu? Particularly when it comes to the mala'ika.
这是在克服他人（的挑衅）时的情况。那么当你克服自己的私欲时呢？因此，这就是我想过渡到欧麦尔·本·哈塔布的地方。这里可以提取一个普遍规律：凡恶魔不在之处，必有更多天使；凡天使所在之处，恶魔就不在。因此，他们无法同时出现在同一个地方。最终，是我们的行为决定了谁在哪里，我们周围有多少天使或有多少恶魔。所以，当我们行为如天使时，我们就被天使环绕。当我们行为如恶魔或屈服于他们的低语时，我们就被恶魔环绕。那么这对欧麦尔·本·哈塔布有何重要意义？尤其是在涉及天使方面。
Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu is a man who was known for karamat al-awliya. He had so many miracles, things that happened to him that were seemingly super natural. And it actually has to start from this point where the Prophet sallAllahu alaihi wasallam says to him, ma laqiaqash shaytaanu qattu salikan fajjan illa salaka fajjan ghayra fajjik That shaytan does not see you take a pathway, O Umar, except that shaytan flees you and takes another pathway. Shaytan is so distant from you. He's so afraid of you. You have such power over yourself to where he can't penetrate that shaytan will not even be seen on the same street as you, O Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. And so that's where, you know, with that authentic narration, you have other narrations that show up in the books of virtues that are narrated that the Prophet sallAllahu alaihi wasallam had said, inna shaytana la yalqa'a Umar mundhu aslama illa kharra liwajhihi That shaytan does not find Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu or has not come across Umar from the day that Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu became Muslim, except that he was humiliated.
欧麦尔是一个以“圣徒奇迹”著称的人。他拥有如此多的奇迹，发生在他身上的事似乎是超自然的。而这实际上必须从先知ﷺ对他说的这句话开始：“恶魔绝不会看到你选择一条道路，欧麦尔啊，除非恶魔逃离你并选择另一条道路。”恶魔如此远离你。他如此惧怕你。你对自己拥有如此强大的掌控力，以至于恶魔无法渗透，恶魔甚至不会与你出现在同一条街上，欧麦尔啊。因此，正是基于这段可靠的传述，在美德书中还有其它被传述的记载，先知ﷺ曾说过：“恶魔自欧麦尔归信伊斯兰以来，凡遇见他，无不蒙羞。”
Or another narration that ma fis sama'i malakun illa wahu yuwaqqiru Umar That there is not an angel in the heavens, except that that angel supports Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu and loves Umar radiAllahu Anhu. wa ma fil ardi shaytan illa wahu yafirru min Umar And there is not a devil on the earth, except that they flee from Umar. If the devil flees from Umar, then what does that mean in terms of the angels being in his presence? And so that's where you see a man who was so dedicated, so committed that these miracles were natural in the way that they would come to him radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu Because if the shayateen are never around him, then that means that Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu is always under the influence of who? He's always under the influence of the malaika, under the influence of the angels. And so from the moment that he becomes Muslim, the angels celebrating his Islam, the angels being taught to love him in the heavens, the angels surrounding him on earth because he doesn't do anything that would warrant the presence of the shaytan. And so that's where you find that hadith of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, where the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam says that there were people in the nations that came before you that were muhaddathoon. They were spoken to. And when you say they are spoken to, that means that they're naturally inspired towards good. They're naturally inspired by the angels. There's something divine about their ilham, about their inspiration. And if there's anyone in my ummah like that, it's Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu and one of the explanations of that, tatekellam al malaikatu ala lisanihi, it's as if the angels are speaking on his tongue.
另一段传述说：“凡在天上的天使，无不尊敬欧麦尔并喜爱欧麦尔。凡在地上的恶魔，无不在欧麦尔面前逃遁。”如果恶魔从欧麦尔面前逃遁，那么天使在他身边，意味着什么？因此，你会看到一个如此虔诚、如此坚定的人，以至于这些奇迹对他来说自然而然地发生。因为如果恶魔从未在他身边，那就意味着欧麦尔始终处于谁的影响之下？他始终处于天使的影响之下，处于天使的引导之下。因此，从他归信伊斯兰的那一刻起，天使们庆祝他的归信，天使们在天空中被教导去喜爱他，天使们在地上环绕着他，因为他不做任何会招致恶魔出现的事。因此，你会在先知ﷺ的一段圣训中发现这一点，先知说：在你们之前的民族中，曾有一些人是“受启示者”。他们被（天使）启示。当你说他们被启示时，意思是他们天然地被引向善行。他们天然地受到天使的启示。他们的灵感，他们的启示具有某种神圣性。如果我的乌玛中有这样的人，那就是欧麦尔。对此的一种解释是：仿佛天使借他之口说话。
SubhanAllah, imagine a human being that is so in tune with righteousness, with the good part of their souls, with the obedience to Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala, that when they speak, they are so divinely pushed towards good naturally because of what they are seeking. That it's as if the angels are speaking on their tongue. And that is why with Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, he was so divinely inspired because of the amount of angels he invited around him, that it was as if angels were speaking on his tongue. And indeed, there would come times where Jibreel alayhi salam brings the revelation from the heavens in the form of Quran and the wahi agrees with the ilham of Umar. The revelation agrees with the inspiration of Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu because the one who sends Jibreel alayhi salam surrounds Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu with angels and Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu is inspired towards righteousness and Umar radiAllahu Anhu is committed towards righteousness. And that gives him perception and gives him an environment where when you're interacting with this human being of Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, you're interacting with a man that is surrounded by and has within him angels, right, that are inspiring him towards good. And that's why when you read the narration of Umar radiAllahu Anhu calling out to Sariyah, you know, miles and miles away, thousands of miles away, hearing something, seeing something miles away, that a miracle like that is given to Umar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. Because as some of the ilmah mention, the angels caused his voice to be heard in those places that were so far away. And that is not something that was randomly given to Umar radiAllahu Anhu, just like none of the sahaba randomly achieve these things. But that is something due to his sincere pursuit of truth. When you sincerely pursue truth, then you are surrounded by the soldiers of truth and they are the angels of Allah subhana wa ta'ala.
赞主ﷻ超绝，想象一个人如此契合于正义、契合于灵魂中善良的部分、契合于顺服真主ﷻ，以至于当他们说话时，他们天然地如此受神圣力量自然引向善，因为他们所追求的（目标）。仿佛天使借他们的舌头说话。正因如此，欧麦尔拥有如此神圣的灵感，皆因他所吸引的天使之多，仿佛天使借他之口说话。的确，有时吉卜利勒（愿他平安）会从天上带来古兰经形式的启示，而这天启与欧麦尔的灵感相吻合。启示与欧麦尔的灵感一致，因为派遣吉卜利勒的主，使天使们环绕着欧麦尔，而欧麦尔受引向正义，并且欧麦尔致力于正义。这赋予了他洞察力，并为他提供了一个环境，当你与欧麦尔这个人互动时，你是在与一个被天使环绕、内心有天使的人互动，这些天使正激励他行善。这就是为什么当你读到欧麦尔呼唤萨里亚的传述时，你知道，那是相隔数英里、数千英里之外，听到某种声音，看到远方某事的奇迹，这样的奇迹被赐予了欧麦尔。因为正如一些学者所提到的，天使使他的声音在那些遥远的地方被听到。这并非随意赐予欧麦尔的，就像任何圣伴们都不会随意获得这些一样。但这（奇迹）是源于他对真理真诚的追求。当你真诚地追求真理时，那么你就会被真理的战士所环绕，他们就是真主ﷻ的天使。

EP025 天使因他而感到害羞
They Were Shy of Him
All four khulafa’ had beautiful interactions with the angels, but Companion Uthman ibn Affan’s (ra) connection with them was unique. Though he was known for his recitation of the Qur’an and his giving hands, it was his haya’—modesty and shyness—that was so unparalleled that even the angels changed their demeanor around him.
四位哈里发都与天使有着美好的互动，但圣伴奥斯曼·本·阿凡（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）与天使的联系是独特的。尽管他以诵读古兰经和慷慨施舍而闻名，但他那无与伦比的敬畏与廉耻使得甚至连天使在他面前都改变了他们的举止。
Every one of the four khulafa has a connection to Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala and an interaction with the angels that fits exactly what they are famous for. So with Abu Bakr radiAllahu ta'ala anhu, it is he is as-siddiq. The highest name that you could be written with Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala with is as-siddiq, right? And there's even a hadith, حَتَّى يُكْتَبَ عِنْدَ اللَّهِ صِدِّيقٍ that a person continues to tell the truth and do the actions of truth until they're written with Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala as a person of truth. So this name comes down from Jibreel alayhi salam to the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam. And this is what he's known by and Abu Bakr radiAllahu anhu is a celebrity of sorts amongst the angels, right? On the day of judgment, they're all calling him to their gate of Jannah.
四位哈里发中的每一位都与真主ﷻ有着联系，并与天使有着与其最著名特质完全契合的互动。对于艾布·伯克尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他），他是“诚实者”。你能在真主ﷻ那里被记录的最高名字就是“诚实者”，对吧？甚至有一段圣训提到：一个人持续说实话并践行真理的行为，直到他在真主ﷻ那里被记录为诚实之人。所以这个名字从吉卜利勒天使（愿他平安）降示给先知ﷺ。这就是他所闻名的，而艾布·伯克尔在某种程度上是天使中的名人，对吧？在审判日，他们都在召唤他走向他们的天园之门。
And Umar radiAllahu ta'ala anhu is the one who's in touch with the truth, right? The one who subhanAllah is speaking to the angels and the angels speak through him in regards to his judgment, in regards to his perception because the shayateen would be nowhere around him. And then Ali radiAllahu anhu, right? Who is a man of courage, bravery, who is so courageous and so dedicated and relentless in his dedication that he would be blessed with Jibreel alayhi salam and Mikail alayhi salam next to him as he goes forth carrying the banner of Islam.
欧麦尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）则是与真理相通之人，对吧？赞主ﷻ超绝，他与天使交谈，天使也通过他说话，关乎他的判断力，关乎他的洞察力，因为恶魔会远离他。然后是阿里（愿主ﷻ喜悦他），对吧？他是一位勇敢无畏的人，如此英勇，如此专注，在奉献上坚定不移，以至于当他高举伊斯兰的旗帜前进时，吉卜利勒天使和米卡伊勒天使（愿他平安）会在他身边祝福他。
Now with Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu, there is something very special about Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu because usually we talk about the angels inspiring goodness in us, meaning the angels in our presence change our demeanor. In the case of Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu, this man reached such a level that he changed the demeanor of the angels and that's not known with anybody else.
而对于奥斯曼，他身上有某种非常特别的东西，因为我们通常谈论的是天使启发我们行善，意思是我们身边的天使改变了我们的举止。在奥斯曼的情况下，这个人达到了如此高的境界，以至于他改变了天使的举止，这在其他人身上是未曾听闻的。
And this goes back to a famous story with the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam with Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu that Aisha radiAllahu anhu narrates that the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam was sitting at home and as Abu Bakr radiAllahu ta'ala anhu came to visit the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam, he was reclined. And so his leg was a little bit exposed. The Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam was in a relaxed state. Abu Bakr radiAllahu anhu came in the house kind of stayed as it was. He sat with the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam for some time. He asked the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam when he had to ask him and then he left. Umar radiAllahu ta'ala anhu came in as the order always is. And the same thing happened. The Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam remained in his place and the house remained as it was. And Umar radiAllahu anhu asked what he had to ask and then he left when he got what he wanted. And then Uthman radiAllahu anhu came. And Aisha radiAllahu anhu says the strangest thing happens that the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam suddenly starts to say fix up the house, you know, put the blanket here and he sits up radiAllahu anhu and he fixes his garments. And Uthman radiAllahu anhu comes in and the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam receives him in a very unique and special way. Now Aisha radiAllahu anhu is watching this and when Uthman leaves, she says I was very curious. You know, we know the status of Abu Bakr. We know the status of Umar. Why did the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam act this way with Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu? I asked the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam, Ya Rasulullah, Abu Bakr came in, Umar came in. You didn't do what you did when Uthman came in. And the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam said, ala astahi mimman yastahi minhu al-mala'ika Should I not be shy of someone whom the angels are shy from? [Sahih Muslim #2401]
这源于先知ﷺ与奥斯曼之间一个著名的故事：阿伊莎（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）传述说，先知ﷺ在家坐着，当艾布·伯克尔来拜访先知时，先知是斜靠着的。所以他ﷺ的腿有点露出来。先知ﷺ当时处于放松状态。艾布·伯克尔进来后，情况基本保持原样。他和先知ﷺ坐了一会儿。当他需要问先知ﷺ时，他就问了先知，然后他就离开了。接着欧麦尔像往常的顺序一样进来了。同样的事情发生了。先知ﷺ保持原样，屋子也保持原样。欧麦尔问了他想问的问题，得到答案后就离开了。然后奥斯曼来了。阿伊莎说，最奇怪的事情发生了：先知ﷺ突然开始说整理屋子，把毯子放好，他坐直身子，整理好他的衣服。奥斯曼进来了，先知ﷺ以一种非常独特和特别的方式接待了他。阿伊莎目睹了这一切，当奥斯曼离开后，她说：我非常好奇。我们了解艾布·伯克尔的地位。我们了解欧麦尔的地位。为什么先知ﷺ对奥斯曼有这样的举动？我问先知ﷺ：“真主ﷻ的使者啊，艾布·伯克尔进来，欧麦尔进来时，您都没有像奥斯曼进来时那样做。”先知ﷺ说：“难道我不该对一个连天使都感到害羞的人保持害羞吗？（《穆斯林圣训集》#2401）
Should I not be shy from someone whom the angels are shy from? Even the angels have hayat around Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. So subhanAllah, Uthman changes the demeanor of the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam, the best of all creation, and he changes the demeanor of the angels by just being who he is. Now let's break this down because most people have heard this hadith about Uthman radiAllahu anhu, but there is wisdom to it.
难道我不该对一个连天使都感到害羞的人保持害羞吗？甚至天使在奥斯曼周围也怀有害羞。所以赞主ﷻ超绝，奥斯曼改变了先知ﷺ——众生中最优秀者的举止，并且他仅凭其自身的存在就改变了天使的举止。现在让我们来剖析这一点，因为大多数人都听过关于奥斯曼的这段圣训，但其中蕴含智慧。
Number one, Uthman radiAllahu anhu we know is dhun nurain. He is the possessor of two lights. Someone who is so beloved to Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala that Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala blessed him by sending Jibreel alayhi salam to command the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam to marry not one but two of his daughters to Uthman, right? So when Ruqayya radiAllahu anha passes away, the Prophet salAllahu alayhi wa sallam receives a command through Jibreel alayhi wa sallam to marry Um Kulthum radiAllahu anha to Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu as well. So he's the possessor of two lights. But think about all the ahadith about what the angels praise a man for and compare that to Uthman radiAllahu anhu.
第一，奥斯曼，我们知道是“拥有两道光芒的人”。他是如此受真主ﷻ喜爱，以至于真主ﷻ通过派遣吉卜利勒天使命令先知ﷺ将他的两个女儿都嫁给奥斯曼来赐福于他，对吧？所以当茹卡娅（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）去世时，先知ﷺ通过吉卜利勒天使接到命令，也将乌姆·库勒苏姆（愿主ﷻ喜悦她）嫁给奥斯曼。因此他是拥有两道光芒的人。但请思考所有关于天使因何而赞美一个人的圣训，并将其与奥斯曼相比较。
Uthman is known for his lengthy recitation of the Quran, finishing an entire khatm in one raka'ah, right?
