Amongst those who would have said that he would read the Quran more than once a day sometimes, the entirety of the Quran. And he said if the hearts are in a good place with Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala, then they are never full of the recitation of the Quran. [Al-Bidaya Wal Nihaya] They never get to a point where they're tired or they feel like they've reached their fill from the recitation of the Quran. He loves the Quran. So he recites the Quran constantly.
（第二，）奥斯曼以长时间诵读古兰经而闻名，能在一拜中完成整遍古兰经的诵读，对吧？他是那些有时一天会诵读整部古兰超过一遍的人之一。他说过，如果心灵与真主ﷻ同在善境之中，那么它们永远不会对诵读古兰感到满足。（《始与终》）他们永远不会达到疲倦或感到已经从诵读古兰中得到满足的地步。他热爱古兰经，所以他持续不断地诵读它。
Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu is the man who comes forth with sadaqah that is unparalleled, with charity that's unparalleled. So when you think about the angels that descend for the Quran, the angels that descend for sadaqah, right? For charity, crying out, Allahumma a'ti munfiqan khalafa that, oh Allah, give to the one who gave and, oh Allah, withhold from the one who withheld. Think about Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu coming forth every single time the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam asks for anything. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam holding the coins of Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu and saying, nothing will harm Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu after what he did today.
（第三，）奥斯曼是奉献出无与伦比的施舍、无与伦比慈善的人。所以当你想到为古兰经而降临的天使，为施舍（慈善）而降临的天使时，对吧？为慈善而呼唤：“主啊，请赐予施舍者补偿”，“主啊，请使吝啬者损失”。想一想，每次先知ﷺ有所需求时，奥斯曼都挺身而出。先知ﷺ拿着奥斯曼的钱币说：“在今日他所做之事后，没有什么能伤害奥斯曼了。”
But there is a particular trait, al-hayya, al-hayya. The concept of shyness, the concept of bashfulness. And there are multiple narrations about this concept of hayya. The Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam mentioned al-hayya minal iman. Bashfulness is from faith. It's a form of iman. The Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, al-hayya la ya'ti illa bikhayr. That anything that comes through hayya, through this concept of modesty and shyness and bashfulness, anything that comes through it is good. [Riyad as-Sahihin #681] Al-hayya khayrun kullu. Hayya is all good. It is the main characteristic of Islam. And Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu embodied hayya.
但有一种特定的品德——الحياء，“哈雅”（译注：该词译为“对主的敬畏和对被造物的廉耻。”）该词包含羞耻心的概念、谦逊的概念。关于哈雅这个概念有多个传述。先知ﷺ提到：“哈雅属于信仰”。敬畏（谦逊）是信仰的一部分。它是信仰的一种形式。先知ﷺ说：“哈雅只带来益处”。即任何通过哈雅，通过这种谦逊、敬畏和羞耻心的概念而产生的事物，任何由此而来的事物都是善的。（《利雅得圣训集》#681）“哈雅全是善。”它是伊斯兰的主要特性。而奥斯曼体现了哈雅。
Now, what makes it so impressive with Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu that he was so shy? First of all, his shyness was in public and in private. And it was with his family and it was with the community. And more than all of that, it was with Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala. He had a shyness to sin, a shyness to do anything that would be displeasing to Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala. He had this modesty, this humility, this shyness that he carried throughout every facet of life. But the thing that makes it even more impressive is that Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu had every reason to not be shy. Okay. You think about the things that make people a little arrogant, that make people a little boastful, that cause people to degrade others. Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu had beauty. Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu had wealth. Uthman radiAllahu anhu had the look. He had the wealth. He had the clothes. He had the status in society. And with all of that, Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu was the most humble and shy person that you would meet.
那么，是什么使得奥斯曼的敬畏和羞惭如此令人印象深刻？首先，他的敬畏和羞惭在公开场合和私下都存在。它存在于与家人的相处中，也存在于与社区的相处中。而最重要的是，它存在于与真主ﷻ的关系中。他对罪恶怀有敬畏和羞惭，对做任何会惹恼真主ﷻ的事情怀有敬畏和羞惭。他拥有这种谦逊、这种谦卑、这种贯穿生活方方面面的敬畏和羞惭。但使之更加令人印象深刻的是，奥斯曼完全有理由不敬畏（不谦逊）。好吧。想想那些会使人有些傲慢、使人有些自夸、导致人贬低他人的东西。奥斯曼拥有美貌、财富，仪表、财富、华服和社会地位。尽管拥有这一切，奥斯曼却是你所遇到的最谦卑、最敬畏，最有廉耻心的人。
And the ulama say, subhanAllah, the haya of the mala'ika with Uthman is not that they would be shy to be in his presence. No, it's that they longed for his presence, but that their demeanor changed in the presence of Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. And they were shy in his presence, including Jibreel alayhi salam. And part of that was what? They said, Ya Rasulullah, a man that will be killed by his people. Despite all this goodness that he does for the people, the shyness of Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu is a contributing factor to his assassination. Because he refused to use his position with the people or his previous good deeds to enforce authority over the people. Uthman radiAllahu anhu instead held back. And so the angels knowing what is with Allah subhanahu wa ta'ala for Uthman radiAllahu ta'ala anhu also caused them to have a shyness in his presence radiAllahu ta'ala anhu.
学者们说，赞主ﷻ超绝，天使在奥斯曼面前所怀有的敬畏和羞惭并非指他们羞于出现在他面前。不，是他们渴望他的在场，但他们的举止在奥斯曼面前发生了变化。并且他们在他的面前感到敬畏和羞惭，包括吉卜利勒天使。
而这其中部分原因是什么？他们说：“真主ﷻ的使者啊，一个将被自己人民杀害的人。”尽管他为人们做了所有这些善事，但奥斯曼的敬畏和羞惭也是导致他被刺杀的一个因素。因为他拒绝利用自己在人民中的地位或他先前的善行来对人民强施权威。奥斯曼反而克制了自己。
因此，天使知晓真主ﷻ为奥斯曼所准备的（崇高地位），这也使得他们在他的面前怀有敬畏和羞惭。

EP026 邻居的申诉
The Complaint of a Neighbor

An unknown Companion witnessed the best of angels, Jibreel (as), dressed in white, speaking to the Prophet ﷺ about an essential principle in Islam: the importance of neighbors, regardless of what faith they followed. “Be good to your neighbor” is a common rule of thumb cited by many traditions, but in Islam, it was emphasized to such an extent that he ﷺ thought they may be assigned inheritance next.
一位身份不明的圣伴目睹了最尊贵的天使——吉卜利勒（愿他平安），身着白衣，正在与先知ﷺ谈论伊斯兰的一个基本原则：邻居的重要性，无论他们信仰什么宗教。“善待你的邻居”是许多传统中常被引用的普遍准则，但在伊斯兰中，它被强调到如此程度，以至于先知ﷺ曾以为邻居可能会被指定为继承人了。
So this is one of those narrations where you have an unknown companion, but clear instructions that we can benefit from as an Ummah of the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam. So the first narration is actually Muslimah Ahmed. It's an authentic narration that there's this man who was looking at the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam was standing and speaking to another man for a very very long time. And he said that the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam was occupied by this conversation to the point that I started to feel sorry for him. Right? So the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam is a busy man and he gets stopped all the time. You can imagine how many people want to talk to him Alayhi Salaatu Wasalam. So he said, Ashfaqtu alayhi min tuli qiyamihi ma'a. I felt sorry for the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam because of how long he had to stand and talk to this man.
因此，这是那些传述之一：虽然传述者身份不明，但其中明确的教导，我们作为先知ﷺ的乌玛可以受益。第一段传述实际上在穆斯林和艾哈迈德的圣训集中。这是一段可靠的传述：有一个人看着先知ﷺ，先知当时正站着和另一个人谈了非常非常长的时间。他说，先知ﷺ被这次谈话占据到如此程度，以至于我开始为他感到难过（怜惜）。对吧？先知ﷺ是个大忙人，他随时会被拦住。你可以想象有多少人想和他ﷺ说话。所以他说：“我因先知ﷺ不得不长时间站着和那个人交谈而感到怜惜他。”
So eventually I waited for the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and Rasulullah Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam came and I said, Ya Rasulullah, who was that man that was talking to you because I felt sorry for you for how long you had to stand with him and the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam says, Did you see him? And I said, Yes. So the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam said, Thaka Jibreel. That was Jibreel Alayhi Salam. Qala walaw salamta alayhi la radda alayka salam. He said, If you would have come to him and said salam, he would have actually answered your salam. So it was a missed opportunity for you to come to him and say salam. And then he said, mazala yuslini bil jar hatta wanantu anahu sayi warithuhu. He said, Jibreel Alayhi Salam kept on coming to me and telling me, take care of the neighbor, take care of the neighbor, take care of the neighbor until I thought that he was going to assign inheritance to the neighbor.
最终我等着先知ﷺ，真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ过来了，我说：“真主ﷻ的使者啊，刚才和你说话的那个人是谁？因为我为你不得不和他站那么久而感到怜惜。”先知ﷺ说：“你看见他了吗？”我说：“是的。”于是先知ﷺ说：“那是吉卜利勒，如果你当时过去向他道“色兰”，他会回应你的。所以这是你错失的一个向他道“色兰”的机会。然后他说：“吉卜利勒不断地嘱咐我要善待邻居、善待邻居、善待邻居，直到我以为他将要指定邻居为继承人了。”
Now, why do I start with that narration? It actually precedes a chapter in the book Al-Adab Al-Mufrad by Imam Al-Bukhari. Imam Bukhari, of course, Sahih Al-Bukhari has the most authentic ahadith from Imam Al-Bukhari. Al-Adab Al-Mufrad are some of the mannerisms and etiquettes that we can learn. And there's some ahadith that he includes there that might have a weakness in the chain. So this chapter is called Bab Shikayat Al-Jar. The chapter of Shikayat Al-Jar, the complaint of the neighbor.
那么，为什么我从这个传述开始讲呢？它实际上在伊玛目布哈里的著作《礼仪大全》中某一章的开篇。伊玛目布哈里，当然，他的《布哈里圣训集》包含了伊玛目布哈里传述的最可靠的圣训集。《礼仪大全》则包含了一些我们可以学习的礼仪规范。其中他收录的一些圣训在传述链上可能存在羸弱。这一章名为“邻居的申诉”章。
And it's narrated by Jabir radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. That this man comes to the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam to complain to him about how he is being mistreated by his neighbor. And while he's sitting between al-rukun and al-maqam, the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam is between the rukun and the maqam. This man approaches the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and he sees that the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam is with a man who's wearing a white garment. And he mentions the beauty of this man and the fact that, you know, this man seems completely unknown and out of place. So he said they went to the maqam where they were praying upon the dead. And he said, I asked the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam, I said, Ya Rasulullah, may my mother and my father be sacrificed for you. Who was this man that was with you? And the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam says, did you see him? He said, yes. He said, ra'ayta khayran kathira, then you have seen much good. And he said, that was Jibreel alayhi salam coming to me. And he said, he kept on speaking to me about the importance of the neighbors until I thought that Jibreel alayhi salam was going to command me to assign inheritance to the neighbors.
它由贾比尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）传述：有一个人来到先知ﷺ面前，向他申诉他的邻居如何虐待他。当他正坐在（天房的）角落与易卜拉欣立足处之间时——先知ﷺ当时正在角落和立足处之间——这个人走近先知ﷺ，他看到先知ﷺ正和一个身穿白衣的人在一起。他提到了这个人的俊美以及这个看起来完全陌生且不合时宜的事实。他说他们后来去了停放亡人并为之举行殡礼的地方。他说：“我问先知ﷺ：‘真主ﷻ的使者啊，愿我的父母为你牺牲。刚才和你在一起的那个人是谁？’先知ﷺ说：‘你看见他了？’他说：‘是的。’先知说：‘那你已看到许多善了。’他说：‘那是吉卜利勒来找我。’他说：‘他不断地向我强调邻居的重要性，直到我以为吉卜利勒将要命令我为邻居分配继承权了。’”
SubhanAllah, in both of these incidents, and they could indeed be the same incident, you have a situation where the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam is talking to Jibreel alayhi salam for a long time. And Jibreel alayhi salam keeps on saying, al jar, al jar, al jar, the neighbor, the neighbor, the neighbor. Be careful with the mistreatment of the neighbor. You know, warn the ummah about the way that the neighbors are supposed to be treated. And you know, you know that famous story of the woman that used to pray a lot and she used to fast a lot. She used to give a lot of sadaqa, but she had an abusive tongue towards her neighbor, right? All of these ahadith, all of these narrations are coming from a place. And there's a spirit that is often lost in our discourse today because the emphasis on the neighbor is not really there. And that's something that even the sahaba, you know, they took from the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and they didn't just take it with one another.
赞主ﷻ超绝，在这两个事件中（它们很可能就是同一个事件），先知ﷺ与吉卜利勒进行了长时间的交谈，而吉卜利勒不断地强调：邻居啊，邻居啊，邻居啊。要小心对邻居的虐待。你要知道，要警告乌玛关于如何对待邻居的方式。你知道那个著名的故事吧？那个经常礼拜、经常封斋、大量施舍的妇女，但她却对她的邻居恶语相向（滥用她的舌头），对吧？所有这些圣训，所有这些传述都源自于此。而其中蕴含的哲理在我们今日的论述中常常忽视，因为对邻居的强调已不复存在。而圣伴们，他们从先知ﷺ那里领受了这一教导，并且他们不仅彼此之间践行它。
There is a beautiful narration in Abu Dawood that's an authentic narration. That Mujahid Rahim Allah says that Abdullah ibn Amr radiAllahu ta'ala anhuma, and we know who he is. He sacrificed a sheep. And as soon as it was sacrificed, before they could start to, you know, take the portions of it, he said, have you given some of it to my Jewish neighbor? SubhanAllah, I mean, this is, he's sitting amongst sahaba, sitting amongst tabi'een. What do you mean my Jewish neighbor? He said, have you given some of it to my Jewish neighbor? For I heard the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam say, Jibreel Alayhi Salam kept on coming to me and talking to me about the neighbor, the neighbor, the neighbor, until I thought that Jibreel Alayhi Salam was going to make the neighbor an inheritor.
在艾布·达乌德圣训集中有一段优美的、可靠的传述：穆贾希德（愿主ﷻ慈悯他）说，阿卜杜拉·本·阿慕尔（愿主ﷻ喜悦他们）——我们知道他是谁——他宰了一只羊。羊刚被宰完，在他们还没来得及开始分割羊肉时，他就问：“你们有没有分一些给我的犹太邻居？”赞主ﷻ超绝，我的意思是，他当时就坐在圣门弟子和再传弟子中间。“我的犹太邻居”是什么意思？他说：“你们有没有分一些给我的犹太邻居？因为我听先知ﷺ说过：‘吉卜利勒天使不断地来找我，对我谈论邻居、邻居、邻居，直到我以为吉卜利勒天使将要使邻居成为继承人了。’”
And so we learned from this subhanAllah that, you know, if we could listen in on some of these conversations, that much of the conversations we would be able to witness and see from the Prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam and Jibreel Alayhi Salam would actually be about us with an issue that while it is not at the forefront today, is still very much important to us, even if our neighbors are not Muslim as was the case of Abdullah ibn Amr radiAllahu ta'ala anhuma.
赞主ﷻ超绝，因此，我们从中学到，如果我们能听到其中一些对话，我们能够见证和看到的先知ﷺ与吉卜利勒天使的许多对话，实际上将是关于我们面临的一个问题——尽管这个问题在今天并非处于前沿，但它对我们仍然非常重要，即使我们的邻居不是穆斯林，正如阿卜杜拉·本·阿慕尔的情况一样。

EP027 真主ﷻ仍将宽恕他
Allah Will Still Forgive Him

A Companion who spent a lot of time alone with the Prophet ﷺ, Abu Dharr al-Ghifari (ra) was blessed to hear the voice of Jibreel (as) near Mount Uhud as he imparted wisdom about wealth, charity, and entry into Paradise.
一位曾长时间与先知ﷺ独处的圣伴——艾布·达尔·吉法里（愿主ﷻ喜悦他），有幸在吴侯德山附近听到吉卜利勒天使（愿他平安）的声音，他向先知ﷺ传达关于财富、施舍以及进入天园的智慧。
Abu Dharr Al-Ghifari radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu is one of the companions who spent a lot of time with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam alone. And so one of the blessings we have from Abu Dharr radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu is that you have multiple narrations of personal advice from the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam to Abu Dharr radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu. Of course, he's known as the first one to use the greeting of Islam when he came and embraced Islam in Mecca. It's one of the first people to say As-Salamu Alaika Ya Rasulullah. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam to respond to him in the same way.
艾布·达尔·吉法里是那些曾长时间与先知ﷺ独处的圣伴之一。因此，我们从他那里得到的恩典之一就是，我们拥有多段先知ﷺ给予艾布·达尔的个人忠告的传述。当然，他以在麦加归信伊斯兰时第一个使用伊斯兰问候语而闻名。他是最早说出“愿平安降于你，真主ﷻ的使者啊”的人之一。而先知ﷺ也以同样的方式回应了他。
So this incident and he's also of course one of those who narrated the hadith of Jubilee al-Islam. This incident, he says he and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam were walking out and they looked at Uhud. So they're walking out and as they're taking the stroll, they look at the mountain of Uhud. And Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam used to love to use Uhud as an example, right? I mean, it's a very prominent mountain. If you're in Medina, you see it all the time and it really imposes its presence on you. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam as he's staring at Uhud, he says to Abu Dharr radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, he says ma uhibbu anahu yuhawwalu li dhahaban yamkuthu AAindi minhu dinaran fawqa thalath illa dinaran ursiduhu ridayn He said to Abu Dharr radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, I would not like to have this mountain turned into gold for me unless nothing of it, not even a single dinar remains of it for more than three days, except for that which is used for debt. [Sahih al-Bukhari2388] Meaning the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is saying, when I look at this mountain, I wish I could have it and spend it for the sake of Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala. I don't want anything of it of this dunya.
关于这个事件（他也是传述伊斯兰禧年圣训的人之一）。他说，他和先知ﷺ正往外走，他们看到了吴侯德山。他们一边散步，一边看着吴侯德山。真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ过去常常喜欢用吴侯德山作比喻，对吧？我的意思是，它是一座非常显眼的山。如果你在麦地那，你总是能看到它，它确实以其存在感影响着你。当先知ﷺ凝视着吴侯德山时，他对艾布·达尔说：“我不喜欢这座山为我变成黄金，除非其中没有一枚第纳尔（金币）在我这里留存超过三天，除了用于还债的那一枚。”（《布哈里圣训集》#2388）意思是先知ﷺ在说：当我看着这座山时，我希望能拥有它并为真主ﷻ而花费它。我不想要其中的任何一点今世之物。
Now you compare that to the hadith where the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said that if the son of Adam was given a valley of gold, then he wouldn't say alhamdulillah for the valley of gold. He would say I want another one and another one and another one until that person eventually dies and their mouth is full of dirt. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is looking at Uhud and saying, I wish I could give that in sadaqah. I wish I could have this in gold and give it in sadaqah. Then the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, inna al-aktharinah humu al-aqalloon illa man qala bil-mali haa katha wa haa katha wa qaleelun ma'hum. The Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said that those who have much in this world have little in the hereafter. SubhanAllah, al-aktharin, those who have much in this world have little in the hereafter. Except for those who spend their money like this and like this, meaning fee sabeelillah. They give in charity, right and left, fee sabeelillah, in the cause of Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, qaleelun ma'hum, how few in number are they. [Sahih al-Bukhari 2388]
现在，你可以将此与另一段圣训比较：先知ﷺ曾说，如果阿丹之子被赐予一个山谷的黄金，他也不会为这山谷的黄金说感赞真主ﷻ。他会说我还想要一个，再要一个，再要一个，直到这个人最终死去，他的嘴里填满了泥土。而先知ﷺ看着吴侯德山说，我希望我能把它作为施舍捐出去。我希望我能拥有这（变成的）黄金并将它作为施舍捐出。然后先知ﷺ说：“今世拥有最多者，在后世却是拥有最少者。”赞主ﷻ超绝，今世拥有最多者，在后世拥有最少。除了一种人例外：那些像为主道花费钱财，左右施舍的人。先知ﷺ说：“而他们是多么的稀少啊。”（《布哈里圣训集》#2388）
Then, so the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is making an example out of Uhud and speaking just to Abu Dharr radiAllahu ta'ala anhu. Then the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam hears something and he says to Abu Dharr radiAllahu anhu, makanak, stay here and don't leave until I give you permission. So Abu Dharr stays there and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam goes to a place and Abu Dharr radiAllahu ta'ala anhu hears this loud voice speaking to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. Or just the noise and that voice, you know, it scared him a bit and he wanted to go out, but he remembered the order of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. So he stayed in his place and Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam spent some time over there and then the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam comes back and he says, Ya Rasulullah, what was that noise or that voice that I heard? And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, did you hear it? Now you see subhanAllah through all of these narrations. Did you see it? Did you hear it? Because sometimes Allah subh'anaHu wa ta'ala allows some people to hear or see the mala'ika. Sometimes Allah subh'anaHu wa ta'ala keeps them, you know, obscured, which is the majority of time. And sometimes two people looking at the same angel, one can see the other can't. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam says, so you heard him? And Abu Dharr says, yes, Ya Rasulullah. So the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said, Jibreel Alayhi Salam came to me. So he called me out and he came to me.
然后，先知ﷺ正在用吴侯德山作比喻，并且只对艾布·达尔说话。接着，先知ﷺ听到了什么声音，他对艾布·达尔说：“待在原地，直到我允许你离开。”于是艾布·达尔待在那里，先知ﷺ去了一个地方，艾布·达尔听到这个响亮的声音在对先知说话。或者只是那噪音和声音，你知道，这让他有点害怕，他想过去看看，但他想起了先知ﷺ的命令。所以他待在自己的位置上。真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ在那里待了一段时间，然后先知回来了，他说：“真主ﷻ的使者啊，我听到的那个噪音或声音是什么？”先知ﷺ说：“你听到了？”
赞主ﷻ超绝，通过这些所有的传述：“你看见了吗？”，“你听到了吗？”。因为有时真主ﷻ允许一些人听到或看见天使。有时真主ﷻ遮蔽了他们，你知道，这是大多数时候的情况。有时两个人看着同一个天使，一个人能看见，另一个却不能。
先知ﷺ说：“那么你听到他了？”艾布·达尔说：“是的，真主ﷻ的使者啊。”于是先知ﷺ说：“吉卜利勒天使来找我了。他叫我出去，然后他来到我面前。”
Now, before I say what he told him, there are numerous narrations of Jibreel Alayhi Salam calling the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam away from the gathering to give him some glad tidings. So the time where the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam was called away and he was found in sujood praising Allah and thanking Allah because Jibreel Alayhi Salam called him away from the gathering to tell him that Allah just sends him the news that anyone who sends salam on Rasulullah SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam Allah Subhanahu Wa Ta'ala would send that salam back to them 10 times and Jibreel Alayhi Salam would send that salam 10 times and they would be raised by 10 degrees. So here Jibreel called the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam away from Abu Dharr and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam says, and he told me Man maata min ummatika la yushriku billahi shay'a dakhalal jannah I want you to know Ya Rasulullah whoever amongst your followers dies not associating a partner with Allah will enter into Jannah. [Sahih al-Bukhari 2388] Meaning at some point they're entering into Jannah. So, you know the narration of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam, you know crying out for his ummah and Allah sending Jibreel Alayhi Salam to comfort him. This is a glad tiding that Jibreel Alayhi Salam wants to share with the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam.
在我转述他告诉我的内容之前，有许多关于吉卜利勒天使把先知ﷺ从聚会中叫走，给他带来一些喜讯的传述。比如有一次先知ﷺ被叫走，人们发现他在叩头中赞美真主ﷻ和感谢真主ﷻ，因为吉卜利勒天使把他从聚会中叫走，告诉他，真主ﷻ刚刚给他带来的消息：任何人只要向真主ﷻ的使者ﷺ道“色兰”，真主ﷻ就会将回赐给他十次，吉卜利勒天使也会回赐十次，并且他们将被提升十个品级。
所以这里吉卜利勒把先知ﷺ从艾布·达尔身边叫走，先知说：“他告诉我：‘真主ﷻ的使者啊，我希望你知道，凡是你的乌玛里有人死去时不以任何物匹配真主ﷻ（不举伴真主ﷻ）的人，都将进入天园。’”（《布哈里圣训集》#2388）意思是在某个时候他们会进入天堂。
你知道先知ﷺ为他的乌玛呼求的传述，真主ﷻ派遣吉卜利勒天使去安慰他。这是吉卜利勒天使想与先知ﷺ分享的喜讯。
Now Abu Dharr radiAllahu ta'ala anhu he's already moved on from the surprise that this was Jibreel Alayhi Salam that he heard talking to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. He says Ya Rasulullah infa'ala katha wa katha Oh messenger of Allah even if he did this and that even if he committed adultery and stole, okay. And the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam said even if he did this and that. He said Ya Rasulullah even if he did this and that. This is another narration that extends this conversation even if he did this and that. So he's saying wait a minute, how will he go to Jannah and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam is saying even if he did those things. So after repeating this to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam responding to him, the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam says even if he did this and that even if Abu Dharr doesn't like it. Even if Abu Dharr radiAllahu ta'ala anhu objects eventually this person will go to Jannah.
现在艾布·达尔已经从惊讶中回过神来，惊讶于他听到的是吉卜利勒天使在对先知ﷺ说话。他说：“真主ﷻ的使者啊！即使他做了这个和那个？即使他通奸和偷窃了，（也能进入天园吗？）”先知ﷺ说：“即使他做了这个和那个。”他说：“真主ﷻ的使者啊！即使他做了这个和那个？”（这是另一个延伸这次对话的传述：即使他做了这个和那个。）所以他在说，等等，这样的人怎么会进天园呢？而先知ﷺ说即使他做了那些事。在艾布·达尔向先知ﷺ重复了这个问题，先知回应他之后，先知（愿主福安之）说：“即使他做了这个和那个，即使艾布·达尔不情愿（或：即使艾布·达尔反对）”，最终这个人也将进入天园。
Now of course there are different ways to go to Jannah and there are some that are punished on the way to Jannah. But this in and of itself was a Bushra to the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. But at the same time we aspire to the death of the likes of Sa'd ibn Mu'adh radiAllahu ta'ala anhu and not someone that is just being mentioned in this way between Jibreel A'islam and the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam.
当然，进入天园有不同的方式，有些人在通往天园的路上会受到惩罚。但这件事本身对先知ﷺ来说是一个喜讯。但同时，我们向往的是像萨阿德·本·穆阿兹（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）那样的归真者的品级，而非仅仅像吉卜利勒天使与先知ﷺ之间所提到的这种方式（进入天园的人）。
EP028 当他们在你临终时向你致意
When They Greet You at Death

Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz (ra) lived an extraordinary life pursuing Allah’s pleasure, and his death was even more extraordinary. Included amongst the rightly guided khulafa’, he so sincerely feared Allah and hoped for His mercy that even after he passed, a message clearly not written by human hands was miraculously left in his grave. His awe-inspiring story serves as a reminder to us all to live in such a way that even we are received by the most radiant of angels in our final moments.
欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）度过了一生非凡的时光以追求真主ﷻ的喜悦，而他的死亡则更为非凡。他被列入正统哈里发之中，他是如此真诚地敬畏真主ﷻ并渴望祂ﷻ的慈悯，以至于在他去世后，一份显然非人手所写的讯息奇迹般地留在了他的坟墓里。他那令人敬畏的故事提醒我们所有人，要以这样的方式生活，以至于在我们生命的最后时刻，也能由最光辉的天使来迎接。
Umar ibn Abdul Aziz radiAllahu ta'ala anhum is one of the most fascinating people in Islamic history because he is unanimously by consensus, the first mujaddid, the first reviver of this ummah after the companions of the Prophet SallAllahu Alaihi Wasallam. And he is included in Khulafa' Rashideen amongst the righteous guided Khulafa' even though he's not a sahabi. He just ascends in rank in every way. And it's such a fascinating story because like when we're talking about Sa'ad ibn Wa'ad radiAllahu ta'ala anhum, we're talking about a man who really didn't live long, right? He died at either 39 or 40. He was the governor of Medina from 25 to 32, and then becomes the Khalifa in the last two years of his life. So his Khulafa is only about two years.
欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹是伊斯兰历史上最引人入胜的人物之一，因为根据一致的公议，他是第一位复兴者，是先知ﷺ的圣门弟子之后，这个稳麦的第一位复兴者。他虽不是圣门弟子，却被列入正统的、受引导的哈里发之中。他在各个方面都提升了品级。这是一个非常引人入胜的故事，因为当我们谈论萨阿德·本·瓦阿德（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）时，我们谈论的是一位寿命并不长的人，对吧？他去世时年仅39或40岁。他在25至32岁期间担任麦地那总督，然后在生命的最后两年成为哈里发。所以他的哈里发任期只有大约两年。
He's someone subhanAllah who was able to achieve much and was a scholar, was someone who would be described as having more reverence for Allah in his heart than anyone that you would ever meet. Someone whose knowledge was unparalleled, someone whose asceticism, his zuhid was unparalleled. And so his death is going to be extraordinary. And I wanted to include it because it is very much so connected to what we should be aspiring to as we're hearing all of these stories of the sahaba.
Now, before I get into his, you know, there's a narration that when Salman radiAllahu ta'ala anhum was dying, Salman al-Farisi was dying, that he had musk in his room, good smells in his room, and he laid down and he said, tonight there are creatures that are going to be coming to me and they smell fragrances, but they don't eat food. Meaning he was ready for the malaikah, ready for the angels to come and to take his soul. And that's one of the signs of husn al-khitam, one of the signs of a good ending is that you kind of feel it at the end and you prepare yourself.
赞主ﷻ超绝，他是一个成就斐然的人，是一位学者，被描述为心怀对真主ﷻ的敬畏胜过你所遇见的任何人。他的学识无与伦比，他的苦行无与伦比。因此，他的死亡将是不同寻常的。我想把它包括进来，因为它与我们听到所有这些圣门弟子故事时所应向往的目标密切相关。
在我讲述他的故事之前，有一段传述：当萨利曼（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）——萨利曼·法尔西——临终时，他的房间里有麝香，有美好的香气，他躺下说：“今晚将有一些造物来找我，他们闻香气但不吃食物。”意思是他已准备好迎接天使，准备好迎接天使来取走他的灵魂。这就是善终的迹象之一，善终的迹象之一就是你在最后时刻能感受到它并为之做准备。
Now, Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz had a great fear of Allah subhanAllah ta'ala, just like his grandfather Umar ibn al-Khattab radiAllahu ta'ala anhum, despite everything amazing that he did, and despite all of the praise that he received for what he did until the very last moment, he's worried about going to hellfire. He's worried about Jahannam, though no one would speak to them in that vein, in that spirit. So he calls a friend of his, who is a great scholar by the name of Al-Raja' ibn Haywa. Al-Raja' ibn Haywa radiAllahu ta'ala anhum, who actually is a Ghazawi, he's a scholar from Gaza, in Palestine, amazing scholar of Islam and a contemporary of Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz. And Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz gives him instructions. He says, listen, when I die, I want you to be the one to receive my body in the grave. And when I'm there, I want you to uncover my face. If you find that in the grave, I'm already facing the Qibla, then thank Allah subhanAllah ta'ala and leave me there for Allah has granted me Al-Jannah. And if you find otherwise, then insist on the people to ask Allah to forgive me and have mercy on me. So it's something between him and Allah subhanAllah ta'ala. It's not something a practice that, you know, we go into the graves and if they're facing the Qibla, we say Alhamdulillah, though we would say Alhamdulillah regardless, but that we would assume otherwise in a different situation. So he gives that instruction to him.
欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹对真主ﷻ怀有极大的敬畏，赞主ﷻ超绝，就像他的祖父欧麦尔·本·哈塔卜（愿主ﷻ喜悦他）一样。尽管他做了所有惊人的事情，尽管他因所做之事受到所有赞誉，直到生命的最后一刻，他仍在担心自己会进入火狱。尽管没有人会以那种语气、那种精神对他说话。因此，他召唤了他的一位朋友，一位名叫拉贾·本·海瓦的大学者。拉贾·本·海瓦（愿主ﷻ喜悦他），他实际上是加沙人，是来自巴勒斯坦加沙的学者，一位了不起的伊斯兰学者，也是欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹的同时代人。欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹向他交代了指示。他说：“听着，当我死后，我希望你是那个在坟墓里接收我遗体的人。当我在那里时，我希望你揭开我的裹尸布（露出我的脸）。如果你发现我在坟墓里已经面朝克尔白，那么感赞真主ﷻ，就把我留在那里，因为真主ﷻ已赐予我天园。如果你发现情况并非如此，那么你要坚持让人们祈求真主ﷻ宽恕我、慈悯我。”所以这是他与真主ﷻ之间的事。这不是一种我们可以效仿的实践，比如我们进入坟墓，如果亡者面朝克尔白，我们就说感赞真主ﷻ——尽管无论如何我们都会说感赞真主ﷻ——但在不同的情况下我们不会那样假设。于是他把这个指示告诉了他。
And then he calls for his wife and his children. Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz had a very righteous wife, Fatima ibn Abd al-Malik radiAllahu ta'ala anhum, SubhanAllah, wonderful, righteous woman that he would praise often. And he embraces her and he bids her farewell with an ayah from the Quran, innama yuwafaa as-sabiroona ajrahum bi ghayri hisab Allah subhanAllah ta'ala compensates the patient without any limits. （39：10）
然后他召唤了他的妻子和孩子们。欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹有一位非常虔诚的妻子，法蒂玛·宾特·阿卜杜·马利克（愿主ﷻ喜悦她），赞主ﷻ超绝，一位他经常称赞的、美好而虔诚的女子。他拥抱了她，并用一段古兰经文与她告别：“惟有坚忍的人，得享受完全的、无量的报酬。”（39：10）
So gives her the glad tidings of what she would receive should she maintain patience in the case of his death. Then suddenly he looks up and his gaze was fixed on something. And he asks his family to leave. He says, ukhruju anni, he asked them to leave. So they leave, but they stay at the door and they peek in and they can hear what Umar ibn Abd al-Aziz is saying. And they said that a light entered into the room and Umar says the following, he says, marhaban bihadhihi al-wujuh, welcome to these beautiful faces, al-lati laysath bih wujuhi insan wa la jaan these faces that don't belong to human beings or jinn. So he's looking at their faces and he is praising them. And then he recites, tilkad daru al-akhira naj'aluha lillazeena la yuriduna alooan fil ardi wa la fasaada wal-aakhibatu al-muttaqeen This is the home of the hereafter. We grant it to those who don't seek elevation or corruption in this earth and victory belongs to believers. （28:83）And he kept on reciting that verse until they stopped hearing him recited.
因此，他给予了她喜讯，告诉她如果在他去世时保持坚忍，她将得到的回赐。然后突然，他抬起头，目光凝视着某物。他要求家人离开。于是他们离开了，但他们守在门口，偷偷往里看，他们能听到欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹在说什么。他们说一道光进入了房间，欧麦尔说了如下的话，他说：“欢迎你们这些美好的面容，这些不属于人类或精灵的面容。”所以他看着他们的面容并赞美他们。然后他诵读道：“这是后世的住宅，我要用来报答那不愿傲慢也不愿堕落的人。善果只归敬畏的人。”（28：83）他不断地诵读这节经文，直到他们再也听不到他诵读的声音。
Then his wife and his children walked in and they found him facing towards the qibla, his face shining with a big smile on it. And he was reclining his head on his palm. So as if he's staring at Jannah, he's staring at something as his soul was coming out of his body. SubhanAllah, I mean, how would you want to leave? You think about the ayat, inna allatheena qaloo rabbuna allahu thumma istaqamoo tatanazzalo alayhimu almalaika allatakhafoo walatahzanoo waabashiroo biljannati allatikuntum tuAAadoon When Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la says that those who believed and worked righteousness and they were firm, the angels come to them at the time of death and say, allatakhafoo walatahzanoo Don't grieve, don't be afraid, abashiroo biljanna And here's the paradise that you were promised. （41：30）
然后他的妻子和孩子们走了进来，他们发现他面朝克尔白，他的面容闪耀着灿烂的笑容。他正用手掌托着头倚靠着。仿佛他正凝视着天园，在灵魂离开身体时凝视着某物。赞主ﷻ超绝，我的意思是，你希望如何离开人世？想想这节经文：凡说过“我们的主是真主”，然后遵循正道者，众天神将来临他们，说：“你们不要恐惧，不要忧愁，你们应当为你们被预许的乐园而高兴。（41：30）
So with Omar radiAllahu ta'ala Anhu, you're seeing that happen, his family is seeing that happen. And you go back to that conversation between him and Al-Raja'a Ibn Haywa. Al-Raja'a says, so I went to the grave of Omar Ibn Abdul Aziz and his janazah was attended by tens of thousands of people. It was a very painful janazah because the people understood that all of the reform that Omar Ibn Abdul Aziz brought to the Ummah, it was at risk. So Al-Raja'a says, I was waiting in the grave and I received the body of Omar Ibn Abdul Aziz. And he said, I uncovered his face and it was turned towards the Qibla already. And he said, I'd never seen anything that bright in my life, not even the full moon. SubhanAllah, it was completely, you know, lit up with this big smile on his face. And he praised Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la and thanked Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la for what he had seen.
因此，在欧麦尔身上，你看到了这一幕的发生，他的家人也目睹了这一幕。你回想一下他和拉贾·本·海瓦之间的对话。拉贾说：于是我去到欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹的坟墓。他的殡礼有数万人参加。那是一场非常令人悲痛的殡礼，因为人们明白欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹带给乌玛的所有改革都岌岌可危。拉贾说：我在坟墓里等候，接收了欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹的遗体。他说：我揭开了他的裹尸布（露出他的脸），发现它已经面朝克尔白。他说：我这辈子从未见过如此明亮的东西，甚至满月也比不上。赞主ﷻ超绝，它完全被照亮，脸上带着灿烂的笑容。他赞主ﷻ超绝，为他所看到的感赞真主ﷻ。
And then the last karama, the last miracle that happens with Omar Ibn Abdul Aziz Rahimahullah ta'ala This was a man that feared hellfire, despite resembling the people of paradise his entire life. Yusuf Ibn Mahak Rahimahullah ta'ala He says that when we buried Omar Ibn Abdul Aziz, when we dug up his grave, we found a note in his grave. And that note was clearly not written by human hands. And it said, Bismillah Rahman Rahim Amanun min Allah li Omar Ibn Abdul Aziz min an-nar Bismillah Rahman Rahim This is a guarantee for Omar Ibn Abdul Aziz that he has been protected from the fire. So it's not like, you know, we can expect that note. And maybe we don't have the ending to the same level of beauty, a witnessed ending. But what is instructive here is that if you live your life pursuing Allah's pleasure, then your transition to paradise looks a lot like that transition. Maybe not with the same level of detail, but certainly with the same level of tranquility. And the one who seeks protection from the fire will be protected. And the one who seeks Allah's pleasure will be granted it.
然后是发生在欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹身上的最后一个奇迹。这是一个畏惧火狱的人，尽管他一生都宛如天园居民。优素福·本·马哈克（愿主ﷻ慈悯他）说：当我们埋葬欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹，当我们挖掘他的坟墓时，我们在他的坟墓里发现了一张字条。那张字条显然不是人手所写。上面写着：“奉至仁至慈的真主ﷻ之名。这是来自真主ﷻ的保护，保护欧麦尔·本·阿卜杜·阿齐兹免受火狱之苦。”所以这不是说，你知道，我们可以期待得到那样的字条。也许我们不会有同样美丽程度的、被见证的善终。但这里具有启发性的是：如果你一生追求真主ﷻ的喜悦，那么你通往天园的过渡将会很像那种过渡。也许细节上不会完全相同，但安宁的程度肯定是一样的。寻求免遭火狱保护的人将得到保护。寻求真主ﷻ喜悦的人将被赐予喜悦。
May Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la grant us his pleasure. May Allah protect us from the fire. May Allah forgive us for our shortcomings. May Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la gather us with his righteous servants, the humans of them and the angels of them. And may Allah Subh'anaHu Wa Ta-A'la grant us the companionship of our beloved prophet Sallallahu Alaihi Wasallam in Jannatul Firdaus Allahumma Ameen JazakumAllahu Khayran Wa Salamualikoum wa Rahmatullahi wa Barakatuhu
愿真主ﷻ赐予我们，祂ﷻ的喜悦。
愿真主ﷻ保护我们，免遭火狱之苦。
愿真主ﷻ宽恕我们的不足。
愿真主ﷻ使我们与祂ﷻ清廉的仆人们——他们中的人类和天使——相聚。
愿真主ﷻ在菲尔道斯天园中赐予我们心爱的先知ﷺ的陪伴。
主啊，求你准承。
愿真主ﷻ回赐你。
愿真主ﷻ的平安、慈悯和吉庆降临你们。
